-- MERIDIAN optimized build (.meridian)
-- Performance changes:
--   * Removed injected unconditional console spam.
--   * Reduced anti-lag scan burst size to avoid frame-time spikes.
--   * Yielded large visual scans more frequently.
--   * Throttled idle auto-steal polling slightly.

-- Meridian (Luarmor auth removed)
-- SLAXER ANTI DROP (always active: starts with the source, no toggle needed)
do
	local okAntiDrop, errAntiDrop = pcall(function()
		if type(_G.SLAXER_IsAntiDropOn) == "function" and _G.SLAXER_IsAntiDropOn() then
			return
		end

		local Players = game:GetService("Players")
		local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
		local mt = type(getrawmetatable) == "function" and getrawmetatable(game) or nil
		local oldIdx, oldNewIdx
		local spoofedVelocity = Vector3.zero
		local antiDropActive = false

		local function startAntiDrop()
			if antiDropActive then return end
			if not mt then return end

			oldIdx = mt.__index
			oldNewIdx = mt.__newindex
			pcall(function() setreadonly(mt, false) end)

			local newc = (typeof(newcclosure) == "function") and newcclosure or function(f) return f end
			local chk = (typeof(checkcaller) == "function") and checkcaller or function() return false end

			mt.__index = newc(function(self, key)
				if (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity")
					and typeof(self) == "Instance"
					and not chk()
					and self:IsA("BasePart")
					and self.Name == "HumanoidRootPart"
					and LP and LP.Character
					and self:IsDescendantOf(LP.Character)
				then
					return spoofedVelocity
				end
				return oldIdx(self, key)
			end)

			mt.__newindex = newc(function(self, key, value)
				if (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity")
					and typeof(self) == "Instance"
					and not chk()
					and self:IsA("BasePart")
					and self.Name == "HumanoidRootPart"
					and LP and LP.Character
					and self:IsDescendantOf(LP.Character)
				then
					spoofedVelocity = value
					return
				end
				return oldNewIdx(self, key, value)
			end)

			pcall(function() setreadonly(mt, true) end)
			antiDropActive = true
		end

		local function stopAntiDrop()
			if not antiDropActive then return end
			if mt and oldIdx then
				pcall(function()
					setreadonly(mt, false)
					mt.__index = oldIdx
					mt.__newindex = oldNewIdx
					setreadonly(mt, true)
				end)
				oldIdx, oldNewIdx = nil, nil
			end
			antiDropActive = false
		end

		_G.SLAXER_StartAntiDrop = startAntiDrop
		_G.SLAXER_StopAntiDrop = stopAntiDrop
		_G.SLAXER_IsAntiDropOn = function() return antiDropActive end
		_G.SLAXER_ToggleAntiDrop = function()
			if antiDropActive then stopAntiDrop() else startAntiDrop() end
			return antiDropActive
		end

		startAntiDrop()
	end)

	if not okAntiDrop then
		warn("[Meridian] Anti Drop failed to start: " .. tostring(errAntiDrop))
	end
end

local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

									if not T.conn then
										T.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
											local now = os.clock()
											for name, e in pairs(T.reg) do
												if e.on and now - e.last >= e.interval then
													local elapsed = now - e.last
													e.last = now
													pcall(e.fn, (elapsed > 0.25 or elapsed <= 0) and dt or elapsed)
												end
											end
										end)
									end
								end
								function M.Tick(name, interval, fn)
									M._Tick.reg[name] = { interval = interval, last = 0, fn = fn, on = true }
								end
								function M.TickOff(name)
									local e = M._Tick.reg[name]
									if e then e.on = false end
								end
								function M.TickOn(name)
									local e = M._Tick.reg[name]
									if e then e.on = true end
								end
								function M.TickRemove(name)
									M._Tick.reg[name] = nil
								end
								function M.TickConn(name, interval, fn)
									M.Tick(name, interval, fn)
									local entry = M._Tick.reg[name]
									return {
										Disconnect = function()
											if M._Tick.reg[name] == entry then
												M._Tick.reg[name] = nil
											end
										end,
										Connected = true,
									}

							else
								if not (type(meridianStealBarPos.nx) == "number" and type(meridianStealBarPos.ny) == "number") then
									return
								end
								n34 = meridianStealBarPos.nx * currentCamera.X
								n35 = meridianStealBarPos.ny * currentCamera.Y

							local n37 = math.max(0, currentCamera.Y - math.max(absoluteSize.Y, 54))

-- identity row (same bar size): avatar + name + FPS on the left, STEAL | % in the centre, PING on the right
do
	local function accentRgb()
		local c = M.Accent or color2
		return string.format("rgb(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
	end

	-- full name always shown: the name row is full width; only very long names shrink the font
	local fitBusy = false
	local function fitName()
		if fitBusy then return end
		fitBusy = true
		pcall(function()
			if nameLabel.AbsoluteSize.X <= 4 then return end
			for size = 10, 5, -1 do
				nameLabel.TextSize = size
				if nameLabel.TextBounds.X <= nameLabel.AbsoluteSize.X then break end
			end
		end)
		fitBusy = false
	end
	nameLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fitName)
	nameLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FontFace"):Connect(fitName)
	nameLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Font"):Connect(fitName)
	frame2:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(fitName)
	task.defer(fitName)

									local function syncFillFx()
										if M.LowEnd then return end
										local on = frame.Size.X.Scale > 0.015 or frame.Size.X.Offset > 2
										glowStroke.Enabled = on
										for _, h in ipairs(halos) do h.Visible = on end
									end
									if not M.LowEnd then frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(syncFillFx) end

									-- theme-adaptive neon palette: brightens dull themes, deepens for inverted (light) UI
									local function applyFillFx()
										local inv = M.InvertUI == true
										local accent = M.Accent or color2
										local h, s, v = accent:ToHSV()
										local base
										if inv then
											base = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.min(1, s * 1.1), math.min(v, 0.85))
										else
											base = Color3.fromHSV(h, s, math.max(v, 0.78))
										end
										if M.LowEnd then
											frame.BackgroundColor3 = base
											instance3.Enabled = false
											return
										end
										local hi = base:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), inv and 0.2 or 0.5)
										local lo = base:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), inv and 0.25 or 0.3)
										frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
										instance3.Color = ColorSequence.new({
											ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, hi),
											ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, base),
											ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, lo),
										})
										glowStroke.Color = inv and lo or hi
										glowStroke.Transparency = inv and 0.25 or 0.3
										halos[1].BackgroundColor3 = base
										halos[1].BackgroundTransparency = inv and 0.8 or 0.74
										halos[2].BackgroundColor3 = base
										halos[2].BackgroundTransparency = inv and 0.9 or 0.88
										syncFillFx()
									end
									applyFillFx()

							M.ApplyStealBarInvert = function()
								local flag17 = M.InvertUI == true

						task.spawn(function()
							local now3 = tick()
							local n34 = 0

								n34 += 1

								if 0.5 <= now4 - now3 then

										return localPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * v79
									end)

									if ok and result then

									if fpsShown then fpsShown.Text = "FPS: " .. n36 end
									n34 = 0
									now3 = now4
								end

								task.wait()
							end
						end)

						task.spawn(function()
							while instance.Parent do

									instance.TextTransparency = math.clamp(0.55 - (frame.Size.X.Scale or 0) * 0.55, 0, 0.55)

					UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)

					if not M.flag3 then

					local animalPodiums = arg:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
					if not animalPodiums then
						return
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(animalPodiums:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChild("Base") then
							local str9 = arg.Name .. "_" .. child.Name
							local flag17 = false

							for _, v79 in ipairs(tbl36) do
								if v79.uid == str9 then
									flag17 = true
									break
								end
							end

									name = child.Name,

									worldPosition = child:GetPivot().Position,

				fn42 = function(arg)
					if not arg then
						return nil
					end
					local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
					if not plots then
						return nil
					end
					local v79 = plots:FindFirstChild(arg.plot)
					if not v79 then
						return nil
					end
					local animalPodiums = v79:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
					if not animalPodiums then
						return nil
					end
					local v80 = animalPodiums:FindFirstChild(arg.slot)
					if not v80 then
						return nil
					end
					local base = v80:FindFirstChild("Base")
					if not base then
						return nil
					end
					local spawn_ = base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
					if not spawn_ then
						return nil
					end
					local v81 = tbl38[arg.uid]
					if v81 and v81.Parent and v81:IsDescendantOf(spawn_) then
						return v81
					end
					tbl38[arg.uid] = nil
					local v82 = spawn_:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
					local v83 = nil

					if v82 then
						v83 = nil

						for _, child in ipairs(v82:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
								v83 = child
								break
							else
								v83 = nil
							end
						end
					end

					if not v83 then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(spawn_:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
								v83 = descendant
								break
							end
						end
					end

					if v83 then
						tbl38[arg.uid] = v83
					end

					return v83
				end
			end

			local fn43

			do
				local fn44, fn45, fn46, fn47

				do
					do
						do
							local function fn48_stealCallbacks(arg)
								if tbl37[arg] then
									return
								end
								local tbl38 = { holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, ready = true }

								if getconnections then
									local ok, result = pcall(getconnections, arg.PromptButtonHoldBegan)

									if ok and type(result) == "table" then
										for _, v78 in ipairs(result) do
											local v79 = "function"

											if type(v78.Function) == v79 then
												table.insert(tbl38.holdCallbacks, v78.Function)
											end
										end
									end

									local ok2, result2 = pcall(getconnections, arg.Triggered)

									if ok2 and type(result2) == "table" then
										for _, v78 in ipairs(result2) do
											if type(v78.Function) == "function" then
												table.insert(tbl38.triggerCallbacks, v78.Function)
											end
										end
									end
								end

								tbl37[arg] = tbl38
							end

							fn43 = function()
								local character = localPlayer.Character
								if not character then
									return nil, nil, nil
								end
								local v78 = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
								local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
								if not v78 or not plots then
									return nil, nil, nil
								end
								local huge = math.huge
								local position = v78.Position
								local tbl38 = nil
								local v79 = nil

								for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
									if not fn40(child.Name) then
										local animalPodiums = child:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

										if animalPodiums then
											for _, child2 in ipairs(animalPodiums:GetChildren()) do
												local base = child2:FindFirstChild("Base")
												base = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")

												if base then
													local magnitude = (base.Position - position).Magnitude

													if magnitude < huge and magnitude <= tbl19.StealRadius then
														local promptAttachment = base:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")

														if promptAttachment then
															for _, child3 in ipairs(promptAttachment:GetChildren()) do
																if child3:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tostring(child3.ActionText):find("Steal") then
																	tbl38 = {
																		name = child2.Name,
																		plot = child.Name,
																		slot = child2.Name,
																		worldPosition = base.Position,
																		uid = child.Name .. "_" .. child2.Name,
																	}

																	v79 = child3
																	huge = magnitude
																	break
																end
															end
														end
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end

								return tbl38, v79, huge
							end

							local v78 = nil
							local v79 = nil

							local function fn49_doNormalSteal(arg, arg2, arg3)
								local v80 = tbl37[arg]
								if not v80 or not v80.ready or flag16 then
									return false
								end

								if tick() - (M.LastNormalSteal or 0) < 0.12 then
									return false
								end

								if tween then
									tween:Cancel()
									tween = nil
								end

								v80.ready = false
								flag16 = true
								v78 = arg3
								v79 = arg
								n33 += 1
								local v81 = n33
								M.LastNormalSteal = tick()
								now2 = tick()
								local now3 = nil
								local n34 = 0
								textLabel.Text = "0%"
								frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
								uiStroke.Color = color
								local v82 = M.NormalizeNormalPausePercent(tbl19.NormalStealPausePercent or 75)
								local n35 = 1.3 * v82 / 100

								local function fn50_a()
									return v81 == n33 and flag16 and tbl20.AutoSteal
								end

								local function fn51()
									if connection2 then
										connection2:Disconnect()
										connection2 = nil
									end

									if v81 == n33 then
										fn39()
									end

									v80.ready = true

									if v81 == n33 then
										flag16 = false
										v78 = nil
										v79 = nil
									end
								end

								local function fn52()
									local character = localPlayer.Character

									if character then
										character = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
									end

									if not character then
										return nil
									end
									local parent = arg.Parent
									local worldPosition

									while true do
										local flag17 = parent and parent ~= workspace
										worldPosition = nil

										if flag17 then
											if parent:IsA("Attachment") then
												worldPosition = parent.WorldPosition
												break
											elseif parent:IsA("BasePart") then
												worldPosition = parent.Position
												break
											else
												parent = parent.Parent
												continue
											end
										end

										break
									end

									if not worldPosition then
										return nil
									end
									local n36 = character.Position - worldPosition
									return Vector3.new(n36.X, 0, n36.Z).Magnitude
								end

								if connection2 then
									connection2:Disconnect()
									connection2 = nil
								end

								local v83 = nil

								connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
									if not fn50_a() then
										v83:Disconnect()

										if connection2 == v83 then
											connection2 = nil
										end

										return
									end

									local n36 = math.clamp(((now3 or tick()) - now2 - n34) / 1.3, 0, 1)
									frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(n36, 1)
									textLabel.Text = math.floor(n36 * 100) .. "%"
								end)

								task.spawn(function()
									pcall(function()
										arg.HoldDuration = 0
									end)

									for _, holdCallback in ipairs(v80.holdCallbacks) do
										task.spawn(holdCallback)
									end

									while true do
										if fn50_a() and tick() - now2 < n35 then
											local v84 = fn52()
											if not (not v84 or v84 > tbl19.StealRadius) then
												task.wait()
												continue
											end
										end

										break
									end

									if not fn50_a() then
										if v81 == n33 then
											v80.ready = true
										end

										return
									end

									local n36 = math.clamp(v82 / 100, 0, 1)
									frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(n36, 1)
									textLabel.Text = math.floor(n36 * 100) .. "%"
									now3 = tick()
									local n37 = math.max(2.99 - n35 - math.max(1.3 - n35, 0), 0.05)
									local now4 = tick()

									while fn50_a() do
										if n37 <= tick() - now4 then
											fn51()
											return
										end
										local v84 = fn52()
										if not v84 or v84 > tbl19.StealRadius then
											fn51()
											return
										end

										if v84 <= 6 then
											n34 += tick() - now3
											now3 = nil
											break
										end

										task.wait()
									end

									if not fn50_a() then
										if v81 == n33 then
											v80.ready = true
										end

										return
									end

									local now5 = tick()
									local n38 = math.max(1.3 - n35, 0.05)

									while fn50_a() and tick() - now5 < n38 do
										task.wait()
									end

									if fn50_a() then
										pcall(function()
											for _, triggerCallback in ipairs(v80.triggerCallbacks) do
												task.spawn(triggerCallback)
											end

											local stealAnimal = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("StealAnimal")

											if stealAnimal and arg2 then
												pcall(function() stealAnimal:FireServer(arg2) end)
											end

											if arg then
												pcall(function() arg:Fire() end)
											end
										end)
									end

									fn51()
								end)

								return true
							end

							task.spawn(function()
								local v80 = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 12)
								if not v80 then
									return
								end

								for _, child in ipairs(v80:GetChildren()) do
									pcall(fn41, child)
								end

								v80.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
									task.wait(1)
									pcall(fn41, child)
								end)
							end)

							task.spawn(function()
								while task.wait(24) do
									local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

									if plots then
										tbl36 = {}

										for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
											pcall(fn41, child)
										end
									end
								end
							end)

							fn44 = function()
								if M.NormalStealRagdollPaused == true then
									return
								end

								if tbl21.autoSteal then
									return
								end

								pcall(function()
									local v80 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

									if v80 then
										for _, descendant in ipairs(v80:GetDescendants()) do
											if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
												pcall(function()
													descendant.HoldDuration = 0
												end)
											end
										end
									end
								end)

								tbl21.autoSteal = M.TickConn("autoSteal", 0.10, function()
									if not tbl20.AutoSteal or flag16 then
										return
									end

									if tick() < (tbl21.autoStealNextScan or 0) then
										return
									end
									tbl21.autoStealNextScan = tick() + 0.10
									local v80 = v78
									local v81 = v79
									local v82 = v78

									if v80 then
										if not v81 or not v81.Parent then
											v78 = nil
											v79 = nil
											v82 = nil
										else
											v82 = v80
										end
									end

									local magnitude

									if v82 then
										local character = localPlayer.Character

										if character then
											character = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
										end

										magnitude = character and v82.worldPosition and (character.Position - v82.worldPosition).Magnitude or math.huge
									else
										v82, v81, magnitude = fn43()
									end

									if not v82 then
										return
									end

									if tbl19.StealRadius < magnitude then
										if v78 == v82 then
											v78 = nil
										end

										return
									end

									v81 = v81 or fn42(v82)

									if v81 then
										fn48_stealCallbacks(v81)
										fn49_doNormalSteal(v81, v82.name, v82)
									end
								end)
							end

							fn45 = function()
								n33 += 1

								if tbl21.autoSteal then
									tbl21.autoSteal:Disconnect()
									tbl21.autoSteal = nil
								end

								flag16 = false

								if connection2 then
									connection2:Disconnect()
									connection2 = nil
								end

								if v79 then
									local v80 = tbl37[v79]

									if v80 then
										v80.ready = true
									end
								end

								fn39()
							end
						end
					end

					M.StealMode = M.StealMode or "NORMAL"

					if M.BatMode ~= "DEFAULT" and M.BatMode ~= "BYPASS" then
						M.BatMode = "DEFAULT"
					end

					M.TpBatMode = M.TpBatMode or "HIGH PING"

					if M.TpBatMode == "SURE HIT" then
						M.TpBatMode = "LOW PING"
					end

					tbl30 = {
						CFG = {
							HOLD_MIN = 1.3,
							HOLD_MAX = 2.6,
							ENTRY_DELAY = 0.3,
							STEAL_RANGE = 10,
							COOLDOWN = 0.05,
						},
						plotSync = { caches = {}, connections = {} },
						allAnimalsCache = {},
						PromptMemoryCache = {},

							active = false,
							startTime = 0,
							phase = "idle",
							label = "",
							lastResult = "",
							lastResultTime = 0,

						stealConn = nil,
						scanThread = nil,
						initted = false,
						syncRemotes = nil,
						AnimalsData = nil,
						plots = nil,
					}

					fn46 = function()
						if tbl30.initted then
							return true
						end

						if not pcall(function()
							local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
							local packages = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages", 10)
							local datas = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas", 5)

							if not packages or not datas then
								error("no packages")
							end

							tbl30.plots = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 5)
							local synchronizer = packages:WaitForChild("Synchronizer", 10)

							tbl30.syncRemotes = {
								channelFolder = synchronizer:WaitForChild("Channel"),
								routeRemote = synchronizer:WaitForChild("Router"),
								requestData = synchronizer:FindFirstChild("RequestData"),
							}
							end) then
							return false
						end

						local function fn48_syncParsePath(arg)
							local v78 = "table"
							if typeof(arg) == v78 then
								return arg
							end
							local tbl38 = {}

							for match in string.gmatch(tostring(arg), "[^%.]+") do
								table.insert(tbl38, tonumber(match) or match)
							end

							return tbl38
						end

						local function fn49_b(arg, arg2)
							local v78 = nil
							local v79 = nil

							for _, v80 in ipairs(fn48_syncParsePath(arg)) do
								local v81 = arg2 and arg2[v80]

								if v81 then
									v78 = arg2
									v79 = v80
									arg2 = v81
								else
									v78 = arg2
									v79 = v80
									arg2 = nil
								end
							end

							return arg2, v78, v79
						end

						local function fn50_b(arg, arg2)
							local v78 = tbl30.plotSync.caches[arg]
							local v79 = "table"
							if typeof(v78) ~= v79 then
								return
							end
							local v80 = arg2[2]
							local v81 = arg2[3]
							local v82 = arg2[4]
							local v83, v84, v85 = fn49_b(arg2[1], v78)

							if v80 == "Changed" then
								if v84 ~= nil then
									v84[v85] = v81
								end
							elseif v80 == "ArrayInsert" then
								if v83 ~= nil then
									table.insert(v83, v82, v81)
								end
							elseif v80 == "ArrayRemoved" then
								if v83 ~= nil then
									table.remove(v83, v82)
								end
							elseif v80 == "DictionaryInsert" then
								if v83 ~= nil then
									v83[v82] = v81
								end
							elseif v80 == "DictionaryRemoved" then
								if v83 ~= nil then
									v83[v82] = nil
								end
							end
						end

						local function fn51(arg)
							if tbl30.plotSync.connections[arg] then
								return
							end
							local str9 = tostring(arg.Name)
							if not tbl30.plots:FindFirstChild(str9) then
								return
							end

							if tbl30.syncRemotes.requestData and tbl30.plotSync.caches[str9] == nil then
								local ok, result = pcall(function()
									return tbl30.syncRemotes.requestData:InvokeServer(str9)
								end)

								if ok then
									local v78 = "table"
									ok = typeof(result) == v78
								end

								if ok then
									tbl30.plotSync.caches[str9] = result
								else
									tbl30.plotSync.caches[str9] = {}
								end
							elseif tbl30.plotSync.caches[str9] == nil then
								tbl30.plotSync.caches[str9] = {}
							end

							tbl30.plotSync.connections[arg] = arg.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg2)
								for _, v78 in ipairs(arg2) do
									fn50_b(str9, v78)
								end
							end)
						end

						local function fn52(arg)
							for k, connection3 in pairs(tbl30.plotSync.connections) do
								if tostring(k.Name) == tostring(arg) then
									connection3:Disconnect()
									tbl30.plotSync.connections[k] = nil
									tbl30.plotSync.caches[tostring(arg)] = nil
									break
								end
							end
						end

						for _, child in ipairs(tbl30.syncRemotes.channelFolder:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
								fn51(child)
							end
						end

						tbl30.syncRemotes.channelFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
							if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
								fn51(child)
							end
						end)

						task.spawn(function()
							for i = 1, 25 do
								task.wait(1)

								if not (not tbl30.syncRemotes or not tbl30.plots) then
									for _, child in ipairs(tbl30.syncRemotes.channelFolder:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
											pcall(fn51, child)
										end
									end

									continue
								end

								break
							end
						end)

						tbl30.syncRemotes.routeRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg)
							for _, v78 in ipairs(arg) do
								local v79 = v78[1]
								local str9 = tostring(v78[2])

								if tbl30.plots:FindFirstChild(str9) then
									if v79 == "ListenerAdded" then
										local v80 = tbl30.syncRemotes.channelFolder:FindFirstChild(str9)

										if v80 and v80:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
											fn51(v80)
										end
									elseif v79 == "ListenerRemoved" then
										fn52(str9)
									end
								end
							end
						end)

						tbl30.initted = true
						return true
					end

					do
						local function fn48_readPlotOwner(arg)
							local v78 = arg:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
							local frame2 = v78 and v78:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui") and v78.SurfaceGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
							frame2 = frame2 and frame2:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
							if not frame2 or frame2.Text == "Empty Base" then
								return nil
							end
							return frame2.Text:gsub("'s [Bb]ase$", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
						end

						local ownPlotCache = {}
						fn47 = function(arg)
							if not arg or not arg.plot then
								return false
							end
							-- perf: owner text parse (FindFirstChild chain + gsub) was running per animal per frame; cache 0.5s per plot
							local nowOwn = os.clock()
							local cachedOwn = ownPlotCache[arg.plot]
							if cachedOwn and nowOwn - cachedOwn.t < 0.5 and cachedOwn.p.Parent then
								return cachedOwn.v
							end
							local v78 = tbl30.plots:FindFirstChild(arg.plot)
							if not v78 then
								return false
							end
							local v79 = fn48_readPlotOwner(v78)
							local isOwn = v79 == localPlayer.DisplayName or v79 == localPlayer.Name
							ownPlotCache[arg.plot] = { t = nowOwn, p = v78, v = isOwn }
							return isOwn
						end
					end
				end

				local fn48_getPromptFromPlot, fn49_findNearestAnimal, fn50_doSemiSteal

				do
					local fn51

					do
						fn48_getPromptFromPlot = function(arg)
							if not arg then
								return nil
							end
							local v78 = tbl30.PromptMemoryCache[arg.uid]
							if v78 and v78.Parent then
								return v78
							end
							local v79 = tbl30.plots:FindFirstChild(arg.plot)
							if not v79 then
								return nil
							end
							local animalPodiums = v79:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
							if not animalPodiums then
								return nil
							end
							local v80 = animalPodiums:FindFirstChild(arg.slot)
							if not v80 then
								return nil
							end
							local base = v80:FindFirstChild("Base")
							if not base then
								return nil
							end
							local v81 = base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
							if not v81 then
								return nil
							end
							local v82 = v81:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
							if not v82 then
								return nil
							end

							for _, child in ipairs(v82:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
									tbl30.PromptMemoryCache[arg.uid] = child
									return child
								end
							end

							return nil
						end

						do
							local podiumCache = {}
							local function fn52(arg)
								-- perf: cache the podium Instance (static); position is still read live
								local podiumKey = tostring(arg.plot) .. "|" .. tostring(arg.slot)
								local cachedPodium = podiumCache[podiumKey]
								if cachedPodium and cachedPodium.Parent then
									return cachedPodium:GetPivot().Position
								end
								local v78 = tbl30.plots:FindFirstChild(arg.plot)
								if not v78 then
									return nil
								end
								local animalPodiums = v78:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
								if not animalPodiums then
									return nil
								end
								local v79 = animalPodiums:FindFirstChild(arg.slot)
								if not v79 then
									return nil
								end
								podiumCache[podiumKey] = v79
								return v79:GetPivot().Position
							end

							fn51 = function(arg)
								local character = localPlayer.Character
								if not character then
									return math.huge
								end
								local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
								if not humanoidRootPart then
									return math.huge
								end
								local v78 = fn52(arg)
								if not v78 then
									return math.huge
								end
								return (humanoidRootPart.Position - v78).Magnitude
							end

							fn49_findNearestAnimal = function()
								local character = localPlayer.Character
								if not character then
									return nil
								end
								local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
								if not humanoidRootPart then
									return nil
								end
								local huge = math.huge
								local v78 = nil

								for _, v79 in ipairs(tbl30.allAnimalsCache) do
									if not fn47(v79) then
										local v80 = fn52(v79)

										if v80 then
											local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - v80).Magnitude

											if magnitude <= tbl19.StealRadius and magnitude < huge then
												huge = magnitude
												v78 = v79
											end
										end
									end
								end

								if not v78 and tbl30.plots and tick() - (tbl30.semiFallbackAt or 0) > 0.25 then
									tbl30.semiFallbackAt = tick()
									for _, plot in ipairs(tbl30.plots:GetChildren()) do
										local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

										if podiums and not fn47({ plot = plot.Name }) then
											for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
												local spawnPart = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
												spawnPart = spawnPart and spawnPart:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
												local attachment = spawnPart and spawnPart:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
												local prompt = attachment and attachment:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")

												if prompt and tostring(prompt.ActionText):find("Steal") then
													local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - spawnPart.Position).Magnitude

													if magnitude <= tbl19.StealRadius and magnitude < huge then
														huge = magnitude
														v78 = { name = podium.Name, plot = plot.Name, slot = podium.Name, uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. podium.Name }
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end

								return v78
							end
						end
					end

					do
						local function fn52(arg)
							if tbl30.InternalStealCache[arg] then
								return
							end
							local tbl38 = { holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, ready = true }

							if getconnections then
								local ok, result = pcall(getconnections, arg.PromptButtonHoldBegan)

								if ok and type(result) == "table" then
									for _, v78 in ipairs(result) do
										local v79 = "function"

										if type(v78.Function) == v79 then
											table.insert(tbl38.holdCallbacks, v78.Function)
										end
									end
								end

								local ok2, result2 = pcall(getconnections, arg.Triggered)

								if ok2 and type(result2) == "table" then
									for _, v78 in ipairs(result2) do
										local v79 = "function"

										if type(v78.Function) == v79 then
											table.insert(tbl38.triggerCallbacks, v78.Function)
										end
									end
								end
							end

							if tbl38 then
								tbl30.InternalStealCache[arg] = tbl38
							end
						end

						fn50_doSemiSteal = function(arg, arg2)
							if not arg or not arg.Parent or not arg2 then
								return false
							end

							if tbl30.StealState.active then
								return false
							end

							if tick() - (tbl30.StealState.lastResultTime or 0) < tbl30.CFG.COOLDOWN then
								return false
							end
							fn52(arg)
							local v78 = tbl30.InternalStealCache[arg]
							if not v78 or not v78.ready then
								return false
							end

							if tween then
								tween:Cancel()
								tween = nil
							end

							v78.ready = false
							tbl30.StealState.active = true
							tbl30.StealState.startTime = tick()
							tbl30.StealState.phase = "stealing"
							tbl30.StealState.label = arg2.name or "Animal"
							flag16 = true
							n33 += 1
							local v79 = n33
							local startTime = tbl30.StealState.startTime
							local holdMin = tbl30.CFG.HOLD_MIN
							local holdMax = tbl30.CFG.HOLD_MAX
							textLabel.Text = "0%"
							frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
							uiStroke.Color = color

							if connection2 then
								connection2:Disconnect()
							end

							local connection3 = nil

							connection3 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								if v79 ~= n33 or not tbl30.StealState.active then
									connection3:Disconnect()

									if connection2 == connection3 then
										connection2 = nil
									end

									return
								end

								local n34 = math.clamp((tick() - startTime) / holdMin, 0, 1)
								frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(n34, 1)
								textLabel.Text = math.floor(n34 * 100) .. "%"
							end)

							connection2 = connection3

							task.spawn(function()
								local v80 = false

								for _, holdCallback in ipairs(v78.holdCallbacks) do
									task.spawn(function()
										pcall(holdCallback)
									end)
								end

								while true do
									if tbl30.enabled and tbl20.AutoSteal and M.StealMode == "SEMI" and v79 == n33 and tick() - startTime < holdMin then
										local primeRange = tbl19.StealRadius
										if not (fn51(arg2) > primeRange) then
											task.wait()
											continue
										end
									end

									break
								end

								local flag17 = v79 ~= n33 or not tbl30.enabled or not tbl20.AutoSteal

								if not flag17 then
									local primeRange = tbl19.StealRadius
									flag17 = fn51(arg2) > primeRange
								end

								if flag17 then
									if v79 == n33 then
										tbl30.StealState.active = false
										tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
										flag16 = false

										if connection2 == connection3 then
											connection2:Disconnect()
											connection2 = nil
										end

										fn39()
									end

									v78.ready = true
									return
								end

								tbl30.StealState.phase = "waitingRange"
								local stealRange = tbl30.CFG.STEAL_RANGE
								local flag18 = fn51(arg2) <= stealRange

								while tbl30.enabled and tbl20.AutoSteal and M.StealMode == "SEMI" and v79 == n33 and arg.Parent do
									local stealState, lastResult

									if holdMax < tick() - startTime then
										if v79 ~= n33 then
											v78.ready = true
											return
										end
										stealState = tbl30.StealState
										lastResult = v80 and "Stole " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label) or "Missed: " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label)
										stealState.lastResult = lastResult
										tbl30.StealState.active = false
										tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
										tbl30.StealState.lastResultTime = tick()
										flag16 = false

										if connection2 == connection3 then
											connection2:Disconnect()
											connection2 = nil
										end

										if v80 then
											frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
											textLabel.Text = "100%"
										else
											fn39()
										end

										task.wait(tbl30.CFG.COOLDOWN)
										v78.ready = true
										if v79 ~= n33 then
											return
										end
										fn39()
										return
									end

									local v81 = fn51(arg2)

									if v81 <= tbl30.CFG.STEAL_RANGE then
										if not flag18 then
											task.wait(tbl30.CFG.ENTRY_DELAY)
										end

										if v79 == n33 and tbl30.enabled and tbl20.AutoSteal and M.StealMode == "SEMI" then
											frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
											textLabel.Text = "100%"

											for _, triggerCallback in ipairs(v78.triggerCallbacks) do
												task.spawn(function()
													pcall(triggerCallback)
												end)
											end

											if #v78.triggerCallbacks == 0 then
												pcall(function()
													local stealAnimal = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("StealAnimal")
													if stealAnimal and arg2 and arg2.slot then
														stealAnimal:FireServer(arg2.slot)
													end
												end)
											end

											pcall(function()
												if M.AutoCarrySpeed and M.AutoCarrySpeed.WatchPickup then
													M.AutoCarrySpeed.WatchPickup(1.25)
												end
											end)

											v80 = true
											if v79 ~= n33 then
												v78.ready = true
												return
											end
											stealState = tbl30.StealState
											lastResult = v80 and "Stole " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label) or "Missed: " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label)
											stealState.lastResult = lastResult
											tbl30.StealState.active = false
											tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
											tbl30.StealState.lastResultTime = tick()
											flag16 = false

											if connection2 == connection3 then
												connection2:Disconnect()
												connection2 = nil
											end

											if v80 then
												frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
												textLabel.Text = "100%"
											else
												fn39()
											end

											task.wait(tbl30.CFG.COOLDOWN)
											v78.ready = true
											if v79 ~= n33 then
												return
											end
											fn39()
											return
										end

										if v79 ~= n33 then
											v78.ready = true
											return
										end
										stealState = tbl30.StealState
										lastResult = v80 and "Stole " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label) or "Missed: " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label)
										stealState.lastResult = lastResult
										tbl30.StealState.active = false
										tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
										tbl30.StealState.lastResultTime = tick()
										flag16 = false

										if connection2 == connection3 then
											connection2:Disconnect()
											connection2 = nil
										end

										if v80 then
											frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
											textLabel.Text = "100%"
										else
											fn39()
										end

										task.wait(tbl30.CFG.COOLDOWN)
										v78.ready = true
										if v79 ~= n33 then
											return
										end
										fn39()
										return
									end

									if v81 > tbl19.StealRadius then
										if v79 ~= n33 then
											v78.ready = true
											return
										end
										stealState = tbl30.StealState
										lastResult = v80 and "Stole " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label) or "Missed: " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label)
										stealState.lastResult = lastResult
										tbl30.StealState.active = false
										tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
										tbl30.StealState.lastResultTime = tick()
										flag16 = false

										if connection2 == connection3 then
											connection2:Disconnect()
											connection2 = nil
										end

										if v80 then
											frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
											textLabel.Text = "100%"
										else
											fn39()
										end

										task.wait(tbl30.CFG.COOLDOWN)
										v78.ready = true
										if v79 ~= n33 then
											return
										end
										fn39()
										return
									end

									task.wait()
								end

								if v79 ~= n33 then
									v78.ready = true
									return
								end
								tbl30.StealState.lastResult = v80 and "Stole " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label) or "Missed: " .. tostring(tbl30.StealState.label)
								tbl30.StealState.active = false
								tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
								tbl30.StealState.lastResultTime = tick()
								flag16 = false

								if connection2 == connection3 then
									connection2:Disconnect()
									connection2 = nil
								end

								if v80 then
									frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
									textLabel.Text = "100%"
								else
									fn39()
								end

								task.wait(tbl30.CFG.COOLDOWN)
								v78.ready = true
								if v79 ~= n33 then
									return
								end
								fn39()
							end)

							return true
						end
					end
				end

				fn31 = function()
					if not tbl30.plots or not tbl30.AnimalsData then
						return 0
					end
					local allAnimalsCache = {}

					for _, child in ipairs(tbl30.plots:GetChildren()) do
						local v78 = tbl30.plotSync.caches[child.Name]

						if v78 then
							local animalList = v78.AnimalList

							if typeof(animalList) == "table" then
								for k, v79 in pairs(animalList) do
									local v80 = "table"

									if type(v79) == v80 then
										local index = v79.Index
										local v81 = tbl30.AnimalsData[index]

										if v81 then
											table.insert(allAnimalsCache, {
												name = v81.DisplayName or index,
												plot = child.Name,
												slot = tostring(k),
												uid = child.Name .. "_" .. tostring(k),
											})
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					tbl30.allAnimalsCache = allAnimalsCache
					return #allAnimalsCache
				end

				do
					local fn51 = nil

					local function fn52()
						if tbl30.stealConn or tbl30.starting then
							return
						end
						tbl30.starting = true

						-- the heavy require/WaitForChild init used to run on the click path (up to ~25s of nothing = looked OFF); run it off-thread
						tbl30.plots = tbl30.plots or workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
						if not tbl30.initted and tick() - (tbl30.initTryAt or 0) > 30 then
							tbl30.initTryAt = tick()
							task.spawn(function()
								if pcall(fn46) and tbl30.initted then
									pcall(fn31)
								end
							end)
						end

						if not tbl30.plots then
							tbl30.starting = false
							return
						end
						tbl30.enabled = true
						pcall(fn31)

						if not tbl30.scanThread then
							tbl30.scanThread = task.spawn(function()
								while tbl30.enabled do
									pcall(fn31)
									task.wait(5)
								end

								tbl30.scanThread = nil
							end)
						end

						tbl30.stealConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							if not tbl30.enabled or not tbl20.AutoSteal then
								return
							end

							if M.StealMode ~= "SEMI" then
								fn51()
								return
							end

							if tbl30.StealState.active then
								-- watchdog: a steal thread that died mid-way would leave SEMI stuck "active" (looks like it is off)
								if tick() - (tbl30.StealState.startTime or 0) > (tbl30.CFG.HOLD_MAX or 2.6) + 3 then
									n33 += 1
									tbl30.StealState.active = false
									tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
									flag16 = false
									for _, cached in pairs(tbl30.InternalStealCache) do
										cached.ready = true
									end
									pcall(fn39)
								end
								return
							end
							local v78 = fn49_findNearestAnimal()
							if not v78 then
								return
							end
							local v79 = tbl30.PromptMemoryCache[v78.uid]

							if not v79 or not v79.Parent then
								v79 = fn48_getPromptFromPlot(v78)
							end

							if v79 then
								fn50_doSemiSteal(v79, v78)
							end
						end)
						tbl30.starting = false
					end

					fn51 = function()
						tbl30.starting = false
						tbl30.enabled = false
						n33 += 1

						if tbl30.stealConn then
							tbl30.stealConn:Disconnect()
							tbl30.stealConn = nil
						end

						tbl30.StealState.active = false
						tbl30.StealState.phase = "idle"
						flag16 = false

						if connection2 then
							connection2:Disconnect()
							connection2 = nil
						end

						fn39()
					end

					fn32 = function()
						if M.StealMode == "SEMI" then
							fn45()
							fn52()
						else
							fn51()
							fn44()
						end
					end

					fn33 = function()
						fn45()
						fn51()
					end
				end
			end

			do
				do
					do
						if M.RagdollStealEnabled == nil then
							M.RagdollStealEnabled = true
						end

						do
							local function fn44()
								local meridianNormalStealRagdollState = M.NormalStealRagdollState

								if meridianNormalStealRagdollState and meridianNormalStealRagdollState.Destroy then
									pcall(meridianNormalStealRagdollState.Destroy)
								end

								local meridianNormalStealRagdollState2 = { active = false, token = 0, humanoid = nil, conns = {} }
								M.NormalStealRagdollState = meridianNormalStealRagdollState2
								M.NormalStealRagdollPaused = false

								local function fn45(arg)
									local v78 = meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.conns[arg]

									if v78 then
										pcall(function()
											v78:Disconnect()
										end)

										meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.conns[arg] = nil
									end
								end

								local function fn46(arg)
									if not arg then
										return false
									end
									local state = arg:GetState()
									return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
								end

								local function fn47()
									local character = localPlayer.Character
									local meridianStealBarMedusaPart = M.StealBarMedusaPart
									if meridianStealBarMedusaPart and meridianStealBarMedusaPart.Parent and character and meridianStealBarMedusaPart:IsDescendantOf(character) and meridianStealBarMedusaPart.Anchored then
										return 1.2
									end
									return 1.45
								end

								local function fn48_ragdollReset(arg)
									meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.token = meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.token + 1
									meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active = false
									M.NormalStealRagdollPaused = false

									if arg and tbl20.AutoSteal then
										pcall(fn32)
									end
								end

								local function fn49_ragdollStateChanged()
									local humanoid = meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.humanoid
									if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
										return
									end

									if M.RagdollStealEnabled ~= true then
										if meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active then
											fn48_ragdollReset(true)
										end

										return
									end

									if not tbl20.AutoSteal then
										if meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active then
											fn48_ragdollReset(false)
										end

										return
									end

									if fn46(humanoid) and not meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active then
										meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active = true
										meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.token = meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.token + 1
										local token = meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.token
										M.NormalStealRagdollPaused = true
										pcall(fn33)

										task.delay(fn47(), function()
											if meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.token ~= token or not meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active then
												return
											end
											M.NormalStealRagdollPaused = false
											meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active = false

											if M.RagdollStealEnabled == true and tbl20.AutoSteal then
												pcall(fn32)
											end
										end)
									end
								end

								local function fn50_ragdollCharSetup(character)
									fn45("state")

									if meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active then
										fn48_ragdollReset(false)
									end

									local humanoid = character and (character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10))
									meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.humanoid = humanoid
									if not humanoid then
										return
									end
									meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.conns.state = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(fn49_ragdollStateChanged)
									task.defer(fn49_ragdollStateChanged)
								end

								meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.Destroy = function()
									fn48_ragdollReset(false)
									fn45("state")
									fn45("character")
									meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.humanoid = nil
								end

								meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.SetEnabled = function(arg)
									M.RagdollStealEnabled = arg == true

									if not M.RagdollStealEnabled then
										if meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.active then
											fn48_ragdollReset(true)
										end
									else
										task.defer(fn49_ragdollStateChanged)
									end

									return M.RagdollStealEnabled
								end

								meridianNormalStealRagdollState2.conns.character = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn50_ragdollCharSetup)

								if localPlayer.Character then
									task.defer(fn50_ragdollCharSetup, localPlayer.Character)
								end
							end

							fn44()
						end
					end

					do
						tbl31 = {
							L = false,
							R = false,
							lRef = nil,
							rRef = nil,
							startL = nil,
							stopL = nil,
							startR = nil,
							stopR = nil,
						}

						M.AutoPathActive = false

						if type(M.StopAutoCarry) == "function" then
							pcall(M.StopAutoCarry)
						end

						do
							local function fn44()
								local tbl38 = {
									active = false,
									previous = nil,
									charConn = nil,
									nearConn = nil,
									nextNearScan = 0,
								}

								local function fn45()
									if M.RefreshMovementModes then
										pcall(M.RefreshMovementModes)
									end

									if M.SyncMobile then
										pcall(M.SyncMobile)
									end
								end

								local function fn46()
									if not tbl38.active then
										return
									end
									local previous = tbl38.previous
									local v78 = tbl38
									tbl38.active = false
									v78.previous = nil

									if previous then
										local v79 = tbl22
										local carry = previous.carry
										tbl22.family = previous.family
										v79.carry = carry
									end

									fn45()
								end

								local function fn47(arg)
									if tbl31.L or tbl31.R then
										local v78 = tbl38
										tbl38.active = false
										v78.previous = nil
										return
									end

									if not M.AutoCarry or not arg then
										fn46()
										return
									end

									if tbl38.active then
										if tbl38.previous and tbl22.family ~= tbl38.previous.family then
											tbl38.previous = { family = tbl22.family, carry = false }
										end

										if not tbl22.carry then
											tbl22.carry = true
											fn45()
										end

										return
									end

									tbl38.previous = { family = tbl22.family, carry = tbl22.carry }
									tbl38.active = true
									tbl22.carry = true
									fn45()
								end

								local function fn49_e()
									local flag17 = localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
									local flag18 = false

									if not flag17 then
										local _, _, nearDist = fn43()
										flag18 = nearDist ~= nil and nearDist <= 10
									end

									fn47(flag17 or flag18)
								end

								local function fn50_e()
									if tbl38.charConn then
										tbl38.charConn:Disconnect()
										tbl38.charConn = nil
									end

									if tbl38.nearConn then
										tbl38.nearConn:Disconnect()
										tbl38.nearConn = nil
									end
								end

								M.StartAutoCarry = function()
									fn50_e()
									fn46()

									tbl38.charConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
										fn46()
									end)

									tbl38.nearConn = M.TickConn("autoCarry", 0.08, function()
										local now3 = os.clock()
										if now3 < tbl38.nextNearScan then
											return
										end
										tbl38.nextNearScan = now3 + 0.08
										fn49_e()
									end)

									fn49_e()
								end

								M.StopAutoCarry = function()
									fn50_e()
									fn46()
								end

								M.SyncAutoCarry = function()
									if not M.AutoCarry then
										return
									end

									fn49_e()
								end

								M.AutoCarrySelectFamily = function(family, arg)
									if family ~= "normal" and family ~= "lagger" then
										return false
									end

									if not tbl38.active then
										return false
									end
									tbl38.previous = { family = family, carry = arg == true }
									tbl22.family = family
									tbl22.carry = true
									fn45()
									return true
								end
							end

							fn44()
						end
					end

					do
						local function fn44()
							local apL1 = Vector3.new(-476.48, -6.28, 92.73)
						local apL2 = Vector3.new(-483.12, -4.95, 94.8)
						local apLFace = Vector3.new(-482.25, -6.93, 92.09)
						local apR1 = Vector3.new(-476.16, -6.28, 23.41)
						local apR2 = Vector3.new(-483.12, -5.03, 25.48)
						local apRFace = Vector3.new(-482.06, -6.93, 35.47)
						local function apRagdoll(h)
							if not h then
								return true
							end
							local st = h:GetState()
							return h.PlatformStand or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
						end
						local function apStep(root, hum, target, spd)
							local d = target - root.Position
							local u = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
							if u.Magnitude < 0.001 then
								return
							end
							u = u.Unit
							hum:Move(u, false)
							root.Velocity = Vector3.new(u.X * spd, root.Velocity.Y, u.Z * spd)
						end
						local connection3 = nil
							local connection4 = nil
							local n34 = 1
							local n35 = 1

							local function fn45()
								if M.RefreshMovementModes then
									M.RefreshMovementModes()
								end

								if M.SyncMobile then
									M.SyncMobile()
								end
							end

							local function fn46(arg)
								if M.AutoPathMatchSpeed then
									return
								end
								tbl22.family = "normal"
								tbl22.carry = arg and true or false
								fn45()
							end

							local function fn47()
								local character = localPlayer.Character
								local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local v78 = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								if v78 then
									v78:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
								end

								if humanoidRootPart then
									humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
								end

								fn46(true)
								M.AutoPathActive = tbl31.L or tbl31.R

								task.defer(function()
									local v79 = "function"

									if type(M.SyncAutoCarry) == v79 then
										pcall(M.SyncAutoCarry)
									end

									local v80 = "function"

									if type(fn29) == v80 then
										pcall(fn29)
									end
								end)
							end

							tbl31.stopL = function()
								if connection3 then
									connection3:Disconnect()
									connection3 = nil
								end

								pcall(function()
									RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("MeridianAutoPathL")
								end)

								n34 = 1
								tbl31.L = false

								if tbl31.lRef and tbl31.lRef.setState then
									tbl31.lRef.setState(false)
								end

								if M.SetMobileBtn then
									M.SetMobileBtn("autoLeft", false)
								end

								fn47()
							end

							tbl31.stopR = function()
								if connection4 then
									connection4:Disconnect()
									connection4 = nil
								end

								pcall(function()
									RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("MeridianAutoPathR")
								end)

								n35 = 1
								tbl31.R = false

								if tbl31.rRef and tbl31.rRef.setState then
									tbl31.rRef.setState(false)
								end

								if M.SetMobileBtn then
									M.SetMobileBtn("autoRight", false)
								end

								fn47()
							end

							tbl31.startL = function()
								if tbl31.R then
									tbl31.stopR()
								end

								if connection3 then
									connection3:Disconnect()
									connection3 = nil
								end

								n34 = 1
								tbl31.L = true
								M.AutoPathActive = true
								fn46(false)

								connection3 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
									if not tbl31.L then
										return
									end

									if not M.AutoPathMatchSpeed and (tbl22.family ~= "normal" or tbl22.carry) then
										fn46(false)
									end

									local character = localPlayer.Character
									if not character then
										return
									end
									local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
									if not root or not hum then
										return
									end

									if apRagdoll(hum) then
										hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
										return
									end
									local spd = fn38()

									if n34 == 1 then
										if (Vector3.new(apL1.X, root.Position.Y, apL1.Z) - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
											n34 = 2
											apStep(root, hum, apL2, spd)
											return
										end
										apStep(root, hum, apL1, spd)
									elseif n34 == 2 then
										if (Vector3.new(apL2.X, root.Position.Y, apL2.Z) - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
											hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
											root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
											n34 = 1
											if 0.5 < (apLFace - root.Position).Magnitude then
												root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, Vector3.new(apLFace.X, root.Position.Y, apLFace.Z))
											end
											tbl31.stopL()
											return
										end
										apStep(root, hum, apL2, spd)
									end
								end)
							end

							tbl31.startR = function()
								if tbl31.L then
									tbl31.stopL()
								end

								if connection4 then
									connection4:Disconnect()
									connection4 = nil
								end

								n35 = 1
								tbl31.R = true
								M.AutoPathActive = true
								fn46(false)

								connection4 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
									if not tbl31.R then
										return
									end

									if not M.AutoPathMatchSpeed and (tbl22.family ~= "normal" or tbl22.carry) then
										fn46(false)
									end

									local character = localPlayer.Character
									if not character then
										return
									end
									local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
									if not root or not hum then
										return
									end

									if apRagdoll(hum) then
										hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
										return
									end
									local spd = fn38()

									if n35 == 1 then
										if (Vector3.new(apR1.X, root.Position.Y, apR1.Z) - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
											n35 = 2
											apStep(root, hum, apR2, spd)
											return
										end
										apStep(root, hum, apR1, spd)
									elseif n35 == 2 then
										if (Vector3.new(apR2.X, root.Position.Y, apR2.Z) - root.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
											hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
											root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
											n35 = 1
											if 0.01 < (apRFace - root.Position).Magnitude then
												root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, Vector3.new(apRFace.X, root.Position.Y, apRFace.Z))
											end
											tbl31.stopR()
											return
										end
										apStep(root, hum, apR2, spd)
									end
								end)
							end
						end

						fn44()
					end
				end

				do
					local function fn44(meridianSpeedControllerSession)
						M.SpeedControllerSession = meridianSpeedControllerSession
						-- speed method copied from the clean script: keep last move direction while a move key is held, snap velocity to speed, zero it when stopped
						local MOVE_KEYS = {
							[Enum.KeyCode.W] = true,
							[Enum.KeyCode.A] = true,
							[Enum.KeyCode.S] = true,
							[Enum.KeyCode.D] = true,
							[Enum.KeyCode.Up] = true,
							[Enum.KeyCode.Left] = true,
							[Enum.KeyCode.Down] = true,
							[Enum.KeyCode.Right] = true,
						}
						local lastMoveDir = Vector3.zero

						M.SpeedControllerConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
							if M.SpeedControllerSession ~= meridianSpeedControllerSession then
								return
							end
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							if not character then
								return
							end
							local v78 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if not v78 or not humanoidRootPart then
								return
							end

							if tbl23 and (tbl23.aimbot or tbl23.desync) then
								return
							end
							if tbl31.L or tbl31.R then
								return
							end
							local state = v78:GetState()
							if v78.PlatformStand or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
								lastMoveDir = Vector3.zero
								return
							end
							local moveDirection = v78.MoveDirection
							local n34 = math.clamp(tonumber(fn38()) or 0, 0, 10000)

							if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
								lastMoveDir = moveDirection
							else
								moveDirection = nil
								if lastMoveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
									for k in pairs(MOVE_KEYS) do
										if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(k) then
											moveDirection = lastMoveDir
											break
										end
									end
								end
							end

							if moveDirection and moveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
								pcall(function()
									if humanoidRootPart.SetNetworkOwner then
										humanoidRootPart:SetNetworkOwner(localPlayer)
									end
								end)
								local unit = moveDirection.Unit
								humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(unit.X * n34, humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, unit.Z * n34)
							else
								humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
							end
						end)
					end

					fn44(tostring({}))
				end

				do
					do
						local v78 = nil

						local function fn44(arg)
							if not arg then
								return
							end
							local head = arg:FindFirstChild("Head") or arg:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
							if not head then
								return
							end
							local meridianSpeedBB = head:FindFirstChild("MeridianSpeedBB")

							if meridianSpeedBB then
								meridianSpeedBB:Destroy()
							end

							local accent = M.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
							local function gradSeq(c)
								return ColorSequence.new({
									ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c),
									ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.new(1, 1, 1):Lerp(c, 0.25)),
								})
							end
							local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui", head)
							billboardGui.Name = "MeridianSpeedBB"
							-- world-size (studs): stays the same size next to the character when you zoom in/out
							billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(5, 0, 1.55, 0)
							billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0)
							billboardGui.MaxDistance = 250
							billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
							billboardGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
							billboardGui.LightInfluence = 0
							local function styleText(lbl)
								lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
								lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Bangers
								lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
								lbl.TextScaled = true
								lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
								lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
							end
							local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", billboardGui)
							title.Name = "Title"
							title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.45, 0)
							title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
							title.Text = "MERIDIAN DUEL'S"
							styleText(title)
							local titleGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", title)
							titleGrad.Name = "MeridianSpeedGrad"
							titleGrad.Color = gradSeq(accent)
							local divider = Instance.new("Frame", billboardGui)
							divider.Name = "MeridianSpeedDivider"
							divider.Size = UDim2.new(0.96, 0, 0.035, 0)
							divider.Position = UDim2.new(0.02, 0, 0.5, 0)
							divider.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
							divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
							local divGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", divider)
							divGrad.Name = "MeridianSpeedGrad"
							divGrad.Color = gradSeq(accent)
							local textLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel", billboardGui)
							textLabel2.Name = "MeridianSpeedValue"
							textLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.4, 0)
							textLabel2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.58, 0)
							textLabel2.Text = "SPEED: 0"
							styleText(textLabel2)
							local valGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", textLabel2)
							valGrad.Name = "MeridianSpeedGrad"
							valGrad.Color = gradSeq(accent)
							v78 = textLabel2
						end

						task.spawn(function()
							while task.wait(1) do
								local character = localPlayer.Character

								if character then
									local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")

									if head and not head:FindFirstChild("MeridianSpeedBB") then
										pcall(function()
											fn44(character)
										end)
									end
								end
							end
						end)

						local charTok3471 = 0
						localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
							charTok3471 += 1
							local myTok = charTok3471
							task.wait(0.3)
							if charTok3471 ~= myTok then return end
							if localPlayer.Character ~= character then return end

							pcall(function()
								fn44(character)
							end)
						end)

						if localPlayer.Character then
							task.spawn(function()
								task.wait(0.3)

								pcall(function()
									fn44(localPlayer.Character)
								end)
							end)
						end

						RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							if not v78 or not v78.Parent then
								return
							end
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							if not character then
								return
							end
							local v79 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if not v79 then
								return
							end
							local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if not humanoidRootPart then
								return
							end

							if v79.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 or Vector3.new(humanoidRootPart.Velocity.X, 0, humanoidRootPart.Velocity.Z).Magnitude > 1 or tbl31.L or tbl31.R then
								local shownSpeed = fn38()
								if tbl23.aimbot then
									-- aimbot on: show the aimbot's own speed, not the speed mode
									shownSpeed = (M.BypassAimbotEnabled and tonumber(M.BypassAimbotSpeed)) or tbl23.aimSpd or shownSpeed
								end
								local speedText = "SPEED: " .. tostring(math.floor(shownSpeed * 10 + 0.5) / 10)
								if v78.Text ~= speedText then
									v78.Text = speedText
								end
							elseif v78.Text ~= "SPEED: 0" then
								v78.Text = "SPEED: 0"
							end
						end)
					end
				end

				M.ApplyWorldAccent = function(arg)
					local meridianAccent = arg or M.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 240)
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					character = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
					character = character and character:FindFirstChild("MeridianSpeedBB")

					if character then
						for _, child in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
							if child:IsA("UIGradient") and child.Name == "MeridianSpeedGrad" then
								child.Color = ColorSequence.new({
									ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, meridianAccent),
									ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.new(1, 1, 1):Lerp(meridianAccent, 0.25)),
								})
							end
						end
					end
				end

				flag14 = false
				fn34 = nil
				M.InfJumpMode = "HOLD"

				do
					local function fn44()
						UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
							if not flag14 then
								return
							end
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if not character then
								return
							end
							local velocity = character.Velocity
							character.Velocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 55, velocity.Z)
						end)

						UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
							if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Space or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
								return
							end

							task.delay(0.2, function()
								if flag14 and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
									local character = localPlayer.Character
									character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if character then
										local velocity = character.Velocity
										character.Velocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 50, velocity.Z)
									end
								end
							end)
						end)

						RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							if not flag14 or M.InfJumpMode ~= "HOLD" then
								return
							end
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							local v78 = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if not v78 then
								return
							end
							local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

							if (UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or humanoid and humanoid.Jump == true) and v78.Velocity.Y < 30 then
								local velocity = v78.Velocity
								v78.Velocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 50, velocity.Z)
							end

							if v78.Velocity.Y < -120 then
								local velocity = v78.Velocity
								v78.Velocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, -40, velocity.Z)
							end
						end)

						fn34 = function(arg)
							flag14 = arg and true or false
						end
					end

					fn44()
				end
			end
		end

		do
			local fn38, fn39

			do
				tbl32 = { enabled = false, conn = nil, ResetCooldown = 0 }

				if M.RagCountdownEnabled == nil then
				M.RagCountdownEnabled = true
				end

				if M.AntiRagMode ~= "V1" then
				M.AntiRagMode = "V2"
				end

				-- Anti Ragdoll V1: strips ragdoll constraints and boosts out of the ragdoll state
				local antiRagV1 = { active = false, boosting = false, char = nil, hum = nil, root = nil, oldSpeed = 16, conns = {} }

				local function antiRagV1Cache()
				local char = localPlayer.Character
				local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not hum or not root then
				return false
				end
				antiRagV1.char, antiRagV1.hum, antiRagV1.root = char, hum, root
				return true
				end

				local function antiRagV1Stop()
				antiRagV1.active = false
				if antiRagV1.boosting and antiRagV1.hum then
				pcall(function()
				antiRagV1.hum.WalkSpeed = antiRagV1.oldSpeed
				end)
				end
				antiRagV1.boosting = false
				for _, c in ipairs(antiRagV1.conns) do
				pcall(function()
				c:Disconnect()
				end)
				end
				antiRagV1.conns = {}
				antiRagV1.char, antiRagV1.hum, antiRagV1.root = nil, nil, nil
				end

				local function antiRagV1Start()
				if antiRagV1.active then
				return
				end
				antiRagV1Stop()
				if not antiRagV1Cache() then
				return
				end
				antiRagV1.active = true

				antiRagV1.conns[#antiRagV1.conns + 1] = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if camera and antiRagV1.hum then
				camera.CameraSubject = antiRagV1.hum
				end
				end)

				antiRagV1.conns[#antiRagV1.conns + 1] = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
				antiRagV1.boosting = false
				task.wait(0.5)
				antiRagV1Cache()
				end)

				task.spawn(function()
				while antiRagV1.active do
				task.wait()
				local hum, root = antiRagV1.hum, antiRagV1.root
				if hum and root and hum.Parent then
				local state = hum:GetState()
				local ragdolled = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
				if ragdolled then
				pcall(function()
				localPlayer:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
				end)
				for _, d in ipairs(antiRagV1.char:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or (d:IsA("Attachment") and d.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then
				d:Destroy()
				end
				end
				if not antiRagV1.boosting then
				antiRagV1.boosting = true
				antiRagV1.oldSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed
				hum.WalkSpeed = 400
				end
				if hum.Health > 0 then
				hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end
				root.Anchored = false
				elseif antiRagV1.boosting then
				antiRagV1.boosting = false
				hum.WalkSpeed = antiRagV1.oldSpeed
				end
				end
				end
				end)
				end

				fn35 = function()
				if M.AntiRagMode == "V1" then
				tbl32.enabled = true
				pcall(antiRagV1Start)
				return
				end
				if tbl32.conn then
				return
				end
				tbl32.enabled = true

				-- Anti Ragdoll V2 (from the clean): throttled GettingUp reset with a full state restore
				tbl32.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not tbl32.enabled then
				return
				end
				local character = localPlayer.Character
				if not character then
				return
				end
				local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoid or not root then
				return
				end
				if humanoid.Health <= 0 or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
				return
				end
				local state = humanoid:GetState()
				if state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
				return
				end
				local now2 = tick()
				if now2 - tbl32.ResetCooldown <= 0.15 then
				return
				end
				tbl32.ResetCooldown = now2

				pcall(function()
				if humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp then
				return
				end
				if humanoid.Health <= 0 or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
				return
				end
				humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

				for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then
				obj.Enabled = true
				end
				if obj:IsA("Constraint") then

						local function fn48_isAnimSkipTarget(arg)
							local character = arg:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool") and localPlayer.Character and arg:IsDescendantOf(localPlayer.Character)
							local flag28

							if character then
								local v78 = true
								flag28 = localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == v78
							else

							end

								return true
							end

								end

							local flag30 = false

								if not arg then
									break
								end

								if string.find(v78, "brainrot", 1, true) then
									return true
								end
