									flag29 = true
								end

								if arg:IsA("Model") and (arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController") or arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")) then
									flag30 = true
								end

								arg = arg.Parent
							end

							return flag29 and flag30
						end

						local function fn49_stripVisual(arg)
							if not M.VisualStripOn then
								return
							end

							pcall(function()
								local v78 = fn48_isAnimSkipTarget(arg)

								if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
									if not v78 then
										arg.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
										arg.MaterialVariant = ""
									end

									arg.Reflectance = 0
									arg.CastShadow = false
									local v79 = string.lower(arg.Name or "")

									if arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Light", true) or string.find(v79, "light", 1, true) or string.find(v79, "lamp", 1, true) then
										arg.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
									end

									if arg:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory") then
										arg.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
									end
								elseif arg:IsA("Shirt") or arg:IsA("Pants") or arg:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or arg:IsA("BodyColors") then
									arg:Destroy()
								elseif arg:IsA("FaceControls") then
									arg:Destroy()
								elseif arg:IsA("Decal") or arg:IsA("Texture") or arg:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
									if not v78 then
										arg:Destroy()
									end
								elseif arg:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
									if not v78 then
										arg.TextureId = ""
									end
								elseif arg:IsA("Animator") then
									local model = arg:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

									if not (model and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)) then
										for _, v79 in ipairs(arg:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
											pcall(function()
												v79:Stop(0)
												v79:AdjustSpeed(0)
											end)
										end

										table.insert(M.VisualStripConnections, arg.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg2)
											if M.VisualStripOn then
												pcall(function()
													arg2:Stop(0)
													arg2:AdjustSpeed(0)
												end)
											end
										end))
									end
								elseif arg:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or arg:IsA("Trail") or arg:IsA("Beam") or arg:IsA("Smoke") or arg:IsA("Fire") or arg:IsA("Sparkles") or arg:IsA("Highlight") then
									arg:Destroy()
								elseif arg:IsA("Light") then
									local basePart = arg:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")

									if basePart then
										basePart.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
									end

									arg:Destroy()
								elseif arg:IsA("ImageLabel") or arg:IsA("ImageButton") then
									arg.Image = ""
								elseif arg:IsA("Sky") or arg:IsA("Atmosphere") or arg:IsA("Clouds") or arg:IsA("PostEffect") then
									arg:Destroy()
								end
							end)
						end

						pcall(function()
							Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
							Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
							Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
							Lighting.FogStart = 1e9
							Lighting.FogEnd = 1e9
							local v78 = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

							if v78 then
								v78.Decoration = false
								v78.WaterWaveSize = 0
								v78.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
								v78.WaterReflectance = 0
								v78.WaterTransparency = 1
							end

							for _, descendant in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
								fn49_stripVisual(descendant)
							end
						end)

						if not M.LowEnd then
						task.spawn(function()
							for i, descendant in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
								if not M.VisualStripOn then
									return
								end
								fn49_stripVisual(descendant)

								if i % 20 == 0 then
									task.wait()
								end
							end
						end)
						end

						table.insert(M.VisualStripConnections, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
							task.defer(fn49_stripVisual, descendant)
						end))

						table.insert(M.VisualStripConnections, Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
							task.defer(fn49_stripVisual, descendant)
						end))
					end

					do
						local v78 = "function"

						if type(M.DestroyAntiDie) == v78 then
							pcall(M.DestroyAntiDie)
						elseif type(M.StopAntiDie) == "function" then
							pcall(M.StopAntiDie)
						end
					end
				end

				M.TpBatActive = false
				M.AntiDie = nil
				M.StartAntiDie = nil
				M.StopAntiDie = nil
				M.DestroyAntiDie = nil

				do
					local function fn48_antiDieSetup()
						local tbl37 = {
							healthThreshold = 25,
							fallDamageProtection = true,
							ragdollProtection = true,
							invincibilityFrames = 0.5,
							autoRevive = true,
						}

						local tbl38 = {
							userEnabled = false,
							tpBatActive = false,
							active = false,
							dropActive = false,
							invincibleUntil = 0,
							lastSafeCFrame = nil,
							lastSafePosition = nil,
							humanoid = nil,
							root = nil,
							conns = {},
						}

						local function fn49_h(arg)
							local v78 = tbl38.conns[arg]

							if v78 then
								pcall(function()
									v78:Disconnect()
								end)

								tbl38.conns[arg] = nil
							end
						end

						local function fn50_f()
							return tbl38.tpBatActive and M.TpBatActive == true and tbl23.desync == true
						end

						local function fn51(arg, arg2)
							if not arg or not arg.Parent then
								return
							end
							local lastSafeCFrame = tbl38.lastSafeCFrame

							if lastSafeCFrame then
								arg.CFrame = lastSafeCFrame + Vector3.new(0, arg2 or 3, 0)
							else
								arg.CFrame = CFrame.new(arg.Position.X, 8, arg.Position.Z)
							end

							arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

							pcall(function()
								arg.Velocity = Vector3.zero
								arg.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end)
						end

						local function fn52(arg)
							if not tbl38.active or not arg or not arg.Parent then
								return
							end
							local maxHealth = arg.MaxHealth > 0 and arg.MaxHealth or 100
							if arg.Health >= maxHealth and arg.Health > 0 then
								return
							end

							pcall(function()
								arg.Health = maxHealth
							end)

							local invincibilityFrames = tbl37.invincibilityFrames
							tbl38.invincibleUntil = tick() + invincibilityFrames

							pcall(function()
								for _, child in ipairs(arg.Parent:GetChildren()) do
									local str10 = child.Name:lower()
									local isNumberValue = child:IsA("NumberValue")

									if isNumberValue then
										isNumberValue = str10:find("health") or str10:find("hp") or str10:find("life")
									end

									if isNumberValue then
										child.Value = 100
									elseif child:IsA("BoolValue") and str10:find("dead") then
										child.Value = false
									end
								end

								if arg.Health < maxHealth then
									arg.Health = maxHealth
								end
							end)
						end

						local function fn53(arg, cameraSubject, arg2)
							if not tbl37.ragdollProtection or not cameraSubject then
								return
							end
							local state = cameraSubject:GetState()
							if state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
								return
							end

							pcall(function()
								for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
									if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
										descendant.Enabled = true
									end

									if descendant:IsA("Constraint") then
										descendant.Enabled = true
									end
								end

								cameraSubject.PlatformStand = false
								cameraSubject.Sit = false
								cameraSubject.AutoRotate = true
								cameraSubject:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)

								if arg2 then
									arg2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
									arg2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
								end

								local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

								if currentCamera then
									currentCamera.CameraSubject = cameraSubject
								end

								local playerScripts = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
								playerScripts = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
								playerScripts = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("ControlModule")

								if playerScripts then
									local module = require(playerScripts)

									if module and module.Enable then
										module:Enable()
									end
								end
							end)
						end

						local function fn54(arg, arg2)
							if not arg2 or not arg2.Parent or not arg or arg.Health <= 0 then
								return
							end
							local position = arg2.Position

							if position.Y > -30 and position.Y < 400 and arg2.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 50 then
								tbl38.lastSafeCFrame = arg2.CFrame
								tbl38.lastSafePosition = position
							end
						end

						local function fn55(arg, arg2, arg3)
							if not tbl37.fallDamageProtection or tbl38.dropActive or tbl26 and tbl26.active then
								return
							end
							local assemblyLinearVelocity = arg3.AssemblyLinearVelocity
							local assemblyAngularVelocity = arg3.AssemblyAngularVelocity
							local position = arg3.Position
							local magnitude = assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
							fn54(arg2, arg3)

							if magnitude >= 90 or math.abs(assemblyLinearVelocity.Y) >= 80 or assemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude >= 35 then
								arg3.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								arg3.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

								pcall(function()
									arg3.Velocity = Vector3.zero
									arg3.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
								end)

								if tbl38.lastSafePosition and (position - tbl38.lastSafePosition).Magnitude > 25 then
									arg3.CFrame = tbl38.lastSafeCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
								end
							end

							if position.Y < -100 or position.Y > 900 then
								fn51(arg3, 4)
								fn52(arg2)
							end

							if magnitude > 5000 then
								arg3.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
								arg3.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
								fn52(arg2)
							end
						end

						local function fn56(arg)
							if not tbl38.active or not arg then
								return
							end
							fn49_h("health")
							local humanoid = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)
							local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 2)
							if not tbl38.active or not humanoid or not humanoidRootPart then
								return
							end
							local v78 = tbl38
							tbl38.humanoid = humanoid
							v78.root = humanoidRootPart

							pcall(function()
								humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
								humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
							end)

							local v79 = tbl38
							local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
							tbl38.lastSafeCFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
							v79.lastSafePosition = position

							tbl38.conns.health = humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
								if not tbl38.active then
									return
								end

								if humanoid.Health <= 0 and tbl37.autoRevive then
									fn52(humanoid)
									fn51(humanoidRootPart, 10)

									pcall(function()
											humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)

									end)
								elseif humanoid.Health <= tbl37.healthThreshold then
									fn52(humanoid)
								end
							end)

							fn52(humanoid)
						end

						local function fn57()
							tbl38.active = false
							fn49_h("heartbeat")
							fn49_h("character")
							fn49_h("health")
							local v78 = tbl38
							tbl38.humanoid = nil
							v78.root = nil
							local v79 = tbl38
							tbl38.lastSafeCFrame = nil
							v79.lastSafePosition = nil
							tbl38.invincibleUntil = 0
						end

						local function fn58()
							if tbl38.active or not fn50_f() then
								return
							end
							tbl38.active = true
							fn56(localPlayer.Character)

							local charTok4866 = 0
							tbl38.conns.character = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
								charTok4866 += 1
								local myTok = charTok4866
								task.delay(0.1, function()
									if charTok4866 ~= myTok then return end
									if localPlayer.Character ~= character then return end
									if tbl38.active then
										fn56(character)
									end
								end)
							end)

							tbl38.conns.heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								if not tbl38.active or not fn50_f() then
									fn57()
									return
								end
								local character = localPlayer.Character
								local humanoid = tbl38.humanoid
								local root = tbl38.root
								if not character or not humanoid or not root or not humanoid.Parent or not root.Parent then
									return
								end
								tbl38.dropActive = tbl26 and tbl26.active == true

								pcall(function()
									humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
									humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
								end)

								if humanoid.Health <= 0 and tbl37.autoRevive then
									fn52(humanoid)
									fn51(root, 3)

									pcall(function()
										humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
									end)
								elseif humanoid.Health <= tbl37.healthThreshold then
									fn52(humanoid)
								end

								local invincibleUntil = tbl38.invincibleUntil

								if tick() < invincibleUntil and humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
									pcall(function()
										humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
									end)
								end

								fn53(character, humanoid, root)
								fn55(character, humanoid, root)
							end)
						end

						local meridianAntiDie = {
							Config = tbl37,
							toggle = function(arg)
								tbl38.userEnabled = arg == true

								if fn50_f() then
									fn58()
								else
									fn57()
								end
							end,
							start = function()
								tbl38.tpBatActive = true
								fn58()
							end,
							stop = function()
								tbl38.tpBatActive = false
								fn57()
							end,
							setDropActive = function(arg)
								tbl38.dropActive = arg == true
							end,
							destroy = function()
								local v78 = tbl38
								tbl38.userEnabled = false
								v78.tpBatActive = false
								fn57()
							end,
						}

						M.AntiDie = meridianAntiDie
						M.TpBatAntiDie = meridianAntiDie
						M.StartAntiDie = meridianAntiDie.start
						M.StopAntiDie = meridianAntiDie.stop
						M.DestroyAntiDie = meridianAntiDie.destroy
					end

					fn48_antiDieSetup()
				end
			end

			do
				do
					local function fn48_antiVoidSetup()
						if M.TpBatAntiDie then
							pcall(function()
								local meridianTpBatAntiDie = M.TpBatAntiDie

								if meridianTpBatAntiDie.destroy then
									meridianTpBatAntiDie.destroy()
								elseif meridianTpBatAntiDie.stop then
									meridianTpBatAntiDie.stop()
								end
							end)
						end

						if M.GlobalAntiVoid and M.GlobalAntiVoid.Destroy then
							pcall(M.GlobalAntiVoid.Destroy)
						end

						local meridianGlobalAntiVoid = {
							enabled = false,
							loop = nil,
							charConn = nil,
							suspendedUntil = 0,
							lastGroundCF = nil,
							lastGroundAt = 0,
							savedAt = 0,
							config = {
								voidY = -60,
								ceilingY = 900,
								fallSpeed = 180,
								lift = 3,
								saveInterval = 0.15,
								regrab = 0.28,
							},
						}

						local function fn49_i()
							local suspendedUntil = meridianGlobalAntiVoid.suspendedUntil
							return os.clock() < suspendedUntil
						end

						local function fn50_g(arg, arg2)
							local flag28 = not meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundCF
							local flag29

							if flag28 then
								flag29 = flag28
							else
								local lastGroundAt = meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundAt
								flag29 = os.clock() - lastGroundAt < meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.regrab
							end

							if flag29 then
								return
							end
							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundAt = os.clock()
							local cFrame = meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundCF + Vector3.new(0, meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.lift, 0)

							pcall(function()
								arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
								arg.CFrame = cFrame
								arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end)

							if arg2 then
								pcall(function()
									arg2.PlatformStand = false
									arg2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
								end)
							end
						end

						local function fn51()
							if not meridianGlobalAntiVoid.enabled or fn49_i() then
								return
							end
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							if not character then
								return
							end
							local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if not humanoidRootPart then
								return
							end
							local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
								return
							end
							local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
							local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
							local now2 = os.clock()

							if humanoid and humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 60 and position.Y > meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.voidY and position.Y < meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.ceilingY and now2 - meridianGlobalAntiVoid.savedAt >= meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.saveInterval then
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.savedAt = now2
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundCF = CFrame.new(position, position + humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector)
							end

							if position.Y < meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.voidY or position.Y > meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.ceilingY or assemblyLinearVelocity.Y < -meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.fallSpeed and position.Y < 0 then
								fn50_g(humanoidRootPart, humanoid)
							end
						end

						local function fn52()
							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.enabled = false

							if meridianGlobalAntiVoid.loop then
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.loop:Disconnect()
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.loop = nil
							end

							if meridianGlobalAntiVoid.charConn then
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.charConn:Disconnect()
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.charConn = nil
							end

							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundCF = nil
						end

						local function fn53()
							if meridianGlobalAntiVoid.enabled and meridianGlobalAntiVoid.loop then
								return
							end
							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.enabled = true
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if character and character.Position.Y > meridianGlobalAntiVoid.config.voidY then
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundCF = CFrame.new(character.Position, character.Position + character.CFrame.LookVector)
							end

							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.loop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(fn51)

							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.charConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
								local v78 = meridianGlobalAntiVoid
								local v79 = meridianGlobalAntiVoid
								meridianGlobalAntiVoid.lastGroundCF = nil
								v78.lastGroundAt = 0
								v79.savedAt = 0
							end)
						end

						meridianGlobalAntiVoid.SetEnabled = function(arg)
							if arg then
								fn53()
							else
								fn52()
							end
						end

						meridianGlobalAntiVoid.IsEnabled = function()
							return meridianGlobalAntiVoid.enabled
						end

						meridianGlobalAntiVoid.Suspend = function(arg)
							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.suspendedUntil = os.clock() + (tonumber(arg) or 6)
						end

						meridianGlobalAntiVoid.Resume = function()
							meridianGlobalAntiVoid.suspendedUntil = 0
						end

						meridianGlobalAntiVoid.Destroy = function()
							fn52()
							M.GlobalAntiVoid = nil
						end

						M.GlobalAntiVoid = meridianGlobalAntiVoid
						M.GlobalAntiVoidEnabled = true
						fn53()

						local tbl37 = {
							active = false,
							tpBatActive = false,
							conns = {},
							lastSafeCFrame = nil,
							lastSafePosition = nil,
							invincibleUntil = 0,
							humanoid = nil,
							originalBreakJoints = nil,
							originalDeadEnabled = nil,
						}

						local function fn54(arg)
							local v78 = tbl37.conns[arg]

							if v78 then
								pcall(function()
									v78:Disconnect()
								end)

								tbl37.conns[arg] = nil
							end
						end

						local function fn55(arg)
							if not tbl37.active or not arg or not arg.Parent then
								return
							end
							local maxHealth = arg.MaxHealth > 0 and arg.MaxHealth or 100

							pcall(function()
								arg.Health = maxHealth
							end)

							tbl37.invincibleUntil = os.clock() + 0.5

							pcall(function()
								for _, child in ipairs(arg.Parent:GetChildren()) do
									if child:IsA("NumberValue") then
										local str10 = child.Name:lower()

										if str10:find("health") or str10:find("life") or str10:find("hp") then
											child.Value = maxHealth
										end
									elseif child:IsA("BoolValue") and child.Name:lower():find("ragdoll") then
										child.Value = false
									end
								end
							end)
						end

						local function fn56()
							local humanoid = tbl37.humanoid

							if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
								pcall(function()
									if tbl37.originalBreakJoints ~= nil then
										humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = tbl37.originalBreakJoints
									end

									if tbl37.originalDeadEnabled ~= nil then
										humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, tbl37.originalDeadEnabled)
									end

									humanoid.PlatformStand = false
								end)
							end

							local v78 = tbl37
							local v79 = tbl37
							tbl37.humanoid = nil
							v78.originalBreakJoints = nil
							v79.originalDeadEnabled = nil
						end

						local function fn57(arg)
							if not tbl37.active or not arg then
								return
							end
							fn54("health")
							fn56()
							local v78 = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)
							if not tbl37.active or not v78 then
								return
							end
							local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							tbl37.humanoid = v78
							tbl37.originalBreakJoints = v78.BreakJointsOnDeath

							pcall(function()
								tbl37.originalDeadEnabled = v78:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
							end)

							pcall(function()
								v78.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
								v78:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
							end)

							if humanoidRootPart then
								local v79 = tbl37
								local cFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
								local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
								tbl37.lastSafeCFrame = cFrame
								v79.lastSafePosition = position
							end

							tbl37.conns.health = v78:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
								if tbl37.active and v78.Health <= 25 then
									fn55(v78)

									pcall(function()
										v78:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
									end)
								end
							end)
						end

						local function fn58()
							tbl37.active = false
							fn54("heartbeat")
							fn54("character")
							fn54("health")
							fn56()
							local v78 = tbl37
							local v79 = tbl37
							local v80 = 0
							tbl37.lastSafeCFrame = nil
							v78.lastSafePosition = nil
							v79.invincibleUntil = v80
						end

						local function fn59()
							if tbl37.active then
								return
							end
							tbl37.active = true
							fn57(localPlayer.Character)

							local charTok5268 = 0
							tbl37.conns.character = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
								charTok5268 += 1
								local myTok = charTok5268
								task.wait(0.1)
								if charTok5268 ~= myTok then return end
								if localPlayer.Character ~= character then return end

								if tbl37.active then
									fn57(character)
								end
							end)

							tbl37.conns.heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								if not tbl37.active then
									return
								end

								if not tbl37.tpBatActive then
									fn58()
									return
								end
								local character = localPlayer.Character
								local v78 = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								if not v78 or not humanoidRootPart then
									return
								end

								pcall(function()
									v78.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
									v78:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
								end)

								local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
								local assemblyAngularVelocity = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity
								local magnitude = assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
								local position = humanoidRootPart.Position

								if magnitude < 50 and position.Y > -30 and position.Y < 400 then
									local v79 = tbl37
									tbl37.lastSafeCFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
									v79.lastSafePosition = position
								end

								local flag28 = not tbl26.active

								if flag28 then
									flag28 = magnitude >= 90 or math.abs(assemblyLinearVelocity.Y) >= 80 or assemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude >= 40
								end

								if flag28 then
									pcall(function()
										humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
										humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
									end)

									if tbl37.lastSafePosition and (position - tbl37.lastSafePosition).Magnitude > 35 then
										local lookVector = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector

										pcall(function()
											humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(tbl37.lastSafePosition, tbl37.lastSafePosition + lookVector)
										end)
									end
								end

								if tbl37.lastSafeCFrame and (position.Y < -100 or position.Y > 900) then
									pcall(function()
										humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
										humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
										humanoidRootPart.CFrame = tbl37.lastSafeCFrame
									end)
								end

								local state = v78:GetState()

								if v78.PlatformStand or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
									pcall(function()
										v78.PlatformStand = false
										v78:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
									end)
								end

								local flag29 = v78.Health <= 30

								if not flag29 then
									local invincibleUntil = tbl37.invincibleUntil
									flag29 = os.clock() < invincibleUntil and v78.Health < v78.MaxHealth
								end

								if flag29 then
									fn55(v78)
								end
							end)
						end

						local meridianAntiDie = {
							start = function()
								tbl37.tpBatActive = true
								fn59()
							end,
							stop = function()
								tbl37.tpBatActive = false
								fn58()
							end,
							destroy = fn58,
						}

						M.AntiDie = meridianAntiDie
						M.TpBatAntiDie = meridianAntiDie
						M.StartAntiDie = meridianAntiDie.start
						M.StopAntiDie = meridianAntiDie.stop
						M.DestroyAntiDie = meridianAntiDie.destroy
					end

					fn48_antiVoidSetup()
				end

				M.StopVisualStrip = function()
					M.VisualStripOn = false
					local v78 = ipairs
					local meridianVisualStripConnections = M.VisualStripConnections or {}

					for _, meridianVisualStripConnection in v78(meridianVisualStripConnections) do
						pcall(function()
							meridianVisualStripConnection:Disconnect()
						end)
					end

					M.VisualStripConnections = {}
				end

			M.ClearSky = M.ClearSky or function()
				local v78 = ipairs
				local Lighting_2 = game:GetService("Lighting")

				for _, child in v78(Lighting_2:GetChildren()) do
					if child:GetAttribute("MeridianSky") then
						pcall(function()
							child:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end

				local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

				if terrain then
					for _, child in ipairs(terrain:GetChildren()) do
						if child:GetAttribute("_MeridianSky") then
							pcall(function()
								child:Destroy()
							end)
						end
					end
				end
			end

			M.StartSky = M.StartSky or function(arg)
				M.ClearSky()
				local Lighting_2 = game:GetService("Lighting")
				local meridianSkyMode = arg or M.SkyMode or "Off"
				local v78 = M.SkyPresets[meridianSkyMode]

				local function fn48_rgbFromArray(arg2)
					return Color3.fromRGB(arg2[1], arg2[2], arg2[3])
				end

				if not v78 or v78.kind == "off" then
					pcall(function()
						Lighting_2.FogEnd = 100000
						Lighting_2.FogStart = 0
						Lighting_2.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 200)
						Lighting_2.Brightness = 2
						Lighting_2.ClockTime = 14
						Lighting_2.GlobalShadows = true
						Lighting_2.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
						Lighting_2.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(127, 128, 127)
					end)

					M.SkyMode = "Off"
					return
				end

				Lighting_2.FogEnd = 100000
				Lighting_2.FogStart = 0
				Lighting_2.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 210, 200)
				Lighting_2.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
				Lighting_2.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
				Lighting_2.GlobalShadows = true
				Lighting_2.ClockTime = v78.clock or 14
				Lighting_2.Brightness = v78.brightness or 2

				if v78.outAmb then
					Lighting_2.OutdoorAmbient = fn48_rgbFromArray(v78.outAmb)
				end

				if v78.ambient then
					Lighting_2.Ambient = fn48_rgbFromArray(v78.ambient)
				end

				if v78.sky then
					local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
					sky:SetAttribute("MeridianSky", true)

					if v78.sky.stars then
						sky.StarCount = v78.sky.stars
					end

					if v78.sky.moon then
						sky.MoonAngularSize = v78.sky.moon
					end

					if v78.sky.sun then
						sky.SunAngularSize = v78.sky.sun
					end

					if v78.sky.moonTex then
						sky.MoonTextureId = "rbxasset://sky/moon.jpg"
					end

					sky.Parent = Lighting_2
				end

				if v78.atm then
					local atmosphere = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
					atmosphere:SetAttribute("_MeridianSky", true)
					atmosphere.Density = v78.atm.dens or 0.25
					atmosphere.Color = fn48_rgbFromArray(v78.atm.color)
					atmosphere.Decay = fn48_rgbFromArray(v78.atm.decay)
					atmosphere.Glare = v78.atm.glare or 1
					atmosphere.Haze = v78.atm.haze or 1
					atmosphere.Parent = Lighting_2
				end

				local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

				if v78.clouds and terrain then
					local clouds = Instance.new("Clouds")
					clouds:SetAttribute("MeridianSky", true)
					clouds.Cover = v78.clouds.cover or 0.5
					clouds.Density = v78.clouds.dens or 0.5
					clouds.Color = fn48_rgbFromArray(v78.clouds.color)
					clouds.Parent = terrain
				end

				M.SkyMode = meridianSkyMode
			end

			M.StopSky = M.StopSky or function()
				M.ClearSky()

				pcall(function()
					local Lighting_2 = game:GetService("Lighting")
					Lighting_2.ClockTime = 20
					Lighting_2.Brightness = 2
					Lighting_2.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
					Lighting_2.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(127, 127, 127)
				end)

				M.SkyMode = "Off"
			end

			do
				local connection2 = nil
				local connection3 = nil

				fn38 = function()
					local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
					if not currentCamera then
						return
					end

					pcall(function()
						currentCamera.FieldOfViewMode = Enum.FieldOfViewMode.Vertical
					end)

					if connection2 then
						connection2:Disconnect()
					end

					if connection3 then
						connection3:Disconnect()
					end

					connection3 = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
						if not tbl24.stretch then
							return
						end
						local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera

						if currentCamera2 and currentCamera2.FieldOfView ~= tbl24.fovVal then
							pcall(function()
								currentCamera2.FieldOfView = tbl24.fovVal
							end)
						end
					end)

					connection2 = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
						if not tbl24.stretch then
							return
						end
						local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera

						if currentCamera2 then
							currentCamera2.CFrame = currentCamera2.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0.65, 0, 0, 0, 1)
						end
					end)
				end

				fn39 = function()
					if connection2 then
						connection2:Disconnect()
						connection2 = nil
					end

					if connection3 then
						connection3:Disconnect()
						connection3 = nil
					end

					pcall(function()
						workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = 70
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		do
			do
				do
					local connection2 = nil

					fn40 = function()
						if connection2 then
							connection2:Disconnect()
						end

						connection2 = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
							if not tbl24.fov then
								return
							end
							local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

							if currentCamera and currentCamera.FieldOfView ~= tbl24.fovVal then
								pcall(function()
									currentCamera.FieldOfView = tbl24.fovVal
								end)
							end
						end)
					end

					fn41 = function()
						if connection2 then
							connection2:Disconnect()
							connection2 = nil
						end

						pcall(function()
							workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = 70
						end)
					end
				end

				infJump = true
				flag16 = true
				flag17 = false
				flag18 = true
				M.LoadedOnLookAtEnemy = false
				M.LoadedOnNeonWhiteTools = false
				M.meridianLoadedTryHard = false
				flag19 = true
				flag20 = false
				antiLag = true
				flag21 = false
				flag22 = true
				M.LoadedOnPotato = false
				flag23 = false
				flag24 = false
				flag25 = false

				do
					local function fn48_skinSystemInit()
						local HttpService2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
						local str10 = "meridianduel_skin_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json"

						local tbl37 = {
							MERIDIAN = { 10928435480, 6913613757, 9683332649 },
							RED = {
								107377896731216,
								215718515,
								82831174733357,
								122851471549264,
								139051312584295,
								124572458012582,
								105029428205930,
							},
							BLUE = { 76059443, 74891470, 1180433861, 8781331764, 8682542843 },
							GALAXY = {
							82831174733357,
							139051312584295,
							107062966543626,
							71184246276344,
							111383282415116,
							78289009309744,
							71017311084699,
							},
							["OUTFIT 1"] = { 10159600649 },
							["OUTFIT 2"] = { 1744060292 },
							["OUTFIT 3"] = { 8349240186 },
							["OUTFIT 4"] = { 121097973925756 },
							["HOMERO CHINO"] = { 103227869700418, 84952305140948, 122465238537030 },
							}

							-- extra per-preset data from the clean: shirt/pants templates, body colours, keep the head visible
							local tblClothes = {
							["OUTFIT 1"] = { shirt = 9683332638, pants = 93182020184041 },
							["OUTFIT 2"] = { shirt = 9683332638, pants = 93182020184041 },
							["OUTFIT 3"] = { shirt = 11926549070, pants = 13189494471 },
							["OUTFIT 4"] = { shirt = 123181702116947, pants = 93330291631062 },
							["HOMERO CHINO"] = {
							pants = 78591690208112,
							skinColor = Color3.fromRGB(234, 184, 146),
							headColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
							keepHead = true,
							},
							}

						local tbl38 = { MERIDIAN = { 263995361762992 } }
						local tbl39 = {}
						local flag28 = true
						local str11 = "MERIDIAN"
						local flag29 = false
						local tbl40 = nil

						local function fn49_j(arg)
							local v78 = arg or str11
							local tbl41 = {}
							local v79 = ipairs
							local tbl42 = tbl37[v78] or {}

							for _, v80 in v79(tbl42) do
								tbl41[#tbl41 + 1] = v80
							end

							local v80 = ipairs
							local tbl43 = tbl38[v78] or {}

							for _, v81 in v80(tbl43) do
								local tbl44 = tbl39[v81]

								if not tbl44 then
									tbl44 = {}

									local ok, result = pcall(function()
										return game:GetService("AssetService"):GetBundleDetailsAsync(v81)
									end)

									if ok and result and type(result.Items) == "table" then
										for _, item in ipairs(result.Items) do
											if item.Type == "Asset" and tonumber(item.Id) then
												tbl44[#tbl44 + 1] = tonumber(item.Id)
											end
										end
									end

									tbl39[v81] = tbl44
								end

								for _, v82 in ipairs(tbl44) do
									tbl41[#tbl41 + 1] = v82
								end
							end

							return tbl41
						end

						local function fn50_h()
							if M.UnifiedSaveActive and type(M.RequestSave) == "function" then
								M.RequestSave()
								return
							end

							if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
								return
							end
							pcall(writefile, str10, HttpService2:JSONEncode({ version = 2, enabled = flag28, preset = str11 }))
						end

						local function fn51()
							if type(readfile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then
								return
							end

							local ok, result = pcall(function()
								if not isfile(str10) then
									return nil
								end
								return HttpService2:JSONDecode(readfile(str10))
							end)

							if ok and type(result) == "table" and result.version == 2 then
								flag28 = result.enabled == true

								if tbl37[result.preset] then
									str11 = result.preset
								end
							end
						end

						local function fn52(arg)
							tbl40 = {}

							for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Accessory") or child:IsA("Shirt") or child:IsA("Pants") or child:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
									local ok, result = pcall(function()
										return child:Clone()
									end)

									if ok and result then
										tbl40[#tbl40 + 1] = result
									end
								end
							end
						end

						local function fn53(arg)
							for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
								if child:GetAttribute("MeridianSkin") or child:GetAttribute("MeridianVlonESkin") then
									child:Destroy()
								end
							end
						end

						local function fn54(parent, arg, arg2)
							arg2:SetAttribute("MeridianSkin", true)

							pcall(function()
								arg:AddAccessory(arg2)
							end)

							if arg2.Parent ~= parent then
								arg2.Parent = parent
							end

							local handle = arg2:FindFirstChild("Handle")
							if not handle or not handle:IsA("BasePart") or handle:FindFirstChild("AccessoryWeld") then
								return true
							end
							local attachment = handle:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Attachment")
							if not attachment then
								return true
							end
							local v78 = nil

							for _, descendant in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
								if descendant:IsA("Attachment") and descendant.Name == attachment.Name and not descendant:IsDescendantOf(arg2) then
									v78 = descendant
									break
								else
									v78 = nil
								end
							end

							local parent2 = v78 and v78.Parent

							if parent2 and parent2:IsA("BasePart") then
								handle.Anchored = false
								handle.CanCollide = false
								handle.CanTouch = false
								handle.CanQuery = false
								handle.Massless = true
								handle.CFrame = parent2.CFrame * v78.CFrame * attachment.CFrame:Inverse()
								local weldConstraint = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
								weldConstraint.Name = "MeridianAccessoryWeld"
								weldConstraint.Part0 = parent2
								weldConstraint.Part1 = handle
								weldConstraint.Parent = handle
							end

							return true
						end

						local function fn55(parent, arg, arg2)
							for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Accessory") then
									local flag30 = false

									pcall(function()
										flag30 = tonumber(child.SourceAssetId) == tonumber(arg2)
									end)

									if flag30 then
										return true
									end
								end
							end

							local ok, result = pcall(function()
								return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(arg2))
							end)

							if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" or #result == 0 then
								local v78 = nil
								if not (pcall(function()
									v78 = game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(arg2)
									end) and v78) then
									return false
								end
								result = { v78 }
							end

							local flag30 = false

							for _, v78 in ipairs(result) do
								local tbl41 = {}

								if v78:IsA("Accessory") or v78:IsA("Shirt") or v78:IsA("Pants") or v78:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
									tbl41[#tbl41 + 1] = v78
								else
									for _, descendant in ipairs(v78:GetDescendants()) do
										if descendant:IsA("Accessory") or descendant:IsA("Shirt") or descendant:IsA("Pants") or descendant:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
											tbl41[#tbl41 + 1] = descendant
										end
									end
								end

								for _, v79 in ipairs(tbl41) do
									local clone = v79:Clone()
									clone:SetAttribute("MeridianSkin", true)

									if clone:IsA("Accessory") then
										fn54(parent, arg, clone)
									else
										for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
											if child.ClassName == clone.ClassName then
												child:Destroy()
											end
										end

										clone.Parent = parent
									end

									flag30 = true
								end

								pcall(function()
									v78:Destroy()
								end)
							end

							return flag30
						end

						local origBodyColors = nil

						local function fnSkinKeepHead(preset)
						local cfg = tblClothes[preset or str11]
						return cfg ~= nil and cfg.keepHead == true
						end

						local function fnSkinRestoreColors(character)
						if not origBodyColors or not character then
						return
						end
						local bc = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
						if bc then
						bc:Destroy()
						end
						origBodyColors:Clone().Parent = character
						origBodyColors = nil
						end

						local function fnSkinClothes(model, preset)
						if not model then
						return
						end
						local cfg = tblClothes[preset or str11]
						local isPlayer = model == localPlayer.Character

						if not cfg or not cfg.skinColor then
						if isPlayer then
						fnSkinRestoreColors(model)
						end
						end

						if not cfg then
						return
						end

						for _, item in ipairs({ { "Shirt", "ShirtTemplate", cfg.shirt }, { "Pants", "PantsTemplate", cfg.pants } }) do
						if item[3] then
						local inst = model:FindFirstChildOfClass(item[1]) or Instance.new(item[1])
						inst[item[2]] = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(item[3])
						inst:SetAttribute("MeridianSkin", true)
						inst.Parent = model
						end
						end

						if cfg.skinColor then
						local bc = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
						if not bc then
						bc = Instance.new("BodyColors")
						bc.Parent = model
						elseif isPlayer and not origBodyColors then
						origBodyColors = bc:Clone()
						end
						local head = cfg.headColor or cfg.skinColor
						bc.HeadColor3 = head
						bc.TorsoColor3 = cfg.skinColor
						bc.LeftArmColor3, bc.RightArmColor3 = cfg.skinColor, cfg.skinColor
						bc.LeftLegColor3, bc.RightLegColor3 = cfg.skinColor, cfg.skinColor
						end
						end

						local function fn56(arg)
						local humanoidDescription = Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
							local v78 = nil

							if not pcall(function()
								v78 = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescriptionAsync(humanoidDescription, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
								end) or not v78 then
								pcall(function()
									v78 = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(humanoidDescription, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
								end)
							end

							humanoidDescription:Destroy()
							local humanoid = v78 and v78:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

							if v78 and humanoid then
								for _, v79 in ipairs(fn49_j(arg)) do
								pcall(fn55, v78, humanoid, v79)
								end
								pcall(fnSkinClothes, v78, arg)
								end

								return v78
								end

						local function fn57(arg, transparency)
							arg = arg and arg:FindFirstChild("Head")
							if not arg then
								return
							end
							transparency = transparency and 1 or 0
							arg.Transparency = transparency
							arg.LocalTransparencyModifier = transparency

							for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Decal") or child:IsA("Texture") or child:IsA("BasePart") then
									child.Transparency = transparency

									if child:IsA("BasePart") then
										child.LocalTransparencyModifier = transparency
									end
								end
							end
						end

						local function fn58(arg)
							if not arg then
								return
							end
							local meridianKorbloxRightLeg = arg:FindFirstChild("MeridianKorblox_RightLeg")

							if meridianKorbloxRightLeg then
								meridianKorbloxRightLeg:Destroy()
							end

							for _, v78 in ipairs({ "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" }) do
								local v79 = arg:FindFirstChild(v78)

								if v79 and v79:IsA("BasePart") then
									v79.Transparency = 0
									v79.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
								end
							end
						end

						local function fn59(parent)
							if not parent then
								return false
							end
							fn58(parent)
							local rightUpperLeg = parent:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
							if not rightUpperLeg then
								return false
							end

							local ok, result = pcall(function()
								return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://139607718")
							end)

							if not ok or not result or not result[1] then
								return false
							end
							local v78 = result[1]
							v78.Name = "MeridianKorblox_RightLeg"
							v78:SetAttribute("MeridianSkin", true)
							local isBasePart = v78:IsA("BasePart") and v78 or v78:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
							if not isBasePart then
								v78:Destroy()
								return false
							end

							for _, v79 in ipairs({ "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" }) do
								local v80 = parent:FindFirstChild(v79)

								if v80 and v80:IsA("BasePart") then
									v80.Transparency = 1
									v80.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
								end
							end

							isBasePart.Anchored = false
							isBasePart.CanCollide = false
							isBasePart.CanTouch = false
							isBasePart.CanQuery = false
							isBasePart.Massless = true
							isBasePart.CFrame = rightUpperLeg.CFrame
							local weldConstraint = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
							weldConstraint.Name = "MeridianKorbloxWeld"
							weldConstraint.Part0 = rightUpperLeg
							weldConstraint.Part1 = isBasePart
							weldConstraint.Parent = isBasePart
							v78.Parent = parent
							return true
						end

						local function fn60(arg)
							local v78 = fn56(arg)
							local humanoid = v78 and v78:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if not v78 or not humanoid then
								return v78
							end
							if not fnSkinKeepHead(arg) then
							fn57(v78, true)
							pcall(fn59, v78)
							end
							return v78
							end

						local function fn61()
							if flag29 or not flag28 then
								return
							end
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if not character or not humanoid then
								return
							end
							flag29 = true
							fn53(character)

							if not tbl40 then
								fn52(character)
							end

							for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Accessory") or child:IsA("Shirt") or child:IsA("Pants") or child:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
									child.Parent = nil
								end
							end

							local flag30 = false

							for _, v78 in ipairs(fn49_j()) do
								if not fn55(character, humanoid, v78) then
									flag30 = true
								end
							end

							if flag30 then
								local v78 = fn56()

								if v78 then
									for _, child in ipairs(v78:GetChildren()) do
										if child:IsA("Accessory") then
											if not character:FindFirstChild(child.Name) then
												fn54(character, humanoid, child:Clone())
											end
										end
									end

									v78:Destroy()
								end
							end

							pcall(fnSkinClothes, character, str11)

							if fnSkinKeepHead(str11) then
							fn58(character)
							fn57(character, false)
							else
							fn57(character, true)
							fn59(character)
							end
							flag29 = false
						end

						local function fn62()
							local character = localPlayer.Character
							if not character then
							return
							end
							fn53(character)
							pcall(fnSkinRestoreColors, character)
							fn58(character)
							fn57(character, false)

							if tbl40 then
								for _, v78 in ipairs(tbl40) do
									if v78 and v78.Parent == nil then
										v78:Clone().Parent = character
									end
								end
							end

							tbl40 = nil
						end

						fn51()

						M.Skin = {
							Presets = { "MERIDIAN", "RED", "BLUE", "GALAXY", "OUTFIT 1", "OUTFIT 2", "OUTFIT 3", "OUTFIT 4", "HOMERO CHINO" },
							IsEnabled = function()
								return flag28
							end,
							GetPreset = function()
								return str11
							end,
							SetState = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
								local v78 = string.upper(tostring(arg2 or str11))

								if tbl37[v78] then
										str11 = v78
								end

								flag28 = arg == true

								if flag28 then
									fn61()

									task.delay(0.8, function()
										if flag28 then
											fn61()
										end
									end)
								else
									fn62()
								end

								if not arg3 then
									fn50_h()
								end
							end,
							SetEnabled = function(arg)
								flag28 = arg == true

								if flag28 then
									fn61()

									task.delay(0.8, function()
										if flag28 then
											fn61()
										end
									end)
								else
									fn62()
								end

								fn50_h()
							end,
							SetPreset = function(arg)
								local v78 = string.upper(tostring(arg or ""))
								if not tbl37[v78] then
									return
								end
								str11 = v78

								if flag28 then
									fn61()
								end

								fn50_h()
							end,
							Apply = fn61,
							CreatePreviewRig = fn60,
						}

						localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
							tbl40 = nil

							if flag28 then
								task.delay(0.35, fn61)
								task.delay(1.15, fn61)
							end
						end)

						if flag28 and localPlayer.Character then
							task.defer(fn61)
						end
					end

					fn48_skinSystemInit()
				end
			end

			do
				M.SaveFiles = {
					config = "meridianduel_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
					pending = "meridianduel_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".pending.json",
					backup = "meridianduel_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".backup.json",
					controller = "meridianduel_controller_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
					legacyConfig = "AdaptHubV4_Settings_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
					legacyConfigBackup = "AdaptHubV4_Settings_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".backup.json",
					keybinds = "meridianduel_keybinds_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
					buttons = "meridianduel_buttons_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
					skin = "meridianduel_skin_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
					legacy = "AdaptHubMobile_Config_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
					legacyShared = "AdaptHubMobile_" .. tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. ".json",
				}

				M.UnifiedSaveActive = true

				M.SaveFiles.isfile = isfile or syn and syn.isfile or function(arg)
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						local v78 = readfile
						local readfile_

						if v78 then
							readfile_ = v78
						else
							readfile_ = syn and syn.readfile
						end

						return readfile_(arg)
					end)

					return ok and result ~= nil
				end

				M.SaveFiles.read = readfile or syn and syn.readfile
				M.SaveFiles.write = writefile or syn and syn.writefile

				do
					local meridianSaveFiles = M.SaveFiles
					local v78 = "function"
					local canSave = type(M.SaveFiles.read) == v78

					if canSave then
						local v79 = "function"
						canSave = type(M.SaveFiles.write) == v79
					end

					meridianSaveFiles.canSave = canSave
				end

				-- one-time copy of the old save files so settings survive the rename
				pcall(function()
					local sf = M.SaveFiles
					if not (sf.canSave and sf.isfile and sf.read and sf.write) then return end
					local uid = tostring(localPlayer.UserId)
					for _, pair in ipairs({
						{ "AdaptHubV5_All_" .. uid .. ".json", sf.config },
						{ "AdaptHubV5_All_" .. uid .. ".backup.json", sf.backup },
						{ "AdaptHubV5_Controller_" .. uid .. ".json", sf.controller },
						{ "AdaptHubV4_Keybinds_" .. uid .. ".json", sf.keybinds },
						{ "AdaptHubV4_Buttons_" .. uid .. ".json", sf.buttons },
						{ "AdaptSkin_" .. uid .. ".json", sf.skin },
					}) do
						if not sf.isfile(pair[2]) and sf.isfile(pair[1]) then
							local okR, data = pcall(sf.read, pair[1])
							if okR and data then pcall(sf.write, pair[2], data) end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			M.ResolveSaveApi = function()
				local genv = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
				local tbl37 = {}
				local v78 = readfile
				local readfile_ = syn and syn.readfile
				local readfile_2 = genv.readfile
				tbl37[1] = v78
				tbl37[2] = readfile_
				tbl37[3] = readfile_2
				local tbl38 = {}
				local v79 = writefile
				local writefile_ = syn and syn.writefile
				local writefile_2 = genv.writefile
				tbl38[1] = v79
				tbl38[2] = writefile_
				tbl38[3] = writefile_2

				for _, v80 in pairs(tbl37) do
					if type(v80) == "function" then
						M.SaveFiles.read = v80
						break
					end
				end

				for _, v80 in pairs(tbl38) do
					if type(v80) == "function" then
						M.SaveFiles.write = v80
						break
					end
				end

				M.SaveFiles.canSave = type(M.SaveFiles.read) == "function" and type(M.SaveFiles.write) == "function"
				return M.SaveFiles.canSave
			end

			M.SaveFiles.isfile = function(arg)
				if not M.ResolveSaveApi() then
					return false
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(M.SaveFiles.read, arg)
				return ok and type(result) == "string"
			end

			M.WriteJsonVerified = function(arg, arg2)
				if not M.ResolveSaveApi() then
					return false, "no file API"
				end

				local ok, json = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONEncode(arg2)
				end)

				if not ok or type(json) ~= "string" then
					return false, json
				end

				local ok2, err = pcall(M.SaveFiles.write, arg, json)
				if not ok2 then
					return false, err
				end

							return true
			end

			M.IsGamepadBindName = function(arg)
				if type(arg) ~= "string" then
					return false
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return Enum.KeyCode[arg]
				end)

				if not ok or not result then
					return false
				end
				local name = result.Name
				return name:sub(1, 6) == "Button" or name:sub(1, 4) == "DPad" or name:sub(1, 5) == "Thumb"
			end

			M.PersistControllerBindings = function()
				if M.SettingsReady ~= true then
					return false, "settings not ready"
				end
				local meridianCtrlSave = {}
				local v78 = pairs
				local meridianCtrlSave2 = M.CtrlSave or {}

				for k, v79 in v78(meridianCtrlSave2) do
					if type(k) == "string" then
						if v79 == "NONE" then
							meridianCtrlSave[k] = "NONE"
						elseif M.IsGamepadBindName(v79) then
							meridianCtrlSave[k] = v79
						end
					end
				end

				M.CtrlSave = meridianCtrlSave
				local v79, v80 = M.WriteJsonVerified(M.SaveFiles.controller, { version = 2, userId = localPlayer.UserId, savedAt = os.time(), bindings = meridianCtrlSave })

				if not v79 then
					warn("[MeridianControllerSave]", v80)
				end

				return v79, v80
			end

			M.LoadControllerBindings = function()
				if not M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.controller) then
					return false
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONDecode(M.SaveFiles.read(M.SaveFiles.controller))
				end)

				local flag28 = not ok or type(result) ~= "table"

				if not flag28 then
					local userId = localPlayer.UserId
					flag28 = tonumber(result.userId) ~= userId
				end

				if not flag28 then
					local v78 = "table"
					flag28 = type(result.bindings) ~= v78
				end

				if flag28 then
					return false
				end
				M.CtrlSave = M.CtrlSave or {}
				local v78 = 2
				local flag29 = (tonumber(result.version) or 0) >= v78

				for k, binding in pairs(result.bindings) do
					if type(k) == "string" and type(binding) == "string" then
						if flag29 and binding == "NONE" then
							M.CtrlSave[k] = "NONE"
						elseif M.IsGamepadBindName(binding) then
							M.CtrlSave[k] = binding
						end
					end
				end

				return true
			end

			M.HasSavedGamepadBind = function(arg)
				if type(arg) ~= "table" then
					return false
				end

				for _, v78 in pairs(arg) do
					if type(v78) == "string" and (v78:sub(1, 6) == "Button" or v78:sub(1, 4) == "DPad" or v78:sub(1, 5) == "Thumb") then
						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return Enum.KeyCode[v78]
						end)

						if ok and result then
							return true
						end
					end
				end

				return false
			end

			M.SaveButtonPlacements = function(arg)
				if type(M.RequestSave) == "function" then
					return M.RequestSave(arg == true)
				end
				return false
			end

			M.ResolveSaveApi()
			str9 = nil
			flag26 = false

			do
				local meridianBootSaveSession = M.BootSaveSession or {}
				M.BootSaveSession = nil
				M.SaveSession = meridianBootSaveSession

				M.SaveState = {
					lastAt = 0,
					queued = false,
					writing = false,
					dirty = false,
					revision = 0,
				}

				M.ConfigLoadReady = false
				M.SettingsReady = false

				fn42 = function()
					return M.SaveSession == meridianBootSaveSession
				end
			end
		end

		do
			do
				fn29 = function(arg)
					if not fn42() then
						return false
					end

					if M.SettingsReady ~= true then
						return false
					end

					if M.ConfigLoadReady ~= true then
						if not M.ResolveSaveApi() then
							return false
						end

						for _, v78 in ipairs({
							M.SaveFiles.config,
							M.SaveFiles.pending,
							M.SaveFiles.backup,
							M.SaveFiles.legacyConfig,
							M.SaveFiles.legacyConfigBackup,
							M.SaveFiles.keybinds,
							M.SaveFiles.buttons,
							M.SaveFiles.skin,
							M.SaveFiles.legacy,
							M.SaveFiles.legacyShared,
							"AdaptHubMobile.json",
							}) do
							if M.SaveFiles.isfile(v78) then
								str9 = "config was not loaded; overwrite blocked"
								flag26 = false
								print("[MeridianSave] BLOCKED - re-execute so the existing config can load safely")
								return false
							end
						end

						M.ConfigLoadReady = true
					end

					M.SaveState.dirty = true
					local writing = M.SaveState.writing
					local flag28

					if writing then
						flag28 = writing
					else
						local flag29 = not arg

						if flag29 then
							local lastAt = M.SaveState.lastAt
							flag28 = tick() - lastAt < 1.5
						else
							flag28 = flag29
						end
					end

					if flag28 then
						if not M.SaveState.queued then
							M.SaveState.queued = true
							local lastAt = M.SaveState.lastAt

							task.delay(math.max(0.2, 1.5 - (tick() - lastAt)), function()
								M.SaveState.queued = false
								fn29(true)
							end)
						end

						return true
					end

					str9 = nil

					if not M.ResolveSaveApi() then
						str9 = "no file api"
						flag26 = false
						print("[MeridianSave] FAIL - no readfile/writefile API in executor")
						return false
					end

					local tbl37 = {
						configVersion = 5,
						userId = localPlayer.UserId,
						revision = (M.SaveState.revision or 0) + 1,
					}

					tbl37.savedAt = os.time()
					tbl37.NS = tbl22.NS
					tbl37.CS = tbl22.CS
					tbl37.LG_N = tbl22.LG_N
					tbl37.LG_C = tbl22.LG_C
					tbl37.PL = tbl22.PL
					tbl37.family = tbl22.family
					tbl37.carry = tbl22.carry
					tbl37.aimSpd = tbl23.aimSpd
					tbl37.autoPathMatchSpeed = M.AutoPathMatchSpeed == true
					tbl37.fovVal = tbl24.fovVal
					tbl37.autoTPHeight = tbl29.height
					tbl37.lookAtEnemyRadius = M.LookAtEnemyRadius or 24
					tbl37.stealRadius = tbl19.StealRadius
					tbl37.stealDuration = tbl19.StealDuration
					tbl37.normalStealPausePercent = tbl19.NormalStealPausePercent
					tbl37.laggerKey = tbl27.key and typeof(tbl27.key) == "EnumItem" and tbl27.key.Name or nil
					tbl37.lagPackets = tbl27.packets
					tbl37.lagDelay = tbl27.delay
					tbl37.skyMode = M.SkyMode or "Lavender Dream"
					tbl37.animPack = M.AnimPack or "Amazon Unboxed"
					tbl37.keys = M.KbSave or {}
					tbl37.ctrlKeys = M.CtrlSave or {}
					tbl37.bgIdx = M.BgIdx ~= nil and M.BgIdx or 0
					tbl37.bgOrder = 5
					tbl37.btnImgIdx = M.BtnImgIdx or 1
					tbl37.btnImgOrder = 4
					tbl37.btnImgAsset = type(M.BtnImgAsset) == "string" and M.BtnImgAsset or nil
					tbl37.menuOpen = M.MenuOpen ~= false
					tbl37.themeIdx = M.ThemeIdx or 7
					tbl37.backgroundColorMatch = M.BackgroundColorMatch or false
					tbl37.introEnabled = M.IntroEnabled ~= false
					tbl37.introSongIndex = M.IntroSongIndex or 1
					tbl37.introSongLocked = M.IntroSongLocked == true
					tbl37.introStyle = M.IntroStyle or "random"
					tbl37.fontMode = M.FontMode or "DEFAULT"
					tbl37.dropMode = dropMode
					tbl37.tpMode = tpMode
					tbl37.infJumpMode = M.InfJumpMode or "TAP"
					tbl37.jumpPower = 55
					tbl37.batMode = M.BatMode or "DEFAULT"
					tbl37.tpBatMode = M.TpBatMode or "HIGH PING"
					tbl37.tpBatSwingDelay = M.TpBatSwingDelay or 0.007
					tbl37.stealMode = M.StealMode or "NORMAL"
					tbl37.autoCarryMode = M.AutoCarryMode or "WHEN NEAR"
					tbl37.semiRange = tbl30 and tbl30.CFG and tbl30.CFG.STEAL_RANGE or 10
					tbl37.mainPos = M.MainPos
					tbl37.btnScale = M.MobileBtnScale or 1
					tbl37.btnCircle = M.CircleButtons == true
					tbl37.mobileButtonsHidden = M.MobileButtonsHidden == true
					tbl37.hiddenMobileButtons = M.HiddenMobileButtons or {}
					tbl37.uiScale = M.UIScale or 65
					tbl37.stealBarScale = M.StealBarScale or meridianStealBarScale
					tbl37.stealBarPos = M.StealBarPos
					tbl37.btnLocked = M.MobileBtnLocked
					tbl37.uiLocked = M.UiLocked or false
					tbl37.mobileButtonProfiles = M.MobileBtnPositions or {}

					tbl37.skin = {
						enabled = M.Skin and M.Skin.IsEnabled() == true or false,
						preset = M.Skin and M.Skin.GetPreset() or "MERIDIAN",
					}

					tbl37.on = {
						antiRag = tbl32.enabled or false,
						lookAtEnemy = M.LookAtEnemyEnabled == true,
						neonWhiteTools = M.NeonWhiteToolsEnabled == true,
						autoTP = tbl29.enabled or false,
						autoSteal = tbl20.AutoSteal or false,
						ragdollSteal = M.RagdollStealEnabled ~= false,
						batAimbot = tbl23.aimbot == true or M.SafeModeState and M.SafeModeState.pending and M.SafeModeState.pending.aimbot == true or false,
						tpBat = tbl23.desync or false,
						mirrorTP = tbl23.tpMirror or false,
						desyncMode = "V2",
						autoLeft = false,
						autoRight = false,
						autoCarry = M.AutoCarry == true,
						safeMode = M.SafeModeEnabled == true,
						kickWarning = M.KickWarningEnabled ~= false,
						autoSwing = tbl23.swing or false,
						fov = tbl24.fov or false,
						stretch = tbl24.stretch or false,
						antiLag = tbl24.antilag or false,
						potatoGraphics = M.PotatoGraphics == true,
						infJump = flag14 or false,
						tryHard = M.TryHard and M.TryHard.enabled or false,
						antiBat = M.AntiBat and M.AntiBat.enabled or false,
						batCounter = (M.OshaBatCounter and M.OshaBatCounter.on) or false,
						batCounterMode = M.OshaBatCounter and M.OshaBatCounter.mode or M.BatCounterMode or "V1",
						antiRagMode = M.AntiRagMode or "V2",
						medusaCounter = flag25 or false,
						ragCountdown = M.RagCountdownEnabled ~= false,
						esp = M.ESPEnabled or false,
						showTracer = M.ESPShowTracer == nil and true or M.ESPShowTracer,
					}

					local curSig
					do
						local _rev, _sav = tbl37.revision, tbl37.savedAt
						tbl37.revision, tbl37.savedAt = nil, nil
						local okSig, sig = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(tbl37) end)
						tbl37.revision, tbl37.savedAt = _rev, _sav
						if okSig then curSig = sig end
						if curSig and curSig == M.SaveState.lastSig then
							M.SaveState.dirty = false
							return true
						end
					end

					M.SaveState.writing = true

					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						if not fn42() then
							error("superseded save session")
						end

												if tick() - (M.SaveState.lastBackupAt or 0) > 60 and M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.config) then
							M.SaveState.lastBackupAt = tick()
							local ok, result = pcall(M.SaveFiles.read, M.SaveFiles.config)

							local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
								return HttpService:JSONDecode(result)
							end)

							ok2 = ok and ok2

							if ok2 then
								local v80 = "table"
								ok2 = type(result2) == v80
							end

							ok2 = ok2 and result2.configVersion == 5

							if ok2 then
								local userId = localPlayer.UserId
								ok2 = tonumber(result2.userId) == userId
							end

							ok2 = ok2 and type(result2.keys) == "table" and type(result2.ctrlKeys) == "table"

							if ok2 then
								local v80 = "table"
								ok2 = type(result2.on) == v80
							end

							if ok2 then
								local v80 = "table"
								ok2 = type(result2.mobileButtonProfiles) == v80
							end

							if ok2 and type(result2.skin) == "table" then
								pcall(function()
									M.SaveFiles.write(M.SaveFiles.backup, result)

									if M.SaveFiles.read(M.SaveFiles.backup) ~= result then
										error("backup read-back failed")
									end
								end)
							end
						end

						local v80, v81 = M.WriteJsonVerified(M.SaveFiles.config, tbl37)
						if not v80 then
							print("[MeridianSave] write failed, retrying once:", tostring(v81))
							task.wait(0.15)
							v80, v81 = M.WriteJsonVerified(M.SaveFiles.config, tbl37)
						end

						if not v80 then
							print("[MeridianSave] retry failed:", tostring(v81))
							error(v81)
						end
					end)

					M.SaveState.writing = false
					if not fn42() then
						return false
					end
					M.SaveState.lastAt = tick()

					if not ok then
						str9 = tostring(result):sub(1, 60)
						flag26 = false
						print("[MeridianSave] FAIL:", result)
						return false
					end

					M.SaveState.revision = tbl37.revision
					M.SaveState.lastSig = curSig
					M.SaveState.dirty = false
					flag26 = true


					local tbl38 = {}

					for k, v78 in pairs(tbl37.on) do
						if v78 then
							tbl38[#tbl38 + 1] = k
						end
					end

					local concat = table.concat
					print(string.format("[MeridianSave] OK NS=%s CS=%s LG=%s/%s family=%s carry=%s sky=%s anim=%s drop=%s tp=%s inf=%s bat=%s ON=[%s]", tostring(tbl37.NS), tostring(tbl37.CS), tostring(tbl37.LG_N), tostring(tbl37.LG_C), tostring(tbl37.family), tostring(tbl37.carry), tostring(tbl37.skyMode), tostring(tbl37.animPack), tostring(tbl37.dropMode), tostring(tbl37.tpMode), tostring(tbl37.infJumpMode), tostring(tbl37.batMode), concat(tbl38, ",")))
					return true
				end

				M.RequestSave = function(arg)
					if not fn42() then
						return false
					end
					M.SaveState.dirty = true
					return fn29(arg == true)
				end

				fn43 = function()
					if not M.ResolveSaveApi() then
						print("[MeridianLoad] SKIP - no readfile/writefile API")
						return
					end
					-- drop flags left over from a previous execution so the result only depends on the saved config
					M.loadedOn.batAimbot = false
					M.loadedOn.desyncAimbot = false
					M.loadedOn.autoCarry = false
					M.loadedOn.autoSwing = false
					M.meridianLoadedAntiBat = false
					local meridianLoadedUnifiedV5 = false
					local revision = -1
					local flag28 = false
					local str10 = nil
					local str11 = nil
					local tbl37 = nil

					for _, v78 in ipairs({ M.SaveFiles.config, M.SaveFiles.pending, M.SaveFiles.backup }) do
						if M.SaveFiles.isfile(v78) then
							local ok, result = pcall(M.SaveFiles.read, v78)

							local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
								return HttpService:JSONDecode(result)
							end)

							local flag29

							if ok2 then
								local v79 = "table"
								flag29 = type(result2) == v79
							else
								flag29 = ok2
							end

							flag29 = flag29 and tonumber(result2.revision) or -1
							ok2 = ok and ok2 and type(result2) == "table" and result2.configVersion == 5

							if ok2 then
								local userId = localPlayer.UserId
								ok2 = tonumber(result2.userId) == userId
							end

							if ok2 and flag29 >= 0 and type(result2.keys) == "table" and type(result2.ctrlKeys) == "table" and type(result2.on) == "table" and type(result2.mobileButtonProfiles) == "table" and type(result2.skin) == "table" and flag29 > revision then
								str11 = result
								revision = flag29
								str10 = v78
								tbl37 = result2
							end

							flag28 = true
						end
					end

					if tbl37 then
						M.SaveState.revision = revision
						meridianLoadedUnifiedV5 = true
					else
						if flag28 then
							warn("[MeridianLoad] config/pending/backup unreadable or userId mismatch - trying legacy files, else defaults")
						end
						for _, v78 in ipairs({
							M.SaveFiles.legacyConfig,
							M.SaveFiles.legacyConfigBackup,
							M.SaveFiles.legacy,
							M.SaveFiles.legacyShared,
							"AdaptHubMobile.json",
							}) do
							if not tbl37 and M.SaveFiles.isfile(v78) then
								flag28 = true
								local ok, result = pcall(M.SaveFiles.read, v78)

								local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
									return HttpService:JSONDecode(result)
								end)

								ok2 = ok and ok2

								if ok2 then
									local v79 = "table"
									ok2 = type(result2) == v79
								end

								if ok2 then
									str11 = result
									tbl37 = result2
									str10 = v78
								end
							end
						end

						local flag29 = not tbl37 and not flag28
						local v78

						if flag29 then
							v78 = M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.keybinds) or M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.buttons) or M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.skin) or M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.controller)
						else
							v78 = flag29
						end

						if v78 then
							str11 = "{}"
							flag28 = true
							str10 = "V4 split files"
							tbl37 = {}
						end
					end

					if type(tbl37) ~= "table" then
						pcall(M.LoadControllerBindings)
						if flag28 then
							print("[MeridianLoad] FAIL - config files exist but none passed validation")
							return false
						end
						M.ConfigLoadReady = true
						print("[MeridianLoad] SKIP - no config yet")
						return true
					end

					M.LoadedUnifiedV5 = meridianLoadedUnifiedV5
					print(string.format("[MeridianLoad] Reading %d bytes from %s", #str11, str10))

					if type(tbl37.NS) == "number" then
						tbl22.NS = math.max(tbl37.NS, 0)
					end

					if type(tbl37.CS) == "number" then
						tbl22.CS = math.max(tbl37.CS, 0)
					end

					local v78 = "number"

					if type(tbl37.LG_N) == v78 then
						tbl22.LG_N = math.clamp(tbl37.LG_N, 0, 200)
					end

					if type(tbl37.LG_C) == "number" then
						tbl22.LG_C = math.clamp(tbl37.LG_C, 0, 65)
					end

					if type(tbl37.PL) == "number" then
						tbl22.PL = tbl37.PL
					end

					if type(tbl37.carry) == "boolean" then
						tbl22.carry = tbl37.carry
					end

					if tbl37.family == "normal" or tbl37.family == "lagger" or tbl37.family == "pinglagger" then
						tbl22.family = tbl37.family
					end

					if type(tbl37.aimSpd) == "number" then
						tbl23.aimSpd = tbl37.aimSpd
					end

					if type(tbl37.autoPathMatchSpeed) == "boolean" then
						M.AutoPathMatchSpeed = tbl37.autoPathMatchSpeed
					end

					if type(tbl37.fovVal) == "number" then
							tbl24.fovVal = tbl37.fovVal
					end

					if type(tbl37.autoTPHeight) == "number" then
						tbl29.height = tbl37.autoTPHeight
					end

					local v79 = "number"

					if type(tbl37.stealRadius) == v79 then
						tbl19.StealRadius = math.clamp(tbl37.stealRadius, 1, 250)
					end

					local v80 = "number"

					if type(tbl37.normalStealPausePercent) == v80 then
						tbl19.NormalStealPausePercent = M.NormalizeNormalPausePercent(tbl37.normalStealPausePercent)
					end

					if tbl37.fontMode == "PIXEL" or tbl37.fontMode == "DEFAULT" then
						M.FontMode = tbl37.fontMode
					end

					tbl19.StealDuration = 1.3

					if type(tbl37.laggerKey) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[tbl37.laggerKey] then
						tbl27.key = Enum.KeyCode[tbl37.laggerKey]
					end

					if type(tbl37.lagPackets) == "number" then
						tbl27.packets = math.clamp(math.floor(tbl37.lagPackets), 1, 1500)
					end

					local v81 = "number"

					if type(tbl37.lagDelay) == v81 then
						tbl27.delay = math.clamp(tbl37.lagDelay, 0.05, 5)
					end

					if type(tbl37.skyMode) == "string" then
						M.SkyMode = tbl37.skyMode
					end

					if type(tbl37.animPack) == "string" then
						M.AnimPack = tbl37.animPack
					end

					local v82 = tbl37

					if not meridianLoadedUnifiedV5 then
						pcall(function()
							if M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.keybinds) then
								local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(M.SaveFiles.read(M.SaveFiles.keybinds))

								if type(data) == "table" then
									v82 = data
								end
							end
						end)
					end

					if type(v82.keys) == "table" then
						M.KbSave = v82.keys
					end

					if type(v82.ctrlKeys) == "table" then
						M.CtrlSave = v82.ctrlKeys
					end

					if meridianLoadedUnifiedV5 and not M.HasSavedGamepadBind(M.CtrlSave) then
						pcall(function()
							if M.SaveFiles.isfile(M.SaveFiles.keybinds) then
								local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(M.SaveFiles.read(M.SaveFiles.keybinds))

								if type(data) == "table" and M.HasSavedGamepadBind(data.ctrlKeys) then
									M.CtrlSave = data.ctrlKeys
								end
							end
						end)
					end

					pcall(M.LoadControllerBindings)

					if type(v82.laggerKey) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[v82.laggerKey] then
						tbl27.key = Enum.KeyCode[v82.laggerKey]
					end

					if M.KbSave and not M.KbSave["TP Bat Key"] and M.KbSave["Desync Aimbot Key"] then
						M.KbSave["TP Bat Key"] = M.KbSave["Desync Aimbot Key"]
						M.KbSave["Desync Aimbot Key"] = nil
					end

					if M.CtrlSave and not M.CtrlSave["TP Bat Key"] and M.CtrlSave["Desync Aimbot Key"] then
						M.CtrlSave["TP Bat Key"] = M.CtrlSave["Desync Aimbot Key"]
						M.CtrlSave["Desync Aimbot Key"] = nil
					end

					if type(tbl37.bgIdx) == "number" and tbl37.bgIdx >= 0 and tbl37.bgIdx <= 10 then
						local meridianBgIdx = math.floor(tbl37.bgIdx)

						if tbl37.bgOrder ~= 2 and tbl37.bgOrder ~= 3 and tbl37.bgOrder ~= 4 and tbl37.bgOrder ~= 5 then
							meridianBgIdx = ({
								[0] = 0,
								1,
								[2] = 2,
								[3] = 5,
								[4] = 6,
								[10] = 7,
								[6] = 3,
								[7] = 8,
								[24] = 4,
							})[meridianBgIdx] or 0
						end

						if tbl37.bgOrder ~= 3 and tbl37.bgOrder ~= 4 and tbl37.bgOrder ~= 5 then
							-- 4 backgrounds were deleted: map the old 8-entry list onto the 4 that remain
							meridianBgIdx = ({ [0] = 0, [1] = 0, [2] = 1, [3] = 2, [4] = 0, [5] = 0, [6] = 0, [7] = 3, [8] = 4 })[meridianBgIdx] or 0
						end

						if tbl37.bgOrder == 4 then
							-- that list had no #5 (black removed): entries after it move back down by one
							meridianBgIdx = ({ [0] = 0, [1] = 1, [2] = 2, [3] = 3, [4] = 4, [5] = 6, [6] = 7, [7] = 8, [8] = 9, [9] = 10 })[meridianBgIdx] or 0
						end

					local tbl38 = {}
