					local concat = table.concat
					print(string.format("[MeridianLoad] OK NS=%s family=%s carry=%s sky=%s drop=%s tp=%s bat=%s ON=[%s]", tostring(tbl37.NS), tostring(tbl37.family), tostring(tbl37.carry), tostring(tbl37.skyMode), tostring(tbl37.dropMode), tostring(tbl37.tpMode), tostring(tbl37.batMode), concat(tbl38, ",")))
					M.ConfigLoadReady = true
					return true
				end

				fn44 = function()
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					if not character then
						return nil
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Tool") and (child.Name:lower():find("bat") or child.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
							return child
						end
					end

					local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")

					if backpack then
						for _, child in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Tool") and (child.Name:lower():find("bat") or child.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
								return child
							end
						end
					end

					return nil
				end

				fn45 = function()
					local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if not humanoidRootPart then
							return nil
						end
						local huge = math.huge
						local v78 = nil

						for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
								local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								if humanoidRootPart2 and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
									local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

									if magnitude < huge then
										huge = magnitude
										v78 = humanoidRootPart2
									end
								end
							end
						end

						return v78

				end

				if M.StopBypassAimbot then
					pcall(M.StopBypassAimbot)
				end

				do
					local function fn48_bypassAimbotInit()
						local tbl37 = {
							enabled = false,
							equipped = false,
							target = nil,
							intendedVelocity = Vector3.zero,
							angularVelocity = nil,
							attachment = nil,
						}

						local tbl38 = {
							"LockBAV",
							"LockAngVel",
							"LockBodyAtt",
							"BatLock",
							"LockBAVAtt",
							"AutoBatAtt",
							"AutoBatAV",
							"AntiBatDet",
							"AntiAim",
							"VelocityLock",
						}

						local function fn49_k(arg)
							if not arg then
								return
							end

							pcall(function()
								for _, v78 in ipairs(tbl38) do
									local v79 = arg:FindFirstChild(v78)

									if v79 then
										v79:Destroy()
									end
								end

								for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
									local str10 = child.Name:lower()
									local flag28 = child.Name ~= "_GB_AngV" and child.Name ~= "_GB_Att"

									if flag28 then
										flag28 = str10:find("lockb") or str10:find("lockangv") or str10:find("batlock") or str10:find("antiaim") or str10:find("velocitylock")
									end

									if flag28 then
										child:Destroy()
									end
								end
							end)
						end

						local function fn51()
							pcall(function()
								if tbl37.angularVelocity and tbl37.angularVelocity.Parent then
									tbl37.angularVelocity:Destroy()
								end
							end)

							pcall(function()
								if tbl37.attachment and tbl37.attachment.Parent then
									tbl37.attachment:Destroy()
								end
							end)

							local v78 = tbl37
							tbl37.angularVelocity = nil
							v78.attachment = nil
						end

						-- BYPASS AIMBOT (text_12): ping + acceleration prediction, angular aim, velocity follow
						local BAT_V2_SPEED = 55
						local BAT_V2_HIT_DIST = 13
						local BAT_V2_LEAD_STUDS = 3
						local bpZ = {
							targetPlayer = nil,
							lastTargetPos = nil,
							targetVelocity = Vector3.zero,
							smoothedVelocity = Vector3.zero,
							velocityHistory = {},
							accelerationHistory = {},
							aerialVelocityHistory = {},
							previousDirection = nil,
							lastDirectionChangeTime = 0,
							airborneTime = 0,
							lastActivationTime = 0,
							currentPing = 0.1,
							realPingMs = 0,
						}
						local bpCfg = {
							FOLLOW_SPEED = BAT_V2_SPEED,
							ACTIVATE_DISTANCE = BAT_V2_HIT_DIST,
							MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE = 1,
							PREDICTION_TIME = 0.22,
							PREDICT_AHEAD = BAT_V2_LEAD_STUDS,
							MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE = 150,
							VELOCITY_SMOOTHING = 0.2,
							MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY = 80,
							SERVER_TICKRATE = 0.016666666666666666,
							MIN_PING_COMPENSATION = 0.03,
							MAX_PING_COMPENSATION = 0.25,
							ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT = 0.3,
							DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME = 0.12,
							QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER = 1.5,
							GRAVITY = 196.2,
							AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR = 0.8,
							MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME = 0.08,
						}

						local function bpAverage(vectors)
							if #vectors == 0 then
								return Vector3.zero
							end
							local sum = Vector3.zero
							for _, entry in ipairs(vectors) do
								sum += entry
							end
							return sum / #vectors
						end

						local function bpPush(history, entry, maxEntries)
							table.insert(history, entry)
							if #history > maxEntries then
								table.remove(history, 1)
							end
						end

						local function bpReset()
							bpZ.targetPlayer = nil
							bpZ.lastTargetPos = nil
							bpZ.targetVelocity = Vector3.zero
							bpZ.smoothedVelocity = Vector3.zero
							bpZ.velocityHistory = {}
							bpZ.accelerationHistory = {}
							bpZ.aerialVelocityHistory = {}
							bpZ.previousDirection = nil
							bpZ.airborneTime = 0
						end

						local function bpApplyAngularAim(rootPart, direction)
							if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then
								return
							end
							local crossVector = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector:Cross(direction.Unit)
							local angle = math.asin(math.clamp(crossVector.Magnitude, -1, 1))
							rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = crossVector.Magnitude > 0.1 and crossVector.Unit * angle * 80
								or Vector3.zero
						end

						local function bpUpdatePing()
							local ok, result = pcall(function()
								return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
							end)
							if ok and type(result) == "number" then
								bpZ.realPingMs = math.floor(result)
							end
							bpZ.currentPing = math.clamp(bpZ.realPingMs / 1000, bpCfg.MIN_PING_COMPENSATION, bpCfg.MAX_PING_COMPENSATION)
						end

						if not tbl28.bypassPingLoop then
							tbl28.bypassPingLoop = true
							task.spawn(function()
								while true do
									pcall(bpUpdatePing)
									task.wait(0.5)
								end
							end)
						end

						local function meridianStopBypassAimbot()
							tbl37.enabled = false
							tbl37.equipped = false
							tbl37.target = nil
							M.BypassAimbotEnabled = false
							M.BypassAimbotSpeed = nil

							if tbl28.bypassAimbot then
								tbl28.bypassAimbot:Disconnect()
								tbl28.bypassAimbot = nil
							end

							if tbl28.bypassSafety then
								tbl28.bypassSafety:Disconnect()
								tbl28.bypassSafety = nil
							end

							local character = localPlayer.Character
							local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

							if humanoid then
								humanoid.AutoRotate = true
							end

							if humanoidRootPart then
								humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end

							bpReset()
							bpZ.lastActivationTime = 0
							fn51()
						end

						local function meridianStartBypassAimbot()
							if tbl28.aimbot then
								tbl28.aimbot:Disconnect()
								tbl28.aimbot = nil
							end

							meridianStopBypassAimbot()
							tbl37.enabled = true
							tbl37.equipped = false
							tbl37.target = nil
							tbl37.intendedVelocity = Vector3.zero
							M.BypassAimbotEnabled = true
							M.BypassAimbotSpeed = bpCfg.FOLLOW_SPEED
							local startChar = localPlayer.Character
							local startRoot = startChar and startChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if startRoot then
								fn49_k(startRoot)
							end

							tbl28.bypassAimbot = M.TickConn("aimbotBypass", M.LowEnd and 0.033 or 0.016, function(deltaTime)
								if not tbl37.enabled or not tbl23.aimbot or not tbl23.antiMode then
									return
								end

								if _G.AceGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed then
									bpCfg.FOLLOW_SPEED = _G.AceGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
								else
									bpCfg.FOLLOW_SPEED = math.clamp(tbl23.aimSpd or BAT_V2_SPEED, 1, 200)
								end
								M.BypassAimbotSpeed = bpCfg.FOLLOW_SPEED

								local character2 = localPlayer.Character
								local rootPart = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local humanoid = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								if not rootPart or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									return
								end

								fn49_k(rootPart)
								humanoid.AutoRotate = false
								local tool = character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or fn44()

								if tool and tool.Parent ~= character2 then
									pcall(humanoid.EquipTool, humanoid, tool)
								end

								bpZ.targetPlayer = nil
								local targetRoot, targetHum = nil, nil
								local closest = math.huge

								for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
									if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
										local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

										if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
											local magnitude = (rootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude

											if magnitude < closest then
												closest = magnitude
												bpZ.targetPlayer = player
												targetRoot = hrp
												targetHum = hum
											end
										end
									end
								end

								if not targetRoot or not targetHum then
									bpReset()
									return
								end

								tbl37.target = targetRoot
								local position = targetRoot.Position
								local sampleDeltaTime = math.max(deltaTime, 0.0041666666666666666)

								if bpZ.lastTargetPos then
									local targetVelocity = (position - bpZ.lastTargetPos) / sampleDeltaTime
									local change = targetVelocity - bpZ.targetVelocity

									if bpCfg.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE < change.Magnitude then
										targetVelocity = bpZ.targetVelocity + change.Unit * bpCfg.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE
									end

									local flat = Vector3.new(targetVelocity.X, 0, targetVelocity.Z)

									if flat.Magnitude > bpCfg.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY then
										local capped = flat.Unit * bpCfg.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY
										targetVelocity = Vector3.new(capped.X, targetVelocity.Y, capped.Z)
									end

									bpPush(bpZ.accelerationHistory, (targetVelocity - bpZ.targetVelocity) / sampleDeltaTime, 4)
									bpPush(bpZ.velocityHistory, targetVelocity, 8)
									bpZ.targetVelocity = targetVelocity
									bpZ.smoothedVelocity = bpZ.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(targetVelocity, bpCfg.VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)
								end

								bpZ.lastTargetPos = position
								local isAirborne = targetHum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
								bpZ.airborneTime = isAirborne and bpZ.airborneTime + sampleDeltaTime or 0

								if isAirborne and bpZ.airborneTime >= bpCfg.MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME then
									bpPush(bpZ.aerialVelocityHistory, bpZ.targetVelocity, 6)
								elseif not isAirborne then
									bpZ.aerialVelocityHistory = {}
								end

								local smoothedVelocity = bpZ.smoothedVelocity

								if isAirborne and #bpZ.aerialVelocityHistory > 0 then
									local avg = bpAverage(bpZ.aerialVelocityHistory)
									smoothedVelocity = Vector3.new(avg.X, bpZ.targetVelocity.Y, avg.Z) * bpCfg.AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR
								end

								local changedDirectionQuickly = false
								local flatVelocity = Vector3.new(bpZ.targetVelocity.X, 0, bpZ.targetVelocity.Z)

								if flatVelocity.Magnitude > 5 then
									local unit = flatVelocity.Unit

									if bpZ.previousDirection and bpZ.previousDirection:Dot(unit) < 0.5 then
										changedDirectionQuickly = tick() - bpZ.lastDirectionChangeTime < bpCfg.DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME
										bpZ.lastDirectionChangeTime = tick()
									end

									bpZ.previousDirection = unit
								end

								local predictionTime = bpZ.currentPing + bpCfg.SERVER_TICKRATE

								if changedDirectionQuickly then
									predictionTime *= bpCfg.QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER
								end

								local predictedPosition = position
									+ smoothedVelocity * predictionTime
									+ bpAverage(bpZ.accelerationHistory)
										* bpCfg.ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT
										* predictionTime
										* predictionTime
										* 0.5
								local leadTime = bpCfg.PREDICTION_TIME * 1.1
								local leadPosition

								if isAirborne then
									leadPosition = predictedPosition
										+ smoothedVelocity * leadTime
										+ Vector3.new(0, -0.5 * bpCfg.GRAVITY * leadTime * leadTime, 0)
								else
									leadPosition = predictedPosition + smoothedVelocity * leadTime
								end

								local flatSmoothed = Vector3.new(smoothedVelocity.X, 0, smoothedVelocity.Z)

								if flatSmoothed.Magnitude > 1 then
									leadPosition += flatSmoothed.Unit * bpCfg.PREDICT_AHEAD
								end

								local targetDirection = leadPosition - rootPart.Position
								bpApplyAngularAim(rootPart, targetDirection)

								if (position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude <= bpCfg.ACTIVATE_DISTANCE and tick() - bpZ.lastActivationTime >= 0.3 then
									if tool then
										pcall(tool.Activate, tool)
									end

									bpZ.lastActivationTime = tick()
								end

								if bpCfg.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE < targetDirection.Magnitude then
									tbl37.intendedVelocity = targetDirection.Unit * bpCfg.FOLLOW_SPEED
									rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tbl37.intendedVelocity
								else
									rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y * 0.5, 0)
								end
							end)
						end

						if not tbl28.bypassRespawn then
							local charTok8239 = 0
							tbl28.bypassRespawn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
								charTok8239 += 1
								local myTok = charTok8239
								task.wait(0.5)
								if charTok8239 ~= myTok then return end
								if localPlayer.Character ~= character then return end
								tbl37.equipped = false
								tbl37.target = nil
								bpReset()
							end)
						end

						M.StartBypassAimbot = meridianStartBypassAimbot
						M.StopBypassAimbot = meridianStopBypassAimbot

						M.IsBypassAimbotActive = function()
							return tbl37.enabled == true
						end
					end

					fn48_bypassAimbotInit()
				end
			end


		end

		M.YoutAimbot = M.YoutAimbot or { hitCooling = false, previousAutoRotate = nil }

		fn46 = function()
			if tbl28.aimbot then
				tbl28.aimbot:Disconnect()
				tbl28.aimbot = nil
			end

			-- BYPASS mode = the text_12 engine only. DEFAULT mode falls through to the original aimbot below, untouched.
			if tbl23.antiMode and tbl23.aimbot and M.StartBypassAimbot then
				pcall(M.StartBypassAimbot)
				return
			elseif M.StopBypassAimbot then
				pcall(M.StopBypassAimbot)
			end

			local meridianYoutAimbot = M.YoutAimbot
			meridianYoutAimbot.hitCooling = false
			local humanoid = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid and meridianYoutAimbot.previousAutoRotate == nil then
				meridianYoutAimbot.previousAutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
			end

			local function fn48_nearestPlayerRoot(arg)
				local huge = math.huge
				local v78 = nil

				for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
						local humanoidRootPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local humanoid2 = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if humanoidRootPart and humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health > 0 then
							local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - arg.Position).Magnitude

							if magnitude < huge then
								huge = magnitude
								v78 = humanoidRootPart
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return v78
			end

			local function fn49_l(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
				if not arg or arg4 and meridianYoutAimbot.hitCooling then
					return
				end

				if arg4 then
					meridianYoutAimbot.hitCooling = true
				end

				pcall(function()
					if arg.Parent ~= arg3 then
						arg2:EquipTool(arg)
					end

					if arg.Parent ~= arg3 then
						return
					end
					local remoteEvent = arg5 and arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteEvent")

					if remoteEvent then
						remoteEvent:FireServer()
					else
						arg:Activate()
					end
				end)

				if arg4 then
					task.delay(0.08, function()
						meridianYoutAimbot.hitCooling = false
					end)
				end
			end

			tbl28.aimbot = M.TickConn("aimbot", M.LowEnd and 0.033 or 0.016, function()
				if not tbl23.aimbot then
					return
				end
				local character = localPlayer.Character
				local v78 = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local v79 = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not v78 or not v79 then
					return
				end
				v79.AutoRotate = false
				local v80 = fn48_nearestPlayerRoot(v78)
				if not v80 then
					return
				end

				if tbl23.antiMode then
					local n34 = v80.Position - v78.Position
					local vector = Vector3.new(n34.X, 0, n34.Z)
					local unit = vector.Magnitude > 0 and vector.Unit or Vector3.zero
					local n35 = math.clamp(tbl23.aimSpd or 60, 1, 200)
					local n36 = math.clamp((v80.Position.Y + 3.7 - v78.Position.Y) * 19.5, -70, 110)

					if v79.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
						n36 = math.max(n36, 13)
					end

					local v81 = 0.75
					v78.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v78.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(Vector3.new(unit.X * n35, n36, unit.Z * n35), v81)

					if n34.Magnitude > 0.1 then
						local v82, v83, v84 = (v78.CFrame:Inverse() * CFrame.lookAt(v78.Position, v80.Position)):ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
						v78.AssemblyAngularVelocity = v78.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.clamp(v82, -2.5, 2.5) * 42, math.clamp(v83, -1.6, 2.5) * 42, math.clamp(v84, -2.5, 2.5) * 42))
					end

					if tbl23.swing then
						local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or fn44()

						if tool and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
							fn49_l(tool, v79, character, true, false)
						end
					end

					return
				end

				if not character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
					local v81 = fn44()

					if v81 then
						pcall(function()
							v79:EquipTool(v81)
						end)
					end
				end

				local assemblyLinearVelocity = v80.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				local n34 = v80.Position + assemblyLinearVelocity * 0.14 + v80.CFrame.LookVector * 0.3 - v78.Position
				local vector = Vector3.new(n34.X, 0, n34.Z)
				local unit = vector.Magnitude > 0 and vector.Unit or Vector3.zero
				local n35 = math.clamp((v80.Position.Y + 3.7 - v78.Position.Y) * 19.5 + assemblyLinearVelocity.Y * 0.8, -70, 110)
				local n36

				if v79.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
					n36 = n35
				else
					n36 = math.max(n35, 13)
				end

				local aimSpeed = math.clamp(tbl23.aimSpd or 58, 1, 200)
				v78.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v78.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(Vector3.new(unit.X * aimSpeed, n36, unit.Z * aimSpeed), 0.8)
				local n37 = v80.Position + assemblyLinearVelocity * math.clamp(assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude / 150, 0.05, 0.2)

				if (n37 - v78.Position).Magnitude > 0.1 then
					local v81, v82, v83 = (v78.CFrame:Inverse() * CFrame.lookAt(v78.Position, n37)):ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
					v78.AssemblyAngularVelocity = v78.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(math.clamp(v81, -1.6, 2.5) * 42, math.clamp(v82, -1.6, 1.6) * 42, math.clamp(v83, -2.5, 2.5) * 42))
				end

				if (v78.Position - v80.Position).Magnitude <= 8 then
					local v81 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or fn44()

					if v81 and (v81.Name:lower():find("bat") or v81.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
						fn49_l(v81, v79, character, false, false)
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		fn47 = function()
			if tbl28.aimbot then
				tbl28.aimbot:Disconnect()
				tbl28.aimbot = nil
			end

			if M.StopBypassAimbot then
				pcall(M.StopBypassAimbot)
			end

			local meridianYoutAimbot = M.YoutAimbot
			meridianYoutAimbot.hitCooling = false
			local character = localPlayer.Character
			local v78 = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			character = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if character then
				character.AutoRotate = meridianYoutAimbot.previousAutoRotate == nil and true or meridianYoutAimbot.previousAutoRotate
				character.PlatformStand = false
			end

			meridianYoutAimbot.previousAutoRotate = nil

			if v78 then
				v78.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				v78.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end

		tbl36 = {}
		n33 = 0

		do
			local function fn48_mirrorTPCheck()
				return tbl23.aimbot == true
			end

			local function fn49_m()
				local character = localPlayer.Character
				local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				character = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not humanoidRootPart or not character or character.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end
				local now2 = tick()
				if now2 - n33 < 0.08 then
					return
				end
				n33 = now2
				local v78, v79 = humanoidRootPart.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
				humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position.X, -7, humanoidRootPart.Position.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, v79, 0)
				humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero

				pcall(function()
					humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end)
			end

			local n34 = 0

			RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				local now2 = tick()
				if now2 < n34 then
					return
				end
				n34 = now2 + 0.4
				if not M.TPMirrorEnabled or not fn48_mirrorTPCheck() then
					table.clear(tbl36)
					return
				end
				local character = localPlayer.Character
				character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not character then
					return
				end

				for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
						local humanoidRootPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if humanoidRootPart and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
							local n35 = humanoidRootPart.Position.X - character.Position.X
							local n36 = humanoidRootPart.Position.Z - character.Position.Z

							if n35 * n35 + n36 * n36 <= 64 then
								local y = humanoidRootPart.Position.Y
								local v78 = tbl36[player.UserId]

								if v78 and v78 - y >= 3 then
									pcall(fn49_m)
									table.clear(tbl36)
								end

								tbl36[player.UserId] = y
							else
								tbl36[player.UserId] = nil
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local fn48_tpBatStart, fn49_tpBatStop, fn50_mobileActionSync, fn51

	do
		do
			M.DesyncMode = "V2"
			M.BypassAimbotEnabled = false

			if M.TpBatStillCamera and M.TpBatStillCamera.Destroy then
				pcall(M.TpBatStillCamera.Destroy)
			end

			M.TpBatStillCamera = nil
			M.TpBatStillCameraEnabled = nil
			M.TpBatPrediction = nil
			M.TpBatResetPrediction = nil
			M.PredictTpBatPosition = nil

			do
				local function fn52()
					return math.clamp(tonumber(M.TpBatSwingDelay) or 0.007, 0.001, 1)
				end

				local function fn53()
					return tbl23.desyncSwing == true or tbl23.swing == true
				end

				local function fn54()
					return M.TpBatCameraSteering ~= false
				end

				M.TpBatLogic = {}

				M.YznHighPingV1 = M.YznHighPingV1 or {
					spin = 0,
					lastSwing = 0,
					SpinSpeed = 48,
					TeleportsPerFrame = 100,
					TargetCenterOffset = 0.9,
					SwingDelay = 0.02,
					OrbitRadius = 0.2,
					FrameSnaps = 1,
					HitSpam = 1,
				}

								M.TpBatLogic.steerCamera = function(arg)
					if not fn54() then
						return
					end
					local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

					if currentCamera then
						currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentCamera.CFrame.Position, arg.Position)
					end
				end

				-- undo everything TP Bat V1 changed (collisions, AutoRotate, stuck states) when it is turned off
				M.TpBatLogic.restoreV1 = function()
					local state = M.YznHighPingV1

					for part, canCollide in pairs(state and state.savedCollide or {}) do
						if part and part.Parent then
							pcall(function()
								part.CanCollide = canCollide
							end)
						end
					end

					if state then
						state.savedCollide = {}
					end

					local character = localPlayer.Character
					local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if humanoid then
						pcall(function()
							humanoid.AutoRotate = true
							humanoid.Sit = false
							humanoid.PlatformStand = false
							humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
						end)
					end
				end

				-- TP BAT V1 — teleports onto the nearest opponent (+2.5 studs), faces the camera at them,
				-- swings the bat and keeps the character recovered / non-colliding.
				M.TpBatLogic.runYznHighPingV1 = function(character, rootPart, humanoid, targetRoot, deltaTime)
					local state = M.YznHighPingV1
					local now = tick()

					-- recovery (throttled 0.05s): stand up, un-sit, leave Physics/Ragdoll states, no collisions
					if now - (state.lastRecovery or 0) >= 0.05 then
						state.lastRecovery = now
						pcall(function()
							if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
								humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
							end

							if humanoid.PlatformStand then
								humanoid.PlatformStand = false
							end

							if humanoid.Sit then
								humanoid.Sit = false
							end

							local humanoidState = humanoid:GetState()

							if humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
								humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
								humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
							end

							humanoid.AutoRotate = false
						end)

						if M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled then
							pcall(M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled, rootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", nil)
						end

						pcall(function()
							state.savedCollide = state.savedCollide or {}

							for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
								if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
									if state.savedCollide[descendant] == nil then
										state.savedCollide[descendant] = descendant.CanCollide
									end

									descendant.CanCollide = false
								end
							end
						end)
					end

					if not targetRoot or not targetRoot.Parent or (rootPart.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude > 100 then
						return
					end

					-- teleport on top of the target
					rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0))
					rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					M.TpBatLogic.steerCamera(targetRoot)

					-- swing
					local canSwing = fn53()

					if canSwing then
						canSwing = now - (state.lastSwing or 0) >= math.max(fn52(), 0.03)
					end

					if canSwing then
						state.lastSwing = now

						pcall(function()
							local bat = nil

							for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Tool") then
									local lowerName = child.Name:lower()

									if lowerName:find("bat") or lowerName:find("slap") then
										bat = child
										break
									end
								end
							end

							if not bat then
								local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")

								for _, child in ipairs(backpack and backpack:GetChildren() or {}) do
									if child:IsA("Tool") then
										local lowerName = child.Name:lower()

										if lowerName:find("bat") or lowerName:find("slap") then
											bat = child
											pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(child) end)
											break
										end
									end
								end
							end

							if bat and bat.Parent == character then
								pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
								local remoteEvent = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")

								if remoteEvent then
									pcall(function() remoteEvent:FireServer() end)
								end
							end
						end)
					end
				end

							end
		end

		do
						-- TP BAT V2 (HIGH PING) = Bat TP orbit loop from text_2
			M.IrishBatTP = {
				SPIN_SPEED = 11.0,
				RADIUS = 2.0,
				HEIGHT = 0.5,
				ACTIVATE_DELAY = 0.13,
				TARGET_REFRESH = 0.1,
				TOOL_CACHE_TIME = 0.5,
				angle = 0,
				target = nil,
				cachedTool = nil,
				cachedToolAt = 0,
				lastTargetAt = 0,
				lastActivate = 0,
			}

			M.TpBatLogic.resetIrishV2 = function()
				local irish = M.IrishBatTP
				irish.angle = 0
				irish.target = nil
				irish.cachedTool = nil
				irish.cachedToolAt = 0
				irish.lastTargetAt = 0
			end

			M.TpBatLogic.irishFindTool = function()
				local character = localPlayer.Character
				if not character then
					return nil
				end

				for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") and (child.Name:lower():find("bat") or child.Name:lower():find("tp")) then
						return child
					end
				end

				local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")

				if backpack then
					for _, child in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Tool") and (child.Name:lower():find("bat") or child.Name:lower():find("tp")) then
							return child
						end
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			M.TpBatLogic.irishNearestTarget = function()
				local myRoot = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not myRoot then
					return nil
				end

				local closest, chosen = math.huge, nil

				for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
						local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if root and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
							local dist = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude

							if dist < closest then
								closest = dist
								chosen = root
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return chosen
			end

			M.TpBatLogic.runIrishV2 = function(character, root, humanoid, dt)
				if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end

				local irish = M.IrishBatTP
				local now = tick()

				if not irish.cachedTool or not irish.cachedTool.Parent or (now - irish.cachedToolAt) > irish.TOOL_CACHE_TIME then
					irish.cachedTool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or M.TpBatLogic.irishFindTool()
					irish.cachedToolAt = now
				end

				if irish.cachedTool and irish.cachedTool.Parent ~= character then
					pcall(function()
						humanoid:EquipTool(irish.cachedTool)
					end)
				end

				if now - irish.lastTargetAt > irish.TARGET_REFRESH then
					irish.lastTargetAt = now
					irish.target = M.TpBatLogic.irishNearestTarget()
				end

				local target = irish.target

				if not target or not target.Parent then
					return
				end

				irish.angle += irish.SPIN_SPEED * dt

				if irish.angle > math.pi * 2 then
					irish.angle -= math.pi * 2
				end

				local position = target.Position
				local orbit = Vector3.new(
					position.X + math.cos(irish.angle) * irish.RADIUS,
					position.Y + irish.HEIGHT,
					position.Z + math.sin(irish.angle) * irish.RADIUS
				)

				root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(orbit, Vector3.new(position.X, orbit.Y, position.Z))
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

				if irish.cachedTool and now - irish.lastActivate >= irish.ACTIVATE_DELAY then
					irish.lastActivate = now
					pcall(function()
						irish.cachedTool:Activate()
					end)
				end
			end

			fn48_tpBatStart = function()
				M.TpBatActive = true
				M.TpBatLogic.resetIrishV2()
				M.TpBatSwingCooldown = false
				M.TPMirrorEnabled = tbl23.tpMirror == true

				if M.TpBatAntiDie then
					pcall(M.TpBatAntiDie.start)
				end

				if tbl28.desync then
					tbl28.desync:Disconnect()
					tbl28.desync = nil
				end

				if tbl28.desyncLowPing then
					tbl28.desyncLowPing:Disconnect()
					tbl28.desyncLowPing = nil
				end

				if tbl28.desyncPreSimulation then
					tbl28.desyncPreSimulation:Disconnect()
					tbl28.desyncPreSimulation = nil
				end

				tbl28.desync = M.TickConn("tpBat", 0.016, function(deltaTime)
					if not tbl23.desync then
						return
					end
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					if not character then
						return
					end
					local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
						return
					end
					if M.TpBatMode == "HIGH PING" then
						M.TpBatLogic.runIrishV2(character, humanoidRootPart, humanoid, deltaTime)
						return
					end

					local flag28 = fn45()
					if not flag28 then
						return
					end

					M.TpBatLogic.runYznHighPingV1(character, humanoidRootPart, humanoid, flag28, deltaTime)
				end)

				local charTok9494 = 0
				tbl28.desyncLowPing = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
					charTok9494 += 1
					local myTok = charTok9494
					if not tbl23.desync then
						return
					end
					task.wait(0.2)
					if charTok9494 ~= myTok then return end
					if localPlayer.Character ~= character then return end

					if M.TpBatAntiDie then
						pcall(M.TpBatAntiDie.start)
					end

					local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if humanoid then
						pcall(function()
							humanoid.AutoRotate = false
						end)
					end

					if M.TpBatMode == "HIGH PING" then
						M.TpBatLogic.resetIrishV2()
					end
				end)
			end

			fn49_tpBatStop = function()
				M.TpBatActive = false

				if M.TpBatAntiDie then
					pcall(M.TpBatAntiDie.stop)
				end

				if tbl28.desync then
					tbl28.desync:Disconnect()
					tbl28.desync = nil
				end

				if tbl28.desyncLowPing then
					tbl28.desyncLowPing:Disconnect()
					tbl28.desyncLowPing = nil
				end

				if tbl28.desyncPreSimulation then
					tbl28.desyncPreSimulation:Disconnect()
					tbl28.desyncPreSimulation = nil
				end

				if M.TpBatLogic and M.TpBatLogic.restoreV1 then
					pcall(M.TpBatLogic.restoreV1)
				end

				local character = localPlayer.Character
				local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart then
					pcall(function()
						if M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled then
							M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled(humanoidRootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", nil)
						end
					end)

					humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end

				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid then
					pcall(function()
						humanoid.PlatformStand = false
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
					end)
				end

				M.TpBatHitCooldown = false
				M.TpBatSwingCooldown = false
				M.TpBatLogic.resetIrishV2()
				table.clear(tbl36)
				n33 = 0
			end

			do
				local autoCounter = {
					on = false,
					active = false,
					wait = false,
					conn = nil,
					seen = {},
					t0 = 0,
					prevAim = false,
					prevDes = false,
					prevDSw = false,
					detectRange = 16,
					knockRange = 14,
					knockSpeed = 70,
					knockUp = 65,
					timeout = 12,
				}

				local function fn52(arg)
					if arg then
						if M.SafeModeBlockReason and M.SafeModeBlockReason("aimbot") then
							return
						end
						autoCounter.prevAim = tbl23.aimbot
						autoCounter.prevDes = tbl23.desync
						autoCounter.prevDSw = tbl23.desyncSwing

						if tbl23.desync then
							pcall(fn49_tpBatStop)
						end

						tbl23.desyncSwing = false

							pcall(fn49_tpBatStop)

						tbl23.desyncSwing = false

					if not arg then
						return false

					return arg.PlatformStand or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown

				autoCounter.start = function()
					if autoCounter.conn then
						return
					end

					autoCounter.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						if not autoCounter.on then
							return
						end
						local character = localPlayer.Character
						if not character then
							return
						end
						local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not humanoid or not humanoidRootPart or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
							return
						end
						local v78 = fn53(humanoid)

						if autoCounter.wait then
							if not v78 then
								autoCounter.wait = false
							end

							return
						end

						if not autoCounter.active then
							if not v78 then
								return
							end
							local v79 = nil

							for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
									local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if humanoidRootPart2 and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < autoCounter.detectRange then
										local v80 = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

										if v80 then
											local str10 = v80.Name:lower()

											if str10:find("bat") or str10:find("slap") then
												v79 = player
												break
											else
												v79 = nil
											end
										else
											v79 = nil
										end
									else
										v79 = nil
									end
								else
									v79 = nil
								end
							end

							if not v79 then
								return
							end
							autoCounter.active = true
							autoCounter.t0 = tick()
							autoCounter.seen = {}

							for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
									local humanoid2 = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

									if humanoid2 and fn53(humanoid2) then
										autoCounter.seen[player] = true
									end
								end
							end

							pcall(function()
								humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
								humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end)

							if not character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
								local v80 = fn44()

								if v80 then
									pcall(function()
										humanoid:EquipTool(v80)
									end)
								end
							end

							fn52(true)
							return
						end

						local t0 = autoCounter.t0

						if tick() - t0 > autoCounter.timeout then
							autoCounter.active = false
							autoCounter.wait = true
							fn52(false)
							return
						end

						for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character and not autoCounter.seen[player] then
								local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								local v79 = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								if humanoidRootPart2 and v79 and v79.Health > 0 and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < autoCounter.knockRange then
									local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity
									local knockSpeed = autoCounter.knockSpeed

									if Vector3.new(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, assemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude > knockSpeed or assemblyLinearVelocity.Y > autoCounter.knockUp or fn53(v79) then
										autoCounter.active = false
										autoCounter.wait = true
										fn52(false)
										return
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				autoCounter.stop = function()
					if autoCounter.conn then
						autoCounter.conn:Disconnect()
						autoCounter.conn = nil
					end

					if autoCounter.active then
						autoCounter.active = false
						fn52(false)
					end

					autoCounter.wait = false
				end

				autoCounter.setEnabled = function(arg)
					autoCounter.on = arg and true or false

					if autoCounter.on then
						autoCounter.start()
					else
						autoCounter.stop()
					end
				end

				M.AutoCounter = autoCounter
			end
		end

		do

									meridianTpFling.last[player] = v78.Position

											if not meridianTpFling.cool[player] or now2 - meridianTpFling.cool[player] > 3 then
												meridianTpFling.cool[player] = now2
												fn53(v78)

										end
									end

						end

						meridianTpFling.conn = nil

				local x = humanoidRootPart.Position.X

				tbl26.active = true

						M.DropConn:Disconnect()

					local character2 = localPlayer.Character

					local humanoid = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if meridianDropToken ~= M.DropToken or not v79 or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not tbl26.active then
						if M.DropConn then
							M.DropConn:Disconnect()
							M.DropConn = nil
						end

						tbl26.active = false
						M.DropInProgress = false
						fn50_mobileActionSync("Drop Brainrot", false)
						return
					end

					if n34 <= tick() - now2 then
						if M.DropConn then
							M.DropConn:Disconnect()
							M.DropConn = nil
						end

						pcall(function()
							meridianDropRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character2 }
							local hit2 = workspace:Raycast(v79.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.35, 0), Vector3.new(0, -2000, 0), meridianDropRayParams) or hit

							if hit2 then
								v79.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, hit2.Position.Y + humanoid.HipHeight + v79.Size.Y * 0.5 + 0.2, z) * rotation
								v79.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								v79.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end
						end)

						tbl26.active = false
						M.DropInProgress = false
						fn50_mobileActionSync("Drop Brainrot", false)
						return
					end

					v79.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 155, 0)

		M.OshaBatCounter.isRagdolled = function(arg, arg2)
			arg = arg and arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not arg then
				return false
			end
			local state = arg:GetState()
			return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or arg2 == true and state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
		end

		M.OshaBatCounter.getBat = function(arg)
			local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")

			for _, v78 in ipairs({ arg, backpack }) do
				if v78 then
					for _, child in ipairs(v78:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Tool") then
							local str10 = child.Name:lower()
							if str10:find("bat") or str10:find("slap") or str10:find("sword") or str10:find("knife") then
								return child
							end

					end
				end
			end

		end

			local meridianOshaBatCounter = M.OshaBatCounter
			if meridianOshaBatCounter.hitCooldown then
				return
			end
			meridianOshaBatCounter.hitCooldown = true

			pcall(function()
				local v78 = meridianOshaBatCounter.getBat(arg)
				local v79 = arg and arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not v78 or not v79 then
					return
				end

				if v78.Parent ~= arg then
					pcall(function() v79:EquipTool(v78) end)
				end

				pcall(function() v78:Activate() end)
				local remoteEvent = v78:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")

				if remoteEvent then
					pcall(function() remoteEvent:FireServer() end)
				end
			end)

			task.delay(0.08, function()
				meridianOshaBatCounter.hitCooldown = false
			end)
		end

		M.OshaBatCounter.closestAnimalCarrier = function()
				local character = localPlayer.Character
				local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart then
					return nil
				end
				local n34 = 12
				local v78 = nil

				for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					local character2 = player ~= localPlayer and player.Character
					local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local humanoid = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if humanoidRootPart2 and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
						local flag28 = false

						for _, child in ipairs(character2:GetChildren()) do
							local isTool = child:IsA("Tool")
							local handle

							if isTool then
								handle = child:FindFirstChild("Handle") or child.Name:find("Animal") or child.Name:find("Pet")
							else
								handle = isTool
							end

							if handle then
								flag28 = true
								break
							end
						end

						local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

						if flag28 and magnitude < n34 then
							n34 = magnitude
							v78 = player
						end
					end
				end

				return v78

		end

		M.OshaBatCounter.runV2 = function(arg)
			local meridianOshaBatCounter = M.OshaBatCounter
			local debounce = meridianOshaBatCounter.debounce

			if not debounce then
				local lastV2 = meridianOshaBatCounter.lastV2
				debounce = tick() - lastV2 < 0.01
			end

			if debounce then
				return
			end
			local v78 = meridianOshaBatCounter.closestAnimalCarrier()
			if not v78 or meridianOshaBatCounter.isRagdolled(v78.Character, false) then
				return
			end
			local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local humanoidRootPart2 = v78.Character and v78.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoidRootPart2 then
				return
			end
			local now2 = tick()
			meridianOshaBatCounter.debounce = true
			meridianOshaBatCounter.lastV2 = now2

			pcall(function()
				if M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled then
					M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled(humanoidRootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", humanoidRootPart2)
				end

				local n34 = humanoidRootPart2.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)

				if 24 < (humanoidRootPart.Position - n34).Magnitude then
					humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(n34)
				end

				meridianOshaBatCounter.hit(arg)

				task.delay(0.05, function()
					meridianOshaBatCounter.hit(arg)
				end)

				task.delay(0.4, function()
					meridianOshaBatCounter.hit(arg)
				end)
			end)

			task.delay(0.2, function()
				if not tbl23.desync and humanoidRootPart.Parent and M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled then
					pcall(M.VoltHiddenPropertyDisabled, humanoidRootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", nil)
				end

				meridianOshaBatCounter.debounce = false
			end)
		end

			if meridianOshaBatCounter.connection then

				if not meridianOshaBatCounter.on or meridianOshaBatCounter.debounce then

				local character = localPlayer.Character
				local v78 = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						meridianOshaBatCounter.hit(character)

						meridianOshaBatCounter.debounce = false
					end)

			end)
		end

				local function fn55()

				local function fn56()
					local meridianMedusaState = M.MedusaState
					if meridianMedusaState.debounce then
						return
					end
					local lastUsed = meridianMedusaState.lastUsed
					if tick() - lastUsed < meridianMedusaState.cooldown then
						return
					end
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					if not character then
						return
					end
					meridianMedusaState.debounce = true
					local v78 = fn55()
					if not v78 then
						meridianMedusaState.debounce = false
						return
					end

					if v78.Parent ~= character then
						local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if humanoid then
							pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(v78) end)
						end
					end

					pcall(function()
						v78:Activate()
					end)

					meridianMedusaState.lastUsed = tick()
					meridianMedusaState.debounce = false
				end

				M.StopMedusaCounter = function()
					local meridianMedusaState = M.MedusaState

					for _, anchorConn in pairs(meridianMedusaState.anchorConns) do
						pcall(function()
							anchorConn:Disconnect()
						end)
					end

					meridianMedusaState.anchorConns = {}
				end

				M.SetupMedusaCounter = function(arg)
					M.StopMedusaCounter()
					local meridianMedusaState = M.MedusaState
					if not arg then
						return
					end

					local function fn57(arg2)
						return arg2:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
							if flag25 and arg2.Anchored and arg2.Transparency == 1 then
								fn56()
							end
						end)
					end

					for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							table.insert(meridianMedusaState.anchorConns, fn57(descendant))
						end
					end

					table.insert(meridianMedusaState.anchorConns, arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							table.insert(meridianMedusaState.anchorConns, fn57(descendant))
						end
					end))
				end
			end

			fn54()
		end
	end

	local fn54

	do
		do
			M.StartMedusaCounter = function()
				flag25 = true

				if localPlayer.Character then
					M.SetupMedusaCounter(localPlayer.Character)
				end
			end

			M.StopMedusaCounterUI = function()
				flag25 = false
				M.StopMedusaCounter()
			end

			local charTok10472 = 0
			localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
				charTok10472 += 1
				local myTok = charTok10472
				task.wait(0.3)
				if charTok10472 ~= myTok then return end
				if localPlayer.Character ~= character then return end

				if flag25 then
					M.SetupMedusaCounter(character)
				end
			end)

			do
				local function fn55(arg)
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					if not character then
						return
					end
					local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
						return
					end

					if not arg then
						local ok = false

						if type(M.SafeModeHoldingBrainrot) == "function" then
							local result
							ok, result = pcall(M.SafeModeHoldingBrainrot)
							ok = ok and result == true
						end

						if not ok then
							return
						end

						if humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
							return
						end

						if not (humanoidRootPart.Position.Y >= tbl29.height) then
							return
						end
					end

					local cframe = CFrame.Angles
					local cFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
					humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position.X, -7, humanoidRootPart.Position.Z) * cframe(0, select(2, cFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()), 0)
					humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				end

				fn54 = function()
					fn50_mobileActionSync("TP Down", true)

					pcall(function()
						fn55(true)
					end)

					task.delay(0.22, function()
						fn50_mobileActionSync("TP Down", false)
					end)
				end

				tbl29 = { enabled = false, height = 10, pauseToken = 0 }

				M.StopAutoTP = function()
					tbl29.enabled = false
					tbl29.pauseToken = (tbl29.pauseToken or 0) + 1

					if M.AutoTPThread then
						pcall(task.cancel, M.AutoTPThread)
					end

					M.AutoTPThread = nil
				end

				M.StartAutoTP = function()
					if M.AutoTPThread then
						pcall(task.cancel, M.AutoTPThread)
					end

					M.AutoTPThread = nil
					if not tbl29.enabled then
						return
					end

					M.AutoTPThread = task.spawn(function()
						while tbl29.enabled do
							task.wait(0.4)

							pcall(function()
								fn55(false)
							end)
						end

						M.AutoTPThread = nil
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		M.PauseAutoTPForAction = function()
			if not tbl29.enabled then
				return
			end
			tbl29.pauseToken = (tbl29.pauseToken or 0) + 1
			local pauseToken = tbl29.pauseToken

			if M.AutoTPThread then
				pcall(task.cancel, M.AutoTPThread)
			end

			M.AutoTPThread = nil

			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(0.12)

				while tbl29.enabled and pauseToken == tbl29.pauseToken and M.DropInProgress do
					task.wait(0.1)
				end

				if tbl29.enabled and pauseToken == tbl29.pauseToken and not M.AutoTPThread then
					M.StartAutoTP()
				end
			end)
		end

		M.StopAutoTP()

		do
			local function fn55()
				local Players2 = game:GetService("Players")
				local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
				local localPlayer2 = Players2.LocalPlayer
				local color2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 255)
				M.ESPEnabled = false
				M.ESPShowHeader = false
				M.ESPShowTracer = not M.LowEnd
				local tbl37 = {}
				local obj = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
				local obj2 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
				local folder = nil

				local function fn56(arg)
					local v78 = obj2[arg]

					if v78 then
						pcall(function()
							v78:Destroy()
						end)
					end

					obj2[arg] = nil
				end

				local function fn57(arg, arg2)
					fn56(arg)
					local character = arg and arg.Character
					if not character or not arg2 then
						return
					end

					local ok, adornee = pcall(function()
						character.Archivable = true
						return character:Clone()
					end)

					if not ok or not adornee then
						return
					end

					for _, descendant in ipairs(adornee:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("Script") or descendant:IsA("LocalScript") or descendant:IsA("Humanoid") or descendant:IsA("Highlight") then
							descendant:Destroy()
						elseif descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							descendant.Anchored = true
							descendant.CanCollide = false
							descendant.CanQuery = false
							descendant.CanTouch = false
						end
					end

					if not folder or not folder.Parent then
						folder = Instance.new("Folder")
						folder.Name = "MeridianESPVoidGhosts"
						folder.Parent = Workspace
					end

					adornee.Name = "MeridianVoidGhost_" .. arg.Name
					adornee.Parent = folder

					pcall(function()
						adornee:PivotTo(arg2)
					end)

					local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
					highlight.Name = "MeridianESP_VoidGhost"
					highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 240, 0)
					highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 240, 0)
					highlight.FillTransparency = 0
					highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
					highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
					highlight.Adornee = adornee
					highlight.Parent = adornee
					obj2[arg] = adornee
				end

				M.ESPSetVoidGhost = function(arg, arg2)
					if not arg or not arg2 then
						return
					end
					obj[arg] = arg2
					local v78 = tbl37[arg]

					if v78 and v78.hl then
						v78.hl.Enabled = false
					end

					if not obj2[arg] or not obj2[arg].Parent then
						fn57(arg, arg2)
					end
				end

				M.ESPClearVoidGhost = function(arg)
					obj[arg] = nil
					fn56(arg)
					local v78 = tbl37[arg]

					if v78 and v78.hl and v78.hl.Parent then
						v78.hl.Enabled = true
					end
				end

				local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
				screenGui.Name = "MeridianESP_Visual"
				screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
				screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
				screenGui.DisplayOrder = 9999

				pcall(function()
					screenGui.Parent = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
				end)

				if not screenGui.Parent then
					screenGui.Parent = localPlayer2:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
				end

				for _, child in ipairs(screenGui.Parent:GetChildren()) do
					if child ~= screenGui and child.Name == "MeridianESP_Visual" then
						pcall(function()
							child:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end

				local function createHighlight(arg, adornee)
					if not adornee then
						return
					end
					local meridianESPHl = adornee:FindFirstChild("MeridianESP_HL")

					if meridianESPHl then
						meridianESPHl:Destroy()
					end

					local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
					highlight.Name = "MeridianESP_HL"
					highlight.FillColor = color2
					highlight.OutlineColor = color2
					highlight.FillTransparency = 0.4
					highlight.OutlineTransparency = 1
					highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
					highlight.Adornee = adornee
					highlight.Parent = adornee
					return highlight
				end

				local function fn58(arg)
					local frame2 = Instance.new("Frame", screenGui)
					frame2.Name = "ESP_BOX_" .. arg.Name
					frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
					frame2.Visible = false
					frame2.ZIndex = 9999
					local frame3 = Instance.new("Frame", screenGui)
					frame3.Name = "ESP_TRACER_" .. arg.Name
					frame3.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					frame3.BackgroundColor3 = color2
					frame3.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
					frame3.BorderSizePixel = 0
					frame3.Visible = false
					frame3.ZIndex = 9998
					local frame4 = Instance.new("Frame", frame2)
					frame4.Name = "Header"
					frame4.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
					frame4.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -6)
					frame4.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 26)
					frame4.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					frame4.ZIndex = 10000
					local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame4)
					textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
					textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					textLabel.Text = "Speed: 0"
					textLabel.TextColor3 = color2
					textLabel.FontFace = font
					textLabel.TextSize = 7
					textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
					textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
					textLabel.ZIndex = 10001
					return frame2, textLabel, frame3, frame4, nil
				end

				local function fn59(arg)
					if arg == localPlayer2 then
						return
					end

					if tbl37[arg] then
						return
					end
					local v78, v79, v80, v81, v82 = fn58(arg)
					local tbl38 = { box = v78, boxSt = v82, speedLbl = v79, tracer = v80, header = v81, hl = nil }
					tbl37[arg] = tbl38

					if arg.Character then
						tbl38.hl = createHighlight(arg, arg.Character)
					end

					tbl38.charConn = arg.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
						task.wait(0.4)
						tbl38.hl = createHighlight(arg, character)
					end)
				end

				local function fn60(player)
					local v78 = tbl37[player]
					if not v78 then
						return
					end

					if v78.charConn then
						pcall(function()
							v78.charConn:Disconnect()
						end)
					end

					if v78.box then
						pcall(function()
							v78.box:Destroy()
						end)
					end

					if v78.tracer then
						pcall(function()
							v78.tracer:Destroy()
						end)
					end

					if v78.hl then
						pcall(function()
							v78.hl:Destroy()
						end)
					end

					M.ESPClearVoidGhost(player)
					tbl37[player] = nil
				end

				Players2.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
					if M.ESPEnabled then
						fn59(player)
					end
				end)

				Players2.PlayerRemoving:Connect(fn60)

				task.spawn(function()
					while task.wait(2) do
						if M.ESPEnabled then
							for _, player in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
								if player ~= localPlayer2 then
									if not tbl37[player] then
										pcall(fn59, player)
									else
										local v78 = tbl37[player]
										local character = player.Character

										if character and (not v78.hl or v78.hl.Parent ~= character) then
											pcall(function()
												if v78.hl then
													v78.hl:Destroy()
												end
											end)

											v78.hl = createHighlight(player, character)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)

				M.TickConn("esp", 0.033, function()
					if not M.ESPEnabled then
						for _, v78 in pairs(tbl37) do
							v78.box.Visible = false
							v78.tracer.Visible = false
						end

						return
					end

					local currentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
					if not currentCamera then
						return
					end
					local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
					local character = localPlayer2.Character
					local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local n34 = nil
					local n35 = nil

					if humanoidRootPart then
						local v78 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(humanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, -0.5, 0))
						local v79 = nil
						n35 = nil

						if not (0 < v78.Z) then
							n34 = v79
						else
							local n36 = math.clamp(v78.X, 10, viewportSize.X - 10)
							n35 = math.clamp(v78.Y, 10, viewportSize.Y - 10)
							n34 = n36
						end
					end

					if not n34 then
						n34 = viewportSize.X / 2
						n35 = viewportSize.Y * 0.85
					end

					for k, v78 in pairs(tbl37) do
						local character2 = k.Character
						local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						character2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local v79 = obj[k]

						if not humanoidRootPart2 or not character2 or character2.Health <= 0 then
							v78.box.Visible = false
							v78.tracer.Visible = false
						else
							if v78.hl then
								v78.hl.Enabled = v79 == nil
							end

							local position = v79 and v79.Position or humanoidRootPart2.Position
							local n36 = position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
							local n37 = position + Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)
							local v80 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(n36)
							local v81 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(n37)

							if 0 < v80.Z then
								local n38 = math.abs(v81.Y - v80.Y)
								local n39 = n38 * 0.55
								local n40 = (v80.X + v81.X) / 2
								local n41 = (v80.Y + v81.Y) / 2
								v78.box.Position = UDim2.new(0, n40 - n39 / 2, 0, n41 - n38 / 2)
								v78.box.Size = UDim2.new(0, n39, 0, n38)
								v78.box.Visible = true
								local n42 = n41 + n38 / 2
								local n43 = n40 - n34
								local n44 = n42 - n35
								local v82 = math.sqrt(n43 * n43 + n44 * n44)
								v78.tracer.Position = UDim2.new(0, (n34 + n40) / 2, 0, (n35 + n42) / 2)
								v78.tracer.Size = UDim2.new(0, v82, 0, 1)
								v78.tracer.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(n44, n43))
								v78.tracer.Visible = M.ESPShowTracer == true
								local speedTxt = v79 and "" or string.format("Speed: %.1f", humanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude)
								if v78.speedLbl.Text ~= speedTxt then
									v78.speedLbl.Text = speedTxt
								end
								v78.header.Visible = v79 == nil
							else
								v78.box.Visible = false
								v78.tracer.Visible = false
							end
						end
					end
				end)

				M.ESPSetEnabled = function(meridianESPEnabled)
					M.ESPEnabled = meridianESPEnabled
					if meridianESPEnabled then M.TickOn("esp") else M.TickOff("esp") end

					if meridianESPEnabled then
						for _, player in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
							if player ~= localPlayer2 and not tbl37[player] then
								fn59(player)
							end
						end
					else
						for k in pairs(tbl37) do
							fn60(k)
						end

						for k in pairs(obj) do
							M.ESPClearVoidGhost(k)
						end
					end
				end

				M.ESPApplyAccent = function(arg)
					do
						color2 = arg or M.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 255)

						for _, v78 in pairs(tbl37) do
							if v78.boxSt then
								v78.boxSt.Color = color2
							end

							if v78.tracer then
								v78.tracer.BackgroundColor3 = color2
							end

							if v78.speedLbl then
								v78.speedLbl.TextColor3 = color2
							end

							if v78.hl then
								v78.hl.FillColor = color2
								v78.hl.OutlineColor = color2
							end
						end

						return
					end

				end

				M.ESPSetShowHeader = function(arg)
					M.ESPShowHeader = arg and true or false
				end

				M.ESPSetShowTracer = function(arg)
					M.ESPShowTracer = arg and true or false
				end

				M.ESPSetEnabled(false)
			end

			fn55()
		end
	end

	do
		local function fn55()
			local Players2 = game:GetService("Players")
			local RunService2 = game:GetService("RunService")
			local localPlayer2 = Players2.LocalPlayer
			local tbl37 = { minX = -535.7, maxX = -422, minY = -7.9, maxY = 115, minZ = -72, maxZ = 193.3 }
			local obj = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

			local function fn56(arg)
				return arg.X >= tbl37.minX and arg.X <= tbl37.maxX and arg.Y >= tbl37.minY and arg.Y <= tbl37.maxY and arg.Z >= tbl37.minZ and arg.Z <= tbl37.maxZ
			end

			RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not M.ESPEnabled then
					return
				end
				local now2 = os.clock()

				for _, player in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= localPlayer2 then
						local character = player.Character
						local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						character = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local tbl38 = obj[player] or { history = {}, ghost = nil, walked = 0, last = nil }
						obj[player] = tbl38

						if humanoidRootPart and character and character.Health > 0 then
							local cFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
							local position = cFrame.Position

							if tbl38.ghost then
								if tbl38.last and fn56(position) then
									local n34 = position - tbl38.last
									local magnitude = Vector3.new(n34.X, 0, n34.Z).Magnitude

									if magnitude < 50 then
										tbl38.walked = tbl38.walked + magnitude
									end
								end

								tbl38.last = position

								if tbl38.walked >= 15 then
									tbl38.ghost = nil
									tbl38.walked = 0
									tbl38.history = {}

									if M.ESPClearVoidGhost then
										M.ESPClearVoidGhost(player)
									end
								elseif M.ESPSetVoidGhost then
									M.ESPSetVoidGhost(player, tbl38.ghost)
								end
							else
								table.insert(tbl38.history, { time = now2, cframe = cFrame })

								while #tbl38.history > 0 and now2 - tbl38.history[1].time > 0.07 do
									table.remove(tbl38.history, 1)
								end

								local v78 = tbl38.history[1]

								if v78 then
									local magnitude = (position - v78.cframe.Position).Magnitude
									local n34 = math.abs(position.Y - v78.cframe.Position.Y)

									if (magnitude >= 150 or n34 >= 100) and fn56(v78.cframe.Position) then
										local v79 = 0
										tbl38.ghost = v78.cframe
										tbl38.walked = v79
										tbl38.history = {}

										if M.ESPSetVoidGhost then
											M.ESPSetVoidGhost(player, tbl38.ghost)
										end
									end
								end

								tbl38.last = position
							end
						elseif tbl38.ghost then
							tbl38.ghost = nil

							if M.ESPClearVoidGhost then
								M.ESPClearVoidGhost(player)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			Players2.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
				obj[player] = nil

				if M.ESPClearVoidGhost then
					M.ESPClearVoidGhost(player)
				end
			end)
		end

		fn55()
	end

	-- executors can expose readfile/writefile late: wait up to ~6s so the config is not silently skipped
	for _ = 1, 8 do
		if M.ResolveSaveApi() then
			break
		end
		task.wait(0.75)
	end

	do
		-- retry a failed/partial read a few times before giving up (fn43 returns true on success or "no config yet")
		local loadedOk = fn43()
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			if loadedOk == true then
				break
			end
			task.wait(0.4)
			M.ResolveSaveApi()
			loadedOk = fn43()
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(12) do
			if fn42() then
				if tbl25.auto then
					pcall(fn29)
				end

				continue
			end

			break
		end
	end)

	-- Auto-save watcher: any saved setting that changes (speed editor boxes, radius, delays, modes...) is written within ~0.6s
	task.spawn(function()
		local last
		local lastFull = os.clock()
		while task.wait(0.6) do
			if not fn42() then break end
			if M.SettingsReady == true and tbl25.auto then
				local ok, sig = pcall(function()
					return table.concat({
						tostring(tbl22.NS), tostring(tbl22.CS), tostring(tbl22.LG_N), tostring(tbl22.LG_C), tostring(tbl22.PL),
						tostring(tbl22.family), tostring(tbl22.carry), tostring(tbl23.aimSpd), tostring(tbl24.fovVal),
						tostring(tbl29.height), tostring(M.LookAtEnemyRadius), tostring(tbl19.StealRadius),
						tostring(tbl19.NormalStealPausePercent), tostring(M.StealMode), tostring(tbl30 and tbl30.CFG and tbl30.CFG.STEAL_RANGE),
						tostring(M.TpBatSwingDelay), tostring(tbl27.packets), tostring(tbl27.delay),
						tostring(dropMode), tostring(tpMode), tostring(M.InfJumpMode), tostring(M.BatMode),
						tostring(M.TpBatMode), tostring(M.StealMode), tostring(M.AutoCarryMode),
						tostring(M.SkyMode), tostring(M.AnimPack), tostring(M.FontMode),
						tostring(M.ThemeIdx), tostring(M.UIScale), tostring(M.MobileBtnScale),
						tostring(M.UiLocked), tostring(M.CircleButtons), tostring(M.BatCounterMode),
						tostring(tbl32.enabled), tostring(tbl29.enabled), tostring(tbl20.AutoSteal), tostring(tbl23.aimbot),
						tostring(tbl23.desync), tostring(tbl23.tpMirror), tostring(tbl23.swing), tostring(tbl24.fov),
						tostring(tbl24.stretch), tostring(tbl24.antilag), tostring(flag14), tostring(flag25),
						tostring(M.LookAtEnemyEnabled), tostring(M.ESPEnabled),
						tostring(M.AutoPathMatchSpeed), tostring(M.AutoCarry),
						tostring(M.SafeModeEnabled), tostring(M.PotatoGraphics), tostring(M.MenuOpen),
						tostring(M.IntroEnabled), tostring(M.IntroStyle), tostring(M.IntroSongIndex), tostring(M.IntroSongLocked),
					}, "|")
				end)
				if ok then
					if last == nil then
						last = sig
					elseif sig ~= last then
						last = sig
						pcall(fn29)
					end
				end
				-- the signature above does not cover every saved setting: re-check the full payload every ~3s (fn29 skips the write when nothing changed)
				if os.clock() - lastFull > 3 then
					lastFull = os.clock()
					pcall(fn29)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

				if fn42() then
					if not M.flag2 then
						return
					end
					pcall(fn29, true)
				end
			end)
	end)

				local function fn57()
					flag31 = true
					local rotation = imageLabel.Rotation

					while flag31 and not flag29 do
						rotation += 50
						TweenService:Create(imageLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Rotation = rotation }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(uiScale, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = 1.035 }):Play()
						task.wait(0.14)
						TweenService:Create(uiScale, TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
						task.wait(0.78)
					end
				end

				local function fn58()
					flag32 = true
					local n34 = 0
					local v84 = 0
					local v85 = nil
					local n35 = nil
					local n36 = nil
					local v86 = nil
					local n37 = nil

					connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
						if not flag32 or flag29 then
							return
						end
						v84 += deltaTime
						local n38, n39, n40

						if v84 <= 2.1 then
							n34 = (n34 + deltaTime * 1920 * -1) % 360
							n38 = n34
							n39 = 1
							local parent = v83 and v83.Parent
							n40 = -1

							if parent then
								v83.PlaybackSpeed = 1
							end
						elseif v84 <= 3.3 then
							local n41 = (v84 - 2.1) / 1.2

							if not v85 then
								v85 = n34
								n35 = 82

								while (n35 - v85) * -1 < 1180 do
									n35 += -360
								end

								n36 = n35 - 230
							end

							local n42 = n41 * n41
							local n43 = n42 * n41
							n38 = (2 * n43 - 3 * n42 + 1) * v85 + (n43 - 2 * n42 + n41) * -2304 + (-2 * n43 + 3 * n42) * n35 + (n43 - n42) * -384
							n34 = n38
							local n44 = ((6 * n42 - 6 * n41) * v85 + (3 * n42 - 4 * n41 + 1) * -2304 + (-6 * n42 + 6 * n41) * n35 + (3 * n42 - 2 * n41) * -384) / 1.2
							n39 = math.clamp(math.abs(n44) / 1920, 0.16, 1)
							n40 = n44 >= 0 and 1 or -1

							if v83 and v83.Parent then
								local v87 = 0
								v83.PlaybackSpeed = 0.35 + 0.45 * math.clamp(math.abs(n44) / 1920, v87, 1)
							end
						elseif v84 <= 5.8 then
							n36 = n36 or -34

							if not v86 then
								v86 = n35
								n37 = -320
							end

							n37 += math.sin(math.rad((n36 - v86 + 180) % 360 - 160)) * 1100 * deltaTime
							n37 *= math.exp(-2.8 * deltaTime)
							v86 += n37 * deltaTime
							n38 = v86
							n40 = n37 >= 0 and 1 or -1
							n39 = math.clamp(math.abs(n37) / 900, 0, 0.3)

							if v83 and v83.Parent then
								v83.PlaybackSpeed = 0.5 + 0.22 * math.clamp(math.abs(n37) / 320, 0, 1)
							end
						else
							n38 = n36 or -34
							n39 = 0
							local parent = v83 and v83.Parent
							n40 = 1

							if parent then
								v83.PlaybackSpeed = 0.5
							end
						end

						for i, v87 in ipairs(tbl39) do
							local flag34 = i == #tbl39
							v87.Rotation = n38 + tbl41[i] * n39 * n40

							if v84 > 2.1 and not flag34 then
								local v88 = tbl42[i]
								tbl40[i].ImageTransparency = v88 + (1 - v88) * (1 - n39)
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				local function fn59()
					flag33 = true

					task.spawn(function()
						for _, v84 in ipairs(tbl50) do
							fn56(v84, UDim2.new(0.5, -1, 0.5, -3), -1)
						end

						task.wait(0.18)
						if not flag33 or flag29 then
							return
						end
						local flag34 = false

						while flag33 and not flag29 do
							flag34 = not flag34
							local udim2 = flag34 and UDim2.new(0.5, -1, 0.5, -2) or UDim2.new(0.5, 1, 0.5, 1)
							local n34 = flag34 and -1 or 1

							for _, v84 in ipairs(tbl50) do
								fn56(v84, udim2, n34, TweenInfo.new(1.05, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut))
							end

							local udim22 = flag34 and UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 0.5, -3) or UDim2.new(0.5, 2, 0.5, 2)
							local n35 = flag34 and -2 or 2

							for _, v84 in ipairs(tbl51) do
								fn56(v84, udim22, n35, TweenInfo.new(1.05, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut))
							end

							task.wait(1.08)
						end
					end)
				end

				tbl54.play = function()
					tbl54.active = true

					task.spawn(function()
						local now2 = os.clock()
						local layers = tbl54.layers

						while tbl54.active and not flag29 do
							local n34 = (os.clock() - now2) * 2.5
							local v84 = math.sin(n34 * 0.82)
							local v85 = math.sin(n34 * 0.68 + 0.45)
							tbl54.stage.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.sin(n34 * 0.57 + 0.8) * 4, 0.42, v84 * 6)
							tbl54.stage.Rotation = -38 + v85 * 1.15
							tbl54.scale.Scale = 1 + math.sin(n34 * 0.73 + 1.2) * 0.014
							local v86 = 0.62
							layers.torso.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.76) * v86
							layers.head.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.92 + 0.65) * 1.55
							layers.hair.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 1.08 + 1.1) * 0.85
							layers.leftHand.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.88 + 0.25) * 3.25
							layers.rightHand.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.96 + 2.35) * 3.15
							layers.straightLeg.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.72 + 0.2) * 4
							layers.straightBoot.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.91 + 0.75) * 1.25
							layers.bentLeg.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.79 + 3.05) * 4.15
							layers.bentBoot.image.Rotation = math.sin(n34 * 0.98 + 3.55) * 1.35

							for _, bird in ipairs(tbl54.birds) do
								local n35 = n34 * bird.speed + bird.phase
								local n36 = math.sin(n34 * 3.8 + bird.phase) * 7.5
								local n37 = (math.sin(n35) + 1) * 0.5
								local radiusX = bird.radiusX
								local radiusY = bird.radiusY
								bird.frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5 + math.cos(n35) * radiusX, 0.42 + math.sin(n35 * 1.08) * radiusY + math.sin(n34 * 1.3 + bird.phase) * 0.012)
								bird.frame.Rotation = math.sin(n35 * 1.7) * 5
								bird.scale.Scale = bird.baseScale * (0.91 + n37 * 0.13)
								bird.leftWing.Rotation = 20 + n36
								bird.rightWing.Rotation = -24 - n36
								bird.leftFeather.Rotation = 32 + n36 * 0.72
								bird.rightFeather.Rotation = -32 - n36 * 0.72
							end

							tbl54.sunScale.Scale = 1 + math.sin(n34 * 0.18 + 0.6) * 0.018
							RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
						end
					end)
				end

							TweenService:Create(v87.image, tweenInfo, { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()

							if v87.gradientTween then
								v87.gradientTween:Cancel()
							end

							if v87.pulseTween then
								v87.pulseTween:Cancel()
							end

							TweenService:Create(v87.image, tweenInfo, { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()
						end
					end

					TweenService:Create(imageLabel2, tweenInfo, { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()

					for _, piece in ipairs(tbl54.pieces) do
						TweenService:Create(piece, tweenInfo, { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()

					for _, bird in ipairs(tbl54.birds) do
						TweenService:Create(bird.leftWing, tweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(bird.rightWing, tweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(bird.leftFeather, tweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(bird.rightFeather, tweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
					end

					for _, sunLayer in ipairs(tbl54.sunLayers) do
						TweenService:Create(sunLayer.frame, tweenInfo, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()

					TweenService:Create(textLabel, tweenInfo, { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(textLabel2, tweenInfo, { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(blurEffect, tweenInfo, { Size = 0 }):Play()

					task.delay(0.42, function()
						if frame2 and frame2.Parent then
							frame2:Destroy()
						end

						if blurEffect and blurEffect.Parent then
							blurEffect:Destroy()
						end

						M.IntroPlaying = false
						local meridianRevealMainAfterIntro = M.RevealMainAfterIntro

						if meridianRevealMainAfterIntro then
							meridianRevealMainAfterIntro()
						end
					end)

					local tbl39 = obj[arg]

					if not tbl39 then
						tbl39 = {}
						obj[arg] = tbl39
					end

					if tbl39[arg2] then
						tbl39[arg2]:Cancel()
					end

					local tween = TweenService:Create(arg, TweenInfo.new(arg3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), arg4)
					tbl39[arg2] = tween
					tween:Play()
					return tween
				end

						local absoluteSize = frame2.AbsoluteSize

						if absolutePosition.X < -absoluteSize.X + 48 or absolutePosition.Y < -absoluteSize.Y + 48 or absolutePosition.X > viewportSize.X - 48 or absolutePosition.Y > viewportSize.Y - 48 then
							frame2.Position = udim2

			-- default: just under the Roblox icon (topbar bottom + 6px, aligned with the icon), every execute
			local function floatDefaultPosition()
				local topInset = 58
				pcall(function() topInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset().Y end)
				return UDim2.fromOffset(16, topInset + 6)
			end
			M.FloatBtnPos = nil

			local function fn60()
				local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if not currentCamera then
					return
				end
				local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
				local x = frame3.AbsolutePosition.X
				local y = frame3.AbsolutePosition.Y

				if x < 0 or y < 0 or x > viewportSize.X - frame3.Size.X.Offset or y > viewportSize.Y - frame3.Size.Y.Offset then
					frame3.Position = floatDefaultPosition()
					M.FloatBtnPos = nil
				end
