	local function refreshUiLock()
		local on = M.UiLocked == true
		lockButton.Text = on and "🔒" or "🔓"
		lockButton.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or tbl37.row
		lockButton.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.05 or 0.35
		lockButton.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20) or tbl37.text
	end
	M.RefreshUiLock = refreshUiLock
	lockButton:SetAttribute("_MeridianNoInvert", true)

	-- single source of truth: M.UiLocked drives this icon, the mobile buttons, the progress bar and the menu
	M.ApplyUiLock = function(value, noSave)
		M.UiLocked = value == true
		M.MobileBtnLocked = M.UiLocked

		if M.SetMobileBtnLocked then
			pcall(M.SetMobileBtnLocked, M.UiLocked)
		end

		pcall(refreshUiLock)

		if not noSave and tbl25.auto then
			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(fn29, true)
			end)
		end
	end

	if type(M.UiLocked) ~= "boolean" then
		M.UiLocked = false
	end

	M.ApplyUiLock(M.UiLocked, true)

	task.defer(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		pcall(M.ApplyUiLock, M.UiLocked == true, true) -- config loads after the menu is built
	end)

	lockButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		M.ApplyUiLock(not (M.UiLocked == true))
	end)

			local createTextLabel

			createTextLabel = function(parent, arg)
				-- Neon bracket label: Michroma, dim [ ] brackets, breathing glow, gradient sliding inside the letters
				local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				textLabel.Name = "Section_" .. arg
				textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel.RichText = true
				textLabel.Text = '<font color="rgb(109,109,109)">[</font> ' .. string.upper(arg) .. ' <font color="rgb(109,109,109)">]</font>'
				textLabel.TextColor3 = tbl37.text
				textLabel.TextSize = 14
				textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Michroma
				textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 32)
				textLabel.ZIndex = 4
				textLabel.Parent = parent

				local neonGlow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
				neonGlow.Name = "NeonGlow"
				neonGlow.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				neonGlow.Thickness = 1.5
				neonGlow.Transparency = 0.84
				neonGlow.Parent = textLabel

				local neonFill = Instance.new("UIGradient")
				neonFill.Name = "NeonFill"
				neonFill.Color = ColorSequence.new({
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(135, 135, 135)),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
				})
				neonFill.Parent = textLabel

				if M.NeonLabelsDisabled then
					return textLabel
				end
				-- one shared loop animates every section label
				M.NeonLabels = M.NeonLabels or {}
				table.insert(M.NeonLabels, { g = neonFill, s = neonGlow })

				if not M.NeonConn then
					M.NeonConn = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						local list = M.NeonLabels
						local t = os.clock()
						local off = math.sin(t * 0.8) * 0.6
						local tr = 0.84 + math.sin(t * 2) * 0.06

						for i = #list, 1, -1 do
							local e = list[i]

							if e.g.Parent then
								e.g.Offset = Vector2.new(off, 0)
								e.s.Transparency = tr
							else
								table.remove(list, i)
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				return textLabel
			end

				local function fn70(arg)
					local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
					if not currentCamera then
						return
					end
					local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
					if viewportSize.X <= 0 or viewportSize.Y <= 0 then
						return
					end
					M.MobileBtnPos = M.MobileBtnPos or {}
					M.MobileBtnPositions = M.MobileBtnPositions or {}
					M.MobileBtnPositions[M.MobilePositionProfile or "desktop"] = M.MobileBtnPos

					M.MobileBtnPos[arg.Name] = {
						xs = arg.Position.X.Scale,
						xo = arg.Position.X.Offset,
						ys = arg.Position.Y.Scale,
						yo = arg.Position.Y.Offset,
						vw = viewportSize.X,
						vh = viewportSize.Y,
						nx = arg.AbsolutePosition.X / viewportSize.X,
						ny = arg.AbsolutePosition.Y / viewportSize.Y,
					}
				end

				local function fn71(arg, arg2)
					if type(arg2) ~= "table" then
						return false
					end
					local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
					currentCamera = currentCamera and currentCamera.ViewportSize
					local flag29 = currentCamera and type(arg2.vw) == "number" and type(arg2.vh) == "number" and math.abs(currentCamera.X - arg2.vw) < 1
					local flag30

					if flag29 then
						local v92 = 1
						flag30 = math.abs(currentCamera.Y - arg2.vh) < v92
					else
						flag30 = flag29
					end

					if flag30 and type(arg2.xs) == "number" and type(arg2.ys) == "number" then
						arg.Position = UDim2.new(arg2.xs, arg2.xo or 0, arg2.ys, arg2.yo or 0)
						return true
					end

					if type(arg2.nx) == "number" and type(arg2.ny) == "number" then
						arg.Position = UDim2.new(arg2.nx, 0, arg2.ny, 0)
						return true
					end

					if type(arg2.xs) == "number" and type(arg2.ys) == "number" then
						arg.Position = UDim2.new(arg2.xs, arg2.xo or 0, arg2.ys, arg2.yo or 0)
						return true
					end
					return false
				end

					-- simple press: shrinks slightly while held, springs back on release
					do

						textButton2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
							if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
								return
							end

							pressBase = pressBase or uiScale3.Scale
							TweenService:Create(uiScale3, TweenInfo.new(0.06, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = pressBase * 0.92 }):Play()

							input.Changed:Connect(function()
								if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End and pressBase then
									local restore = pressBase
									pressBase = nil
									TweenService:Create(uiScale3, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = restore }):Play()
								end
							end)
						end)

							return

						flag32 = false

						if meridianMobileBtnLocked or not flag31 then

							local n34 = input.Position - position2

							if not flag32 then
								local flag34 = math.abs(n34.X) < 6

									return

							end

							textButton2.Position = UDim2.new(position3.X.Scale, position3.X.Offset + n34.X, position3.Y.Scale, position3.Y.Offset + n34.Y)
							fn70(textButton2)
							fn74()
						end
					end)

					if M.MobileBtnPos and M.MobileBtnPos[name] then
						fn71(textButton2, M.MobileBtnPos[name])
					end

						if flag31 then
							fn70(textButton2)
							fn74()
						end

				local function meridianApplyMobileButtonShape()
					local flag31 = M.CircleButtons == true
					local v92 = ipairs
					local meridianBtnImages = M.BtnImages or {}

					for _, meridianBtnImage in v92(meridianBtnImages) do
						if meridianBtnImage.button then
							meridianBtnImage.button.ClipsDescendants = true
							meridianBtnImage.button.Size = UDim2.new(0, meridianBtnImage.isWide and 104 or 52, 0, 52)
						end

						local n34 = flag31 and 26 or 12

						if meridianBtnImage.buttonCorner then
							meridianBtnImage.buttonCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, n34)
						end

						if meridianBtnImage.mask then
							meridianBtnImage.mask.ClipsDescendants = true
						end

						if meridianBtnImage.maskCorner then
							meridianBtnImage.maskCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, n34)
						end

						if meridianBtnImage.bg then
							meridianBtnImage.bg.ClipsDescendants = true
						end

						if meridianBtnImage.glow then
							meridianBtnImage.glow.ClipsDescendants = true
						end

						if meridianBtnImage.bgCorner then
							meridianBtnImage.bgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, n34)
						end

						if meridianBtnImage.glowCorner then
							meridianBtnImage.glowCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, n34)
						end

						if meridianBtnImage.refresh then
							meridianBtnImage.refresh()
						end
					end

					local meridianRefreshButtonImages = M.RefreshButtonImages

					if type(meridianRefreshButtonImages) == "function" then
						pcall(meridianRefreshButtonImages)
					end
				end

				createTextButton2 = function(parent, arg)
					local textButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")

				local function fn74()
					M.LookAtEnemyEnabled = M.LookAtEnemyEnabled == true
					M.LookAtEnemyRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(M.LookAtEnemyRadius) or 20, 1, 200)

					if M.LookAtEnemy and M.LookAtEnemy.Stop then
						pcall(M.LookAtEnemy.Stop)
					end

					local meridianLookAtEnemy = { conn = nil, diedConn = nil, anchor = nil, align = nil, attachment = nil }
					M.LookAtEnemy = meridianLookAtEnemy

					local function stop()
						if meridianLookAtEnemy.conn then
							meridianLookAtEnemy.conn:Disconnect()
							meridianLookAtEnemy.conn = nil
						end

						if meridianLookAtEnemy.diedConn then
							meridianLookAtEnemy.diedConn:Disconnect()
							meridianLookAtEnemy.diedConn = nil
						end

						if meridianLookAtEnemy.align then
							meridianLookAtEnemy.align:Destroy()
							meridianLookAtEnemy.align = nil
						end

						if meridianLookAtEnemy.attachment then
							meridianLookAtEnemy.attachment:Destroy()
							meridianLookAtEnemy.attachment = nil
						end

						if meridianLookAtEnemy.anchor then
							meridianLookAtEnemy.anchor:Destroy()
							meridianLookAtEnemy.anchor = nil
						end

						local character = localPlayer.Character
						local v92 = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if v92 then
							v92.AutoRotate = true
						end
					end

					local function fn75()
						local character = localPlayer.Character
						character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not character then
							return nil
						end
						local huge = math.huge
						local v92 = nil

						for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							if player ~= localPlayer then
								local character2 = player.Character
								local humanoidRootPart = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								character2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								if humanoidRootPart and character2 and character2.Health > 0 then
									local magnitude = (character.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

									if magnitude <= M.LookAtEnemyRadius and magnitude < huge then
										huge = magnitude
										v92 = humanoidRootPart
									end
								end
							end
						end

						return v92
					end

					local function start()
						stop()
						if not M.LookAtEnemyEnabled then
							return
						end
						local character = localPlayer.Character
						local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						character = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if not humanoidRootPart or not character or character.Health <= 0 then
							return
						end
						local part = Instance.new("Part")
						part.Name = "MeridianLookAtEnemyAnchor"
						part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
						part.Transparency = 1
						part.CanCollide = false
						part.Anchored = true
						part.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
						part.Parent = workspace:FindFirstChild("Terrain") or workspace
						local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
						attachment.Name = "MeridianLookAtEnemyAttachment"
						attachment.Parent = humanoidRootPart
						local attachment2 = Instance.new("Attachment")
						attachment2.Parent = part
						local alignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
						alignOrientation.Name = "MeridianLookAtEnemyOrientation"
						alignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
						alignOrientation.Attachment0 = attachment
						alignOrientation.Attachment1 = attachment2
						alignOrientation.MaxTorque = 999999
						alignOrientation.MaxAngularVelocity = 999999
						alignOrientation.Responsiveness = 200
						alignOrientation.Enabled = false
						alignOrientation.Parent = part
						local v92 = meridianLookAtEnemy
						local v93 = meridianLookAtEnemy
						meridianLookAtEnemy.anchor = part
						v92.attachment = attachment
						v93.align = alignOrientation
						meridianLookAtEnemy.diedConn = character.Died:Connect(stop)

						meridianLookAtEnemy.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								local character2 = localPlayer.Character
								local v94 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
								character2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								if not M.LookAtEnemyEnabled or not v94 or not character2 or character2.Health <= 0 then
									stop()
									return
								end
								local v95 = fn75()

								if v95 and meridianLookAtEnemy.anchor and meridianLookAtEnemy.align then
									character2.AutoRotate = false
									meridianLookAtEnemy.align.Enabled = true
									local vector = Vector3.new(v95.Position.X, v94.Position.Y, v95.Position.Z)
									meridianLookAtEnemy.anchor.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(v94.Position, vector)
								else
									character2.AutoRotate = true
									meridianLookAtEnemy.align.Enabled = false
								end
						end)
					end

					meridianLookAtEnemy.Start = start
					meridianLookAtEnemy.Stop = stop

					meridianLookAtEnemy.SetEnabled = function(arg)
						M.LookAtEnemyEnabled = arg == true

						if M.LookAtEnemyEnabled then
							start()
						else
							stop()
						end
					end

					local charTok14802 = 0
					localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
						charTok14802 += 1
						local myTok = charTok14802
						if M.LookAtEnemyEnabled then
							task.delay(0.15, function()
								if charTok14802 ~= myTok then return end
								if localPlayer.Character ~= character then return end
								start()
							end)
						end
					end)
				end

				fn74()
				Movement = createScrollingFrame("Movement")
				createTextLabel(Movement, "Speed Configuration")

				-- Speed editor: NORM / CARRY markers sit exactly on top of the two boxes of each row.
				-- Header tags and boxes share the same width + x offsets (one place), so they cannot drift apart.
				do
					local BOX_W, X_NORM, X_CARRY = 58, -136, -68

					local header = Instance.new("Frame")
					header.Name = "SpeedColumnHeader"
					header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					header.BorderSizePixel = 0
					header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 14)
					header.ZIndex = 10
					header.Parent = Movement
					for _, col in ipairs({ { "NORM", X_NORM }, { "CARRY", X_CARRY } }) do
						local tag = Instance.new("TextLabel")
						tag.Name = "Column" .. col[1]
						tag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						tag.Text = col[1]
						tag.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
						tag.TextSize = 10
						tag.TextColor3 = tbl37.text
						tag.TextTransparency = 0.25
						tag.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
						tag.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom
						tag.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
						tag.Size = UDim2.new(0, BOX_W, 0, 12)
						-- bottom edge dips 4px below the header so the marker hugs the boxes under it
						tag.Position = UDim2.new(1, col[2], 1, 4)
						tag.ZIndex = 12
						tag.Parent = header
					end

					-- One row = label + (norm) box + (carry) box. The 2nd box is a clone of the 1st named "CarryBox".
					local function speedRow(rowName, normVal, carryVal)
						local row = fn63(Movement, rowName, { value = tostring(normVal) })
						local normBox = row:FindFirstChild("ValueBox")
						local rowLabel = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
						if rowLabel then rowLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -148, 1, 0) end
						if normBox then
							normBox.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
							normBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, BOX_W, 0, 24)
							normBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
							normBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, X_NORM, 0.5, 0)
							local carryBox = normBox:Clone()
							carryBox.Name = "CarryBox"
							carryBox.Text = tostring(carryVal)
							carryBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, X_CARRY, 0.5, 0)
							carryBox.Parent = row
						end
						return row
					end

					speedRow("Normal Speed", tbl22.NS, tbl22.CS)
					-- Lagger: NORM edits LG_C, CARRY edits LG_N (that is how the speed engine reads them)
					speedRow("Lagger Speed", tbl22.LG_C, tbl22.LG_N)
				end

				local function fn75()
					M.NeonWhiteToolsEnabled = M.NeonWhiteToolsEnabled == true

					if M.NeonWhiteTools and M.NeonWhiteTools.SetEnabled then
						pcall(M.NeonWhiteTools.SetEnabled, false)
					end

					local meridianNeonWhiteTools = {
						enabled = false,
						tracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
						watchers = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
					}

					M.NeonWhiteTools = meridianNeonWhiteTools

					local function fn76(arg)
						if not arg or not arg:IsA("Tool") then
							return false
						end
						local str10 = arg.Name:lower()
						return str10:find("bat") ~= nil or str10:find("slap") ~= nil or str10:find("medusa") ~= nil
					end

					local function fn77(arg)
						local tbl44 = { node = arg, values = {} }
						local tbl45

						if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
							tbl45 = { "Color", "Material" }
						elseif arg:IsA("MeshPart") then
							tbl45 = { "Color", "Material", "TextureID" }
						elseif arg:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
							tbl45 = { "VertexColor", "TextureId" }
						elseif arg:IsA("Decal") or arg:IsA("Texture") then
							tbl45 = { "Transparency" }
						elseif arg:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or arg:IsA("Beam") or arg:IsA("Trail") then
							tbl45 = { "Color" }
						elseif arg:IsA("PointLight") or arg:IsA("SpotLight") or arg:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
							tbl45 = { "Color" }
						elseif arg:IsA("Highlight") then
							tbl45 = { "FillColor", "OutlineColor" }
						else
							if not arg:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
								return nil
							end
							tbl45 = { "Parent" }
						end

						for _, v92 in ipairs(tbl45) do
							pcall(function()
								tbl44.values[v92] = arg[v92]
							end)
						end

						return tbl44
					end

					local function fn78(arg)
						local node = arg.node

						if node:IsA("MeshPart") then
							node.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
							node.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
							node.TextureID = ""
						elseif node:IsA("BasePart") then
							node.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
							node.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
						elseif node:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
							node.VertexColor = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
							node.TextureId = ""
						elseif node:IsA("Decal") or node:IsA("Texture") then
							node.Transparency = 1
						elseif node:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or node:IsA("Beam") or node:IsA("Trail") then
							node.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
						elseif node:IsA("PointLight") or node:IsA("SpotLight") or node:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
							node.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
						elseif node:IsA("Highlight") then
							node.FillColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
							node.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
						elseif node:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
							node.Parent = nil
						end
					end

					local function fn79(arg, arg2)
						local v92 = fn77(arg2)
						if not v92 then
							return
						end
						table.insert(arg.nodes, v92)
						pcall(fn78, v92)
					end

					local function fn80(arg, arg2)
						local v92 = meridianNeonWhiteTools.tracked[arg]
						if not v92 then
							return
						end

						if v92.connection then
							v92.connection:Disconnect()
						end

						if arg2 then
							for _, node in ipairs(v92.nodes) do
								if node.node.Parent then
									for k, value in pairs(node.values) do
										pcall(function()
											node.node[k] = value
										end)
									end
								end
							end
						end

						meridianNeonWhiteTools.tracked[arg] = nil
					end

					local function fn81(child)
						if not meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled or meridianNeonWhiteTools.tracked[child] or not fn76(child) then
							return
						end
						local tbl44 = { nodes = {} }
						meridianNeonWhiteTools.tracked[child] = tbl44

						for _, descendant in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
							fn79(tbl44, descendant)
						end

						tbl44.connection = child.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
							if meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled then
								fn79(tbl44, descendant)
							end
						end)
					end

					local function fn82(arg)
						if not arg or meridianNeonWhiteTools.watchers[arg] then
							return
						end
						meridianNeonWhiteTools.watchers[arg] = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(fn81)

						for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
							fn81(child)
						end
					end

					meridianNeonWhiteTools.SetEnabled = function(arg)
						meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled = arg == true
						M.NeonWhiteToolsEnabled = meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled

						if meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled then
							fn82(localPlayer.Character)
							fn82(localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
						else
							for k in pairs(meridianNeonWhiteTools.tracked) do
								fn80(k, true)
							end

							for k, watcher in pairs(meridianNeonWhiteTools.watchers) do
								watcher:Disconnect()
								meridianNeonWhiteTools.watchers[k] = nil
							end
						end
					end

					localPlayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
						if meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled and child:IsA("Backpack") then
							fn82(child)
						end
					end)

					localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
						if meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled then
							for k in pairs(meridianNeonWhiteTools.tracked) do
								fn80(k, false)
							end

							for k, watcher in pairs(meridianNeonWhiteTools.watchers) do
								watcher:Disconnect()
								meridianNeonWhiteTools.watchers[k] = nil
							end

							fn82(character)

							task.defer(function()
								if meridianNeonWhiteTools.enabled then
									fn82(localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
								end
							end)
						end
					end)
				end

				fn75()
				Utility = createScrollingFrame("Utility")
				createTextLabel(Utility, "Players")
				local esp = fn63(Utility, "ESP", { arrow = true, toggle = true })
				local showTracer = fn63(Utility, "Show Tracer", { toggle = true })
				fn63(Utility, "Ragdoll Countdown", { toggle = true })
				local arrowButton = esp:FindFirstChild("ArrowButton")

				if arrowButton then
					fn67(esp, showTracer, arrowButton)
				end

				createTextLabel(Utility, "Visual")
				fn68(Utility, "Font", "DEFAULT", "PIXEL")
				fn63(Utility, "Neon White Tools", { toggle = true })
				fn69(Utility)
			end

						currentCamera = currentCamera and currentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
						local meridianSafeModeToastMaxWidth = math.clamp(math.floor(currentCamera.X * 0.92) - 46, 140, 680)
						M.SafeModeToastMaxWidth = meridianSafeModeToastMaxWidth
						M.SafeModeToastWidth = meridianSafeModeToastMaxWidth + 46
						M.SafeModeToastHeight = meridianSafeModeEnabled and 26 or 30
						frame6.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(meridianSafeModeToastMaxWidth + 46, math.floor(currentCamera.Y * 0.34))
					end

					M.SafeModeResizeNotifier()
					local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

					if currentCamera then
						M.SafeModeViewportConn = currentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(M.SafeModeResizeNotifier)
					end
				end

				M.SafeModeNotify = function(arg)
					pcall(M.SafeModeEnsureNotifier)
					local meridianSafeModeNotifyHolder = M.SafeModeNotifyHolder
					if not meridianSafeModeNotifyHolder or not meridianSafeModeNotifyHolder.Parent then
						return
					end
					M.SafeModeToastSerial = (M.SafeModeToastSerial or 0) + 1
					local meridianSafeModeToastSerial = M.SafeModeToastSerial
					local message = tostring(arg or "SAFE MODE")
					local messageUpper = message:upper()
					local dotColor = Color3.fromRGB(61, 220, 132)

					if messageUpper:find("QUEUED") then
						dotColor = Color3.fromRGB(90, 169, 255)
					elseif messageUpper:find("WARNING") or messageUpper:find("KICK") or messageUpper:find("DISCONNECT") or messageUpper:find("BLOCKED") or messageUpper:find("LOCKED") or messageUpper:find("CANCELED") or messageUpper:find("DISABLED") then
						dotColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 176, 32)
					end

					local textSize = meridianSafeModeEnabled and 12 or 14
					local textFont = Enum.Font.GothamBold
					local maxTextWidth = M.SafeModeToastMaxWidth or 320
					local okBounds, bounds = pcall(function()
						return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(message, textSize, textFont, Vector2.new(maxTextWidth, 1000))
					end)

					if not okBounds or not bounds then
						bounds = Vector2.new(math.min(#message * textSize * 0.62, maxTextWidth), textSize + 2)
					end

					local labelW = math.min(math.ceil(bounds.X) + 16, maxTextWidth)
					local labelH = math.ceil(bounds.Y)
					local pillH = math.max(meridianSafeModeEnabled and 26 or 30, labelH + 16)
					local pillW = labelW + 46

					-- keep clear of the DONT ENTER banner (same top-center spot)
					local topOffset = 14
					local kickGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("MeridianKickWarning")
					local kickBanner = kickGui and kickGui:FindFirstChild("KickTimerBanner")

					if kickBanner and kickBanner.Visible then
						topOffset = meridianSafeModeEnabled and 84 or 92
					end
					meridianSafeModeNotifyHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, topOffset)

					local tbl50 = {}

					for _, child in ipairs(meridianSafeModeNotifyHolder:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Frame") and child.Name:sub(1, 6) == "Toast_" then
							tbl50[#tbl50 + 1] = child
						end
					end

					table.sort(tbl50, function(arg2, arg3)
						return arg2.LayoutOrder < arg3.LayoutOrder
					end)

					while #tbl50 >= 4 do
						tbl50[1]:Destroy()
						table.remove(tbl50, 1)
					end

					local frame6 = Instance.new("Frame")
					frame6.Name = "Toast_" .. meridianSafeModeToastSerial
					frame6.LayoutOrder = meridianSafeModeToastSerial
					frame6.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					frame6.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, pillH)
					frame6.Parent = meridianSafeModeNotifyHolder
					local frame7 = Instance.new("Frame")
					frame7.Name = "Card"
					frame7.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
					frame7.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14)
					frame7.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					frame7.BorderSizePixel = 0
					frame7.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -24)
					frame7.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pillW, pillH)
					frame7.ZIndex = 2
					frame7.Parent = frame6
					Instance.new("UICorner", frame7).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
					local cardStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
					cardStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					cardStroke.Thickness = 1
					cardStroke.Transparency = 1
					cardStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
					cardStroke.Parent = frame7
					local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
					dot.Name = "Dot"
					dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
					dot.BackgroundColor3 = dotColor
					dot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
					dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0.5, 0)
					dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
					dot.ZIndex = 3
					dot.Parent = frame7
					Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
					local dotGlow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
					dotGlow.Color = dotColor
					dotGlow.Thickness = 3
					dotGlow.Transparency = 1
					dotGlow.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
					dotGlow.Parent = dot
					local textLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					textLabel2.Name = "Message"
					textLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					textLabel2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(31, 0)
					textLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(0, labelW, 1, 0)
					textLabel2.Font = textFont
					textLabel2.Text = message
					textLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					textLabel2.TextSize = textSize
					textLabel2.TextTransparency = 1
					textLabel2.TextWrapped = bounds.X + 16 >= maxTextWidth
					textLabel2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
					textLabel2.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
					textLabel2.ZIndex = 3
					textLabel2.Parent = frame7
					TweenService:Create(frame7, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0) }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(frame7, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(cardStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { Transparency = 0.86 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(dotGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { Transparency = 0.6 }):Play()
					TweenService:Create(textLabel2, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()

					task.delay(2.8, function()
						if not frame6.Parent then
							return
						end
						TweenService:Create(frame7, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -24), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(cardStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(dotGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
						TweenService:Create(textLabel2, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()

						task.delay(0.35, function()
							if frame6.Parent then
								frame6:Destroy()
							end
						end)
					end)
				end

				M.SafeModeCountdownActive = function()
					local meridianSafeModeState = M.SafeModeState

					local function fn79()
						if meridianSafeModeState then
							meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = nil

							if meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase == "round" or meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen then
								meridianSafeModeState.timerInactiveSince = meridianSafeModeState.timerInactiveSince or os.clock()
								local timerInactiveSince = meridianSafeModeState.timerInactiveSince

								if os.clock() - timerInactiveSince >= 0.75 then
									meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "inactive"
									meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = false
									meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen = false
									meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = true
								end
							elseif meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase == "unknown" then
								meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "inactive"
								meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = true
							end
						end

						return false
					end

					local duelsMachineTopFrame = playerGui:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame", true)
					duelsMachineTopFrame = duelsMachineTopFrame and duelsMachineTopFrame:FindFirstChild("Timer", true)
					duelsMachineTopFrame = duelsMachineTopFrame and duelsMachineTopFrame:FindFirstChild("Label", true)
					if not duelsMachineTopFrame or not duelsMachineTopFrame:IsA("TextLabel") then
						return (fn79())
					end
					local parent = duelsMachineTopFrame

					while parent and parent ~= playerGui do
						if parent:IsA("GuiObject") and parent.Visible == false then
							return (fn79())
						end

						if parent:IsA("ScreenGui") and parent.Enabled == false then
							return (fn79())
						end
						parent = parent.Parent
					end

					local str10 = tostring(duelsMachineTopFrame.Text or ""):upper():gsub("<.->", ""):gsub("%s+", "")

					if meridianSafeModeState then
						meridianSafeModeState.timerInactiveSince = nil
					end

					if str10 == "READY" or str10 == "STARTING" or str10 == "GETREADY" then
						if meridianSafeModeState then
							if meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase ~= "starting" then
								meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = false
							end

							meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "starting"
							meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = nil
							meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = false
							meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = true
						end

						return true
					end

					if str10 == "GO" or str10 == "FIGHT" then
						if meridianSafeModeState then
							meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "round"
							meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen = true
							meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = true
							meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = nil
						end

						return false
					end

					local match, v95 = str10:match("^(%d+):(%d+)$")

					if match and v95 then
						local timerLastNumber = tonumber(match) * 60 + tonumber(v95)

						if meridianSafeModeState then
							if timerLastNumber <= 0 and meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen then
								meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "inactive"
								meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = false
								meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen = false
								meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = true
							else
								meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "round"
								meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen = true
								meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = true
								meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = false
							end

							meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = timerLastNumber
						end

						return false
					end

					local timerLastNumber = tonumber(str10)
					if not timerLastNumber then
						return false
					end

					if timerLastNumber > 10 then
						if meridianSafeModeState then
							meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "round"
							meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen = true
							meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = true
							meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = timerLastNumber
							meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = false
						end

						return false
					end

					if timerLastNumber <= 0 then
						if meridianSafeModeState then
							meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = timerLastNumber

							if meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase == "round" or meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen then
								meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "inactive"
								meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = false
								meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen = false
								meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = true
							end
						end

						return false
					end

					local timerRoundSeen

					if meridianSafeModeState then
						timerRoundSeen = meridianSafeModeState.timerRoundSeen or meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed
					else
						timerRoundSeen = meridianSafeModeState
					end

					if timerRoundSeen and meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase ~= "starting" then
						meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = timerLastNumber
						return false
					end

					if meridianSafeModeState and meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase == "starting" then
						meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = timerLastNumber
						return true
					end

					if meridianSafeModeState and meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed ~= true and meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive then
						meridianSafeModeState.timerPhase = "starting"
						meridianSafeModeState.timerStartUsed = true
						meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = timerLastNumber
						meridianSafeModeState.timerSawInactive = false
						return true
					end

					if meridianSafeModeState then
						meridianSafeModeState.timerLastNumber = timerLastNumber
					end

					return false
				end

				M.SafeModeHoldingBrainrot = function()
					for _, v95 in ipairs({ "Stealing", "Carrying", "IsCarrying", "HoldingBrainrot", "HasBrainrot" }) do
						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return localPlayer:GetAttribute(v95)
						end)

						if ok and result == true then
							return true
						end
					end

					local character = localPlayer.Character
					if not character then
						return false
					end

					for _, v95 in ipairs({ "Stealing", "Carrying", "IsCarrying", "HoldingBrainrot", "HasBrainrot" }) do
						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return character:GetAttribute(v95)
						end)

						if ok and result == true then
							return true
						end
						local v96 = character:FindFirstChild(v95, true)
						if v96 and v96:IsA("BoolValue") and v96.Value then
							return true
						end
					end

					return false
				end

				M.SafeModeActionActive = function(arg)
					if arg == "aimbot" then
						return tbl23.aimbot == true
					end

					if arg == "tpBat" then
						return tbl23.desync == true
					end

					if arg == "autoLeft" then
						return tbl31.L == true
					end

					if arg == "autoRight" then
						return tbl31.R == true
					end
					return false
				end

				M.SafeModeActionLabel = function(arg)
					return ({ aimbot = "AIMBOT", tpBat = "TP BAT", autoLeft = "AUTO LEFT", autoRight = "AUTO RIGHT" })[arg] or "ACTION"
				end

				M.SafeModeBlockReason = function()
					if M.SafeModeEnabled ~= true then
						return nil
					end
					local meridianSafeModeState = M.SafeModeState
					if meridianSafeModeState and meridianSafeModeState.countdown then
						return "ROUND COUNTDOWN"
					end

					if meridianSafeModeState and meridianSafeModeState.holding then
						return "BRAINROT HELD"
					end
					return nil
				end

				M.SafeModeApplyBackend = function(arg, arg2)
					local aimbot = arg2 == true

					if aimbot then
						local exclusiveState = M.SafeModeState

						for _, otherAction in ipairs({ "aimbot", "tpBat", "autoLeft", "autoRight" }) do
							if otherAction ~= arg then
								if exclusiveState and exclusiveState.pending then
									exclusiveState.pending[otherAction] = false
								end

								if exclusiveState and exclusiveState.queuedAt then
									exclusiveState.queuedAt[otherAction] = 0
								end

								if M.SafeModeActionActive(otherAction) then
									M.SafeModeApplyBackend(otherAction, false)
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if arg == "aimbot" then
						if aimbot and tbl23.desync then
							tbl23.desync = false
							tbl23.desyncSwing = false
							pcall(fn49_tpBatStop)

							if handlers["TP Bat"] then
								handlers["TP Bat"](false, true)
							end
						end

						tbl23.aimbot = aimbot

						if aimbot then
							pcall(fn46)
						else
							pcall(fn47)
						end

						if handlers["Bat Aimbot"] then
							handlers["Bat Aimbot"](aimbot, true)
						end
					elseif arg == "tpBat" then
						if aimbot and tbl23.aimbot then
							tbl23.aimbot = false
							pcall(fn47)

							if handlers["Bat Aimbot"] then
								handlers["Bat Aimbot"](false, true)
							end
						end

						tbl23.desync = aimbot
						tbl23.desyncSwing = aimbot
						M.TPMirrorEnabled = false
						table.clear(tbl36)

						if aimbot then
							pcall(fn48_tpBatStart)
						else
							pcall(fn49_tpBatStop)
						end

						if handlers["TP Bat"] then
							handlers["TP Bat"](aimbot, true)
						end
					elseif arg == "autoLeft" then
						if aimbot then
							if tbl31.R then
								tbl31.R = false
								pcall(tbl31.stopR, true)
							end

							tbl31.L = true
							pcall(tbl31.startL)
						else
							tbl31.L = false
							pcall(tbl31.stopL)
						end

						if handlers["Auto Left"] then
							handlers["Auto Left"](aimbot, true)
						end

						if handlers["Auto Right"] then
							handlers["Auto Right"](tbl31.R == true, true)
						end
					elseif arg == "autoRight" then
						if aimbot then
							if tbl31.L then
								tbl31.L = false
								pcall(tbl31.stopL, true)
							end

							tbl31.R = true
							pcall(tbl31.startR)
						else
							tbl31.R = false
							pcall(tbl31.stopR)
						end

						if handlers["Auto Right"] then
							handlers["Auto Right"](aimbot, true)
						end

						if handlers["Auto Left"] then
							handlers["Auto Left"](tbl31.L == true, true)
						end
					end

					if M.SyncMobile then
						pcall(M.SyncMobile)
					end
				end

				M.SafeModeSetAction = function(arg, arg2)
					local meridianSafeModeState = M.SafeModeState
					if not meridianSafeModeState or not meridianSafeModeState.pending then
						return false
					end

					if not (arg2 == true) then
						local flag29 = meridianSafeModeState.pending[arg] == true
						local flag30

						if flag29 then
							flag30 = os.clock() - (meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt and meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt[arg] or 0) < 0.25
						else
							flag30 = flag29
						end

						if flag30 then
							return true
						end
						meridianSafeModeState.pending[arg] = false

						if meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt then
							meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt[arg] = 0
						end

						M.SafeModeApplyBackend(arg, false)

						if flag29 then
							M.SafeModeNotify(M.SafeModeActionLabel(arg) .. " QUEUE CANCELED")
						end

						return true
					end

					for _, otherAction in ipairs({ "aimbot", "tpBat", "autoLeft", "autoRight" }) do
						if otherAction ~= arg then
							meridianSafeModeState.pending[otherAction] = false

							if meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt then
								meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt[otherAction] = 0
							end
						end
					end

					local v95 = M.SafeModeBlockReason(arg)

					if v95 then
						if arg == "aimbot" then
							meridianSafeModeState.pending.tpBat = false
						end

						if arg == "tpBat" then
							meridianSafeModeState.pending.aimbot = false
						end

						if arg == "autoRight" then
							meridianSafeModeState.pending.autoRight = false
						end

						if arg == "autoRight" then
							meridianSafeModeState.pending.autoLeft = false
						end

						meridianSafeModeState.pending[arg] = true

						if meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt then
							meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt[arg] = os.clock()
						end

						M.SafeModeApplyBackend(arg, false)
						M.SafeModeNotify(M.SafeModeActionLabel(arg) .. " QUEUED - " .. v95)
						return false
					end

					if arg == "aimbot" then
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.tpBat = false
					end

					if arg == "tpBat" then
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.aimbot = false
					end

					if arg == "autoLeft" then
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.autoRight = false
					end

					if arg == "autoRight" then
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.autoLeft = false
					end

					meridianSafeModeState.pending[arg] = false

					if meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt then
						meridianSafeModeState.queuedAt[arg] = 0
					end

					M.SafeModeApplyBackend(arg, true)
					return true
				end

				M.SafeModeToggleAction = function(arg)
					local meridianSafeModeState = M.SafeModeState
					if meridianSafeModeState and meridianSafeModeState.pending and meridianSafeModeState.pending[arg] then
						return M.SafeModeSetAction(arg, false)
					end
					return M.SafeModeSetAction(arg, not M.SafeModeActionActive(arg))
				end

				M.SafeModeSetResetLock = function(arg)
					local state = M.SafeModeState
					local lock = arg == true
					if state and state.resetLocked == lock then return end
					pcall(function()
						game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", lock and false or true)
					end)
					if state then state.resetLocked = lock end
					if lock and M.SafeModeNotify then M.SafeModeNotify("SAFE MODE: RESET LOCKED WHILE HOLDING") end
				end

				M.SafeModeEvaluate = function()
					local meridianSafeModeState = M.SafeModeState
					if not meridianSafeModeState then
						return
					end
					local countdown = M.SafeModeCountdownActive() == true
					local holding = M.SafeModeHoldingBrainrot() == true
					local flag29 = countdown and not meridianSafeModeState.countdown
					local flag30 = meridianSafeModeState.countdown and not countdown
					local flag31 = holding and not meridianSafeModeState.holding
					meridianSafeModeState.countdown = countdown
					meridianSafeModeState.holding = holding
					M.SafeModeSetResetLock(M.SafeModeEnabled == true and holding)
					if M.SafeModeEnabled ~= true then
						return
					end

					if flag29 then
						M.SafeModeNotify("ROUND COUNTDOWN - ACTIONS LOCKED")

						for _, v95 in ipairs({ "aimbot", "tpBat", "autoLeft", "autoRight" }) do
							if M.SafeModeActionActive(v95) then
								meridianSafeModeState.pending[v95] = true
								M.SafeModeApplyBackend(v95, false)
							end
						end
					end

					if flag31 then
						for _, v95 in ipairs({ "aimbot", "tpBat", "autoLeft", "autoRight" }) do
							if M.SafeModeActionActive(v95) then
								meridianSafeModeState.pending[v95] = true
								M.SafeModeApplyBackend(v95, false)
							end
						end
					end

					if flag30 then
						M.SafeModeNotify("ROUND COUNTDOWN ENDED")
					end

					for _, v95 in ipairs({ "autoLeft", "autoRight", "aimbot", "tpBat" }) do
						if meridianSafeModeState.pending[v95] and not M.SafeModeBlockReason(v95) then
							meridianSafeModeState.pending[v95] = false
							M.SafeModeApplyBackend(v95, true)
							M.SafeModeNotify(M.SafeModeActionLabel(v95) .. " ENABLED")
						end
					end

					if M.SyncMobile then
						pcall(M.SyncMobile)
					end
				end

				M.SafeModeSetEnabled = function(arg, arg2)
					M.SafeModeEnabled = arg == true
					if not M.SafeModeEnabled then M.SafeModeSetResetLock(false) end
					local meridianSafeModeState = M.SafeModeState

					if not M.SafeModeEnabled and meridianSafeModeState and meridianSafeModeState.pending then
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.aimbot = false
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.tpBat = false
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.autoLeft = false
						meridianSafeModeState.pending.autoRight = false
					end

					if arg2 then
						M.SafeModeNotify(M.SafeModeEnabled and "SAFE MODE ENABLED" or "SAFE MODE DISABLED")
					end

					if M.SafeModeEnabled then
						if meridianSafeModeState then
							meridianSafeModeState.countdown = false
							meridianSafeModeState.holding = false
						end

						pcall(M.SafeModeEvaluate)
					end

					if M.SyncMobile then
						pcall(M.SyncMobile)
					end
				end

				local function fn79()
					local meridianSafeModeCarryConnections = {}

					local tbl50 = {

						local tbl51 = {}

						for k in pairs(meridianSafeModeCarryConnections) do
							if k:sub(1, 10) == "char_" then
								table.insert(tbl51, k)
							end
						end

							fn80(v95)
						end

						if not arg then
							return
						end

						for k in pairs(tbl50) do
							meridianSafeModeCarryConnections["char_attr_" .. k] = arg:GetAttributeChangedSignal(k):Connect(fn81)
							local v95 = arg:FindFirstChild(k, true)

							if v95 and v95:IsA("BoolValue") then
								fn80("char_value_" .. k)
								meridianSafeModeCarryConnections["char_value_" .. k] = v95:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(fn81)
							end
						end

						meridianSafeModeCarryConnections.char_added = arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
							if tbl50[descendant.Name] then
								if descendant:IsA("BoolValue") then
									fn80("char_value_" .. descendant.Name)
									meridianSafeModeCarryConnections["char_value_" .. descendant.Name] = descendant:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(fn81)
								end

								fn81()
							elseif descendant:IsA("Tool") then
								fn81()
							end
						end)

						meridianSafeModeCarryConnections.char_removed = arg.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(descendant)
							if tbl50[descendant.Name] or descendant:IsA("Tool") then
								fn81()
							end
						end)
					end

						meridianSafeModeCarryConnections["player_attr_" .. k] = localPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal(k):Connect(fn81)
					end

						fn82(character)
						fn81()
					end)

						fn81()

						local n51 = math.max(1, n50 - n49)
						local n52 = 3.1415926535897931 * n47
						local n53 = n51 * 2 + n52 * 2
						local n54 = math.clamp(math.ceil(n53 / 4), 72, 180)

							M.KickWarning.SetEnabled(M.KickWarningEnabled)

						pcall(fn29, true)
					end)

					fn81(Utility, "Font", function()
						M.FontMode = "DEFAULT"
						pcall(M.ApplyFontMode)
						end, function()
							M.FontMode = "PIXEL"
							pcall(M.ApplyFontMode)
						end, M.FontMode == "PIXEL")
				end

				local function fn82()
					if not Combat then
						return
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(Combat:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Frame") then
							if child.Name == "SEMI Range" then
								child.Visible = M.StealMode == "SEMI"
							elseif child.Name == "Normal Pause %" then
								child.Visible = M.StealMode ~= "SEMI"
							elseif child.Name == "Radius" then
								child.Visible = true
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if Combat then
					local function fn83(arg)
						M.BatMode = arg == "BYPASS" and "BYPASS" or "DEFAULT"
						tbl23.antiMode = M.BatMode == "BYPASS"

						if tbl23.aimbot then
							pcall(fn47)
							pcall(fn46)
						end

						pcall(fn29)
					end

					local function antiRagSetMode(mode)
					M.AntiRagMode = mode
					if tbl32.enabled then
					pcall(fn36)
					tbl32.enabled = true
					pcall(fn35)
					end
					pcall(fn29)
					end

					fn81(Movement, "Anti Ragdoll", function()
					antiRagSetMode("V1")
					end, function()
					antiRagSetMode("V2")
					end, M.AntiRagMode == "V2")

					fn81(Combat, "Bat Aimbot", function()
					fn83("DEFAULT")
					end, function()
					fn83("BYPASS")
					end, M.BatMode == "BYPASS")

					fn81(Combat, "TP Bat", function()
						M.TpBatMode = "LOW PING"
						end, function()
							M.TpBatMode = "HIGH PING"
						end, M.TpBatMode == "HIGH PING")

					fn81(Combat, "Bat Counter", function()
						M.OshaBatCounter.mode = "V1"
						M.BatCounterMode = "V1"

						if M.OshaBatCounter.on then
							pcall(M.OshaBatCounter.start)
						end

						pcall(fn29)
						end, function()
							M.OshaBatCounter.mode = "V2"
							M.BatCounterMode = "V2"

							if M.OshaBatCounter.on then
								pcall(M.OshaBatCounter.start)
							end

							pcall(fn29)
						end, M.OshaBatCounter.mode == "V2")

					tbl23.antiMode = M.BatMode == "BYPASS"

					-- Auto Steal switcher (remade): one function owns the whole mode change
					local stealSwitchTok = 0
					local stealSwitchSet = nil

					M.SetStealMode = function(mode, fromUI)
						mode = mode == "SEMI" and "SEMI" or "NORMAL"
						stealSwitchTok += 1
						local myTok = stealSwitchTok
						M.StealMode = mode
						pcall(fn82)

						if not fromUI and stealSwitchSet then
							pcall(stealSwitchSet, mode == "SEMI", true)
						end

						-- hard stop BOTH engines so nothing stale survives the switch
						pcall(fn33)
						if tbl30.stealConn then
							pcall(function() tbl30.stealConn:Disconnect() end)
							tbl30.stealConn = nil
						end
						tbl30.starting = false
						for _, cached in pairs(tbl30.InternalStealCache) do
							cached.ready = true
						end

						task.spawn(function()
							if tbl20.AutoSteal then
								task.wait(0.05)
								if myTok ~= stealSwitchTok then return end
								pcall(fn32)

								task.wait(0.6)
								if myTok ~= stealSwitchTok or not tbl20.AutoSteal then return end
								local running = (mode == "SEMI" and tbl30.stealConn ~= nil) or (mode ~= "SEMI" and tbl21.autoSteal ~= nil)
								if not running then
									pcall(fn33)
									tbl30.starting = false
									pcall(fn32)
									running = (mode == "SEMI" and tbl30.stealConn ~= nil) or (mode ~= "SEMI" and tbl21.autoSteal ~= nil)
								end
								print("[StealMode]", mode, "running =", running)
							end
							pcall(fn29)
						end)
					end

					do
						local pill = M.SwitchFrames and M.SwitchFrames["Auto Steal"]
						local btns = {}
						if pill then
							for _, c in ipairs(pill:GetChildren()) do
								if c:IsA("TextButton") then btns[#btns + 1] = c end
							end
						end

						if #btns >= 2 then
							local marker
							for _, c in ipairs(pill:GetChildren()) do
								if c:IsA("Frame") then marker = c break end
							end

							stealSwitchSet = function(isSemi)
								if marker then
									TweenService:Create(marker, TweenInfo.new(0.14), { Position = isSemi and UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 4) or UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4) }):Play()
								end
							end

							M.SelectorAPI = M.SelectorAPI or {}
							M.SelectorAPI["Auto Steal"] = {
								set = function(isSemi, silent)
									stealSwitchSet(isSemi)
									if not silent then M.SetStealMode(isSemi and "SEMI" or "NORMAL", true) end
								end,
								get = function() return M.StealMode == "SEMI" end,
							}

							btns[1].Activated:Connect(function()
								stealSwitchSet(false)
								M.SetStealMode("NORMAL", true)
							end)
							btns[2].Activated:Connect(function()
								stealSwitchSet(true)
								M.SetStealMode("SEMI", true)
							end)
							stealSwitchSet(M.StealMode == "SEMI")
						else
							-- fallback to the generic selector binder
							fn81(Combat, "Auto Steal", function() M.SetStealMode("NORMAL", true) end, function() M.SetStealMode("SEMI", true) end, M.StealMode == "SEMI")
						end
					end

					fn82()
				end

				local function fn83(arg, arg2, arg3)
					local v95 = fn77(arg, arg2)
					if not v95 then
						return
					end

					for _, descendant in ipairs(v95:GetDescendants()) do

							descendant.Activated:Connect(function()
								pcall(arg3)
							end)

							return
						end
					end

					local instance6 = Instance.new("TextButton", v95)
					instance6.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					instance6.Text = ""
					instance6.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
					instance6.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

				if customSky then
					local meridianSkyOrder = M.SkyOrder or { "Off" }
					local n40 = 1
					local meridianSkyMode = M.SkyMode or "Off"

					for i, v95 in ipairs(meridianSkyOrder) do
						if v95 == meridianSkyMode then
							n40 = i
							break
						end
					end

					local tbl50 = {}

					for _, descendant in ipairs(customSky:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
							if descendant.Text == "<" then
								tbl50.prev = descendant
							elseif descendant.Text == ">" then
								tbl50.next = descendant
							end
						end
					end

					local v95 = nil

					for _, descendant in ipairs(customSky:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("TextButton") and descendant.Text ~= "<" and descendant.Text ~= ">" then
							v95 = descendant
						end
					end

					local function fn84()
						local v96 = meridianSkyOrder[n40]

						if v95 then
							v95.Text = string.upper(v96)
						end

						if v96 == "Off" then
							pcall(M.StopSky)
						else
							pcall(M.StartSky, v96)
						end

						if tbl25.auto then
							task.spawn(function()
								pcall(fn29)
							end)
						end
					end

					fn84()

					if tbl50.prev then
						tbl50.prev.Activated:Connect(function()
							n40 = (n40 - 2) % #meridianSkyOrder + 1
							fn84()
						end)
					end

					if tbl50.next then
						tbl50.next.Activated:Connect(function()
							n40 = n40 % #meridianSkyOrder + 1
							fn84()
						end)
					end
				end

				local function fn85()
					if tbl22.family ~= "lagger" then
						local carry = tbl22.carry == true
						tbl22.family = "lagger"
						tbl22.carry = carry
					else
						tbl22.carry = not tbl22.carry
					end

					if M.AutoCarrySelectFamily then
						M.AutoCarrySelectFamily(tbl22.family, tbl22.carry)
					end
				end

				local function meridianSyncMobile()
					local pending = M.SafeModeState and M.SafeModeState.pending or {}
					fn84("Auto Left", pending.autoLeft and "secondary" or tbl31.L and true or false)
					fn84("Auto Right", pending.autoRight and "secondary" or tbl31.R and true or false)
					fn84("Bat Aimbot", pending.aimbot and "secondary" or tbl23.aimbot and true or false)
					fn84("TP Bat", pending.tpBat and "secondary" or tbl23.desync and true or false)
					fn84("Carry Speed", tbl22.family == "normal" and tbl22.carry == true and true or false)
					fn84("Lagger Mode", tbl22.family == "lagger" and not tbl22.carry and true or tbl22.family == "lagger" and tbl22.carry and M.AdvancedLaggerButton ~= true and "secondary" or false)
					fn84("Lagger Carry", tbl22.family == "lagger" and tbl22.carry and true or false)

					if M.RefreshMovementModes then
						M.RefreshMovementModes()
					end

					if handlers["Bat Aimbot"] then
						handlers["Bat Aimbot"](pending.aimbot == true or tbl23.aimbot == true, true)
					end

					if handlers["TP Bat"] then
						handlers["TP Bat"](pending.tpBat == true or tbl23.desync == true, true)
					end

					if handlers["Auto Left"] then
						handlers["Auto Left"](pending.autoLeft == true or tbl31.L == true, true)
					end

					if handlers["Auto Right"] then
						handlers["Auto Right"](pending.autoRight == true or tbl31.R == true, true)
					end

					if handlers["Match Speed"] then
						handlers["Match Speed"](M.AutoPathMatchSpeed == true, true)
					end

					if handlers["Safe Mode"] then
						handlers["Safe Mode"](M.SafeModeEnabled == true, true)
					end

					if handlers["Kick Warning"] then
						handlers["Kick Warning"](M.KickWarningEnabled ~= false, true)
					end

					if handlers["Auto Steal"] then
						handlers["Auto Steal"](tbl20.AutoSteal and true or false, true)
					end

					if handlers["Ragdoll Steal"] then
						handlers["Ragdoll Steal"](M.RagdollStealEnabled ~= false, true)
					end

					if handlers.Intro then
						handlers.Intro(M.IntroEnabled ~= false, true)
					end

					if handlers["Ragdoll Countdown"] then
						handlers["Ragdoll Countdown"](M.RagCountdownEnabled ~= false, true)
					end
				end

				M.SyncMobile = meridianSyncMobile

						end

								tbl22.family = "normal"

							else
								tbl22.family = "lagger"
								tbl22.carry = false
							end

							if M.AutoCarrySelectFamily then
								M.AutoCarrySelectFamily(tbl22.family, tbl22.carry)
							end
						else
							fn85()
						end
					end,
					["Lagger Carry"] = function()
						if tbl22.family == "lagger" and tbl22.carry then
							tbl22.family = "normal"
							tbl22.carry = false
						else
							tbl22.family = "lagger"
							tbl22.carry = true
						end

						if M.AutoCarrySelectFamily then
							M.AutoCarrySelectFamily(tbl22.family, tbl22.carry)
						end
					end,
				}

				local tbl52 = {}

				local function fn86(arg)
					for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("TextButton") and descendant.Name == "KeybindButton" then
							return descendant
						end
					end
				end

				local function fn87(arg)
					if not arg or arg == "" or arg == "..." or arg == "?" or arg == "NONE" then
						return nil
					end
					local str10 = arg:lower()

					for _, v95 in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do
						if v95.Name:lower() == str10 then
							return v95
						end
					end

					if #arg == 1 then
						local str11 = arg:upper()

						for _, v95 in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do
							if v95.Name == str11 then
								return v95
							end
						end
					end

					if #arg >= 3 then
						for _, v95 in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do
							if v95.Name:sub(1, #arg):lower() == str10 then
								return v95
							end
						end
					end

					return nil
				end

				if Keybinds then
					for _, child in ipairs(Keybinds:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Frame") and tbl51[child.Name] then
							local v95 = fn86(child)

							if v95 then
								local name = child.Name

								if M.KbSave and M.KbSave[name] == "NONE" then
									v95:SetAttribute("KeyCodeName", nil)
									v95.Text = "NONE"
									tbl52[name] = nil
								elseif M.KbSave and M.KbSave[name] then
									local v96 = Enum.KeyCode[M.KbSave[name]]

									if v96 then
										local name2 = tbl42[v96.Name] or v96.Name

										if #name2 > 5 then
											name2 = name2:sub(1, 10)
										end

										v95:SetAttribute("KeyCodeName", v96.Name)
										v95.Text = string.upper(name2)
										tbl52[name] = v96
									end
								end

								local function fn88()
									local attribute = v95:GetAttribute("KeyCodeName")
									attribute = attribute and Enum.KeyCode[attribute] or fn87(v95.Text)

									if attribute and attribute ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
										tbl52[name] = attribute
										M.KbSave = M.KbSave or {}
										M.KbSave[name] = attribute.Name
										print("[Keybind]", name, "->", attribute.Name)

										if tbl25.auto then
											task.spawn(function()
												pcall(fn29)
											end)
										end
									else
										tbl52[name] = nil
										M.KbSave = M.KbSave or {}
										M.KbSave[name] = "NONE"

										if tbl25.auto then
											task.spawn(function()
												pcall(fn29)
											end)
										end
									end
								end

								fn88()
								v95:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fn88)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local function fn88(arg)
					local name = arg.Name
					return name:sub(1, 6) == "Button" or name:sub(1, 4) == "DPad" or name:sub(1, 5) == "Thumb"
				end

										M.CtrlSave[name] = "NONE"
									end

										pcall(M.PersistControllerBindings)
									end

									fn89()
								end

								fn90()
								v95:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fn90)
								v95:GetAttributeChangedSignal("KeyCodeName"):Connect(fn90)

						M.ApplyInvertUI = function()
							local flag31 = M.InvertUI == true
							local tbl55 = { frame2 }

							local meridianSongGallery = screenGui:FindFirstChild("MeridianSongGallery")

							local color2 = fn90(tbl37.accent, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)) and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or tbl37.accent

											local color3

												color3 = v99
											else
												color3 = (v98 == frame2 or v98 == meridianSkinGallery or v98 == meridianSongGallery) and Color3.fromRGB(252, 248, 250) or Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 252)

											end

												v98.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
											end
										elseif not flag31 and v98:GetAttribute("_MeridianInvertOriginalBg") ~= nil then

											v98.BackgroundTransparency = v98:GetAttribute("_MeridianInvertOriginalBgTransparency")

											v98:SetAttribute("_MeridianInvertOriginalBgTransparency", nil)
										end
									end

									local flag33 = v98:IsA("UIStroke") and not flag32

									if flag33 then
										flag33 = not (v98.Parent and v98.Parent:IsA("TextLabel"))
									end

									if flag33 then
										if flag31 then
											if v98:GetAttribute("_MeridianInvertOriginalStroke") == nil then
												v98:SetAttribute("_MeridianInvertOriginalStroke", v98.Color)
												v98:SetAttribute("_MeridianInvertOriginalStrokeTransparency", v98.Transparency)
												v98:SetAttribute("_MeridianInvertOriginalStrokeThickness", v98.Thickness)

											v98.Color = Color3.fromRGB(66, 66, 74)

								if child:IsA("Frame") and tbl51[child.Name] then
									local v95 = fn86(child)

									if v95 then
										v95:SetAttribute("KeyCodeName", nil)
										v95.Text = "?"
									end
								end
							end

							for k in pairs(tbl52) do
								tbl52[k] = nil
							end

							M.KbSave = {}

							if tbl25.auto then
								task.spawn(function()
									pcall(fn29)
								end)
							end

							textLabel2.Text = "CLEARED ✓"

							task.delay(1, function()
								pcall(function()
									textLabel2.Text = "RESET ALL KEYBINDS"
								end)
							end)
						end)
					end
				end

				fn90()
			end

				local function fn72(arg, ...)
					local v93 = table.pack(...)

					for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
						local v94 = arg[select(i, table.unpack(v93, 1, v93.n))]
						if v94 ~= nil then
							return v94
						end
					end
				end

				local function meridianApplyAnimPack(meridianAnimPack)
					if meridianAnimPack == "Off" or not tbl45[meridianAnimPack] then
						local character = localPlayer.Character

						if character then
							local animate = character:FindFirstChild("Animate")

							if animate then
								animate.Disabled = true
								task.wait(0.06)
								animate.Disabled = false
							end
						end

						M.AnimPack = "Off"
						return
					end

					local v93 = tbl45[meridianAnimPack]
					local character = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
					local animate = nil

					for i = 1, 40 do
						animate = character:FindFirstChild("Animate")

						if not (animate and animate:FindFirstChild("run")) then
							task.wait(0.1)
							animate = nil
							continue
						end

						break
					end

					if not animate then
						return
					end

					local function fn73(arg, name, arg2)
						if not arg or not arg2 then
							return
						end
						local animation = arg:FindFirstChild(name)

						if not animation then
							animation = Instance.new("Animation", arg)
							animation.Name = name
						end

						animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(arg2)
					end

					fn73(animate:FindFirstChild("walk"), "WalkAnim", fn72(v93, "WalkAnim", "Walk"))
					fn73(animate:FindFirstChild("run"), "RunAnim", fn72(v93, "RunAnim", "Run"))
					fn73(animate:FindFirstChild("jump"), "JumpAnim", fn72(v93, "JumpAnim", "Jump"))
					fn73(animate:FindFirstChild("fall"), "FallAnim", fn72(v93, "FallAnim", "Fall"))
					fn73(animate:FindFirstChild("climb"), "ClimbAnim", fn72(v93, "ClimbAnim", "Climb"))
					fn73(animate:FindFirstChild("swim"), "Swim", fn72(v93, "Swim"))
					fn73(animate:FindFirstChild("swimidle"), "SwimIdle", fn72(v93, "SwimIdle", "Swim"))
					local idle = animate:FindFirstChild("idle")

					if idle then
						local animation1 = fn72(v93, "Animation1")
						local animation2 = fn72(v93, "Animation2")

						if animation1 or animation2 then
							fn73(idle, "Animation1", animation1 or animation2)
							fn73(idle, "Animation2", animation2 or animation1)
						elseif v93.Idle then
							fn73(idle, "Animation1", v93.Idle[1])
							fn73(idle, "Animation2", v93.Idle[2] or v93.Idle[1])
						end
					end

					animate.Disabled = true
					task.wait(0.06)
					animate.Disabled = false
					local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if humanoid then
						pcall(function()
							humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed)
							task.wait(0.03)
							humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
						end)
					end

					M.AnimPack = meridianAnimPack
				end

				M.ApplyAnimPack = meridianApplyAnimPack
				M.AnimPack = M.AnimPack or "Amazon Unboxed"

				if M.AnimPack ~= "Off" then
					task.defer(meridianApplyAnimPack, M.AnimPack)
				end

				localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
					task.wait(0.6)

					if M.AnimPack and M.AnimPack ~= "Off" then
						meridianApplyAnimPack(M.AnimPack)
					end
				end)

				local animPack = Utility and Utility:FindFirstChild("Anim Pack")

				if animPack then
					local animPackPrev = animPack:FindFirstChild("AnimPackPrev", true)
					local animPackNext = animPack:FindFirstChild("AnimPackNext", true)
					local animPackValue = animPack:FindFirstChild("AnimPackValue", true)
					local n35 = 1

					local function fn73()
						local v93 = tbl46[n35]

						if animPackValue then

							animPackValue.Text = string.upper(v93)
						end

						meridianApplyAnimPack(v93)

						if tbl25.auto then
							task.spawn(function()
								pcall(fn29)
							end)
						end
					end

					if M.AnimPack then
						for i, v93 in ipairs(tbl46) do
							if v93 == M.AnimPack then
								n35 = i
								break
							end
						end

						if animPackValue then
							animPackValue.Text = string.upper(tbl46[n35])
						end
					end

					if animPackPrev then
						animPackPrev.Activated:Connect(function()
							n35 = (n35 - 2) % #tbl46 + 1
							fn73()
						end)
					end

					if animPackNext then
						animPackNext.Activated:Connect(function()
							n35 = n35 % #tbl46 + 1
							fn73()
						end)
					end
				end
			end

			fn71()

			task.spawn(function()
				while task.wait(2) do
					local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

					if currentCamera and screenGui and screenGui.Parent then
						local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
						local visible = frame2 and frame2.Visible
						local visible2 = frame3 and frame3.Visible

						local function fn72(arg)
							if not arg then
								return false
							end
							local absolutePosition = arg.AbsolutePosition
							local absoluteSize = arg.AbsoluteSize
							return absolutePosition.X > -absoluteSize.X * 0.5 and absolutePosition.Y > -absoluteSize.Y * 0.5 and absolutePosition.X < viewportSize.X - absoluteSize.X * 0.5 and absolutePosition.Y < viewportSize.Y - absoluteSize.Y * 0.5
						end

						if not (visible and fn72(frame2) or visible2 and fn72(frame3)) then
							if frame2 then
								frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0.5, 0)
								position = frame2.Position
								frame2.Visible = true
							end

							if frame3 then
								frame3.Position = floatDefaultPosition()
								frame3.Visible = false
							end

							M.MainPos = nil
							M.FloatBtnPos = nil
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		fn55()
	end
	-- Cleanup: disconnect every long-lived connection registered in M
	M.Destroy = function()
		local function safeDisconnect(conn)
			if conn and type(conn) == "table" and type(conn.Disconnect) == "function" then
				pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
			end
		end

		safeDisconnect(M.SpeedControllerConn)
		safeDisconnect(M.SafeModeMonitor)
		safeDisconnect(M.SafeModeViewportConn)
		safeDisconnect(M.AntiLagDescConn)
		safeDisconnect(M.AutoTPThread and { Disconnect = function() pcall(task.cancel, M.AutoTPThread) end } or nil)
		safeDisconnect(M.DropConn)
		safeDisconnect(M.NeonConn)

		if M.SafeModeNotifyGui and M.SafeModeNotifyGui.Parent then
			pcall(function() M.SafeModeNotifyGui:Destroy() end)
		end

		if M.KickWarning and M.KickWarning.Destroy then
			pcall(M.KickWarning.Destroy)
		end

		if M.GlobalAntiVoid and M.GlobalAntiVoid.Destroy then
			pcall(M.GlobalAntiVoid.Destroy)
		end

		if M.LookAtEnemy and M.LookAtEnemy.Stop then
			pcall(M.LookAtEnemy.Stop)
		end

		if M.NeonWhiteTools and M.NeonWhiteTools.SetEnabled then
			pcall(M.NeonWhiteTools.SetEnabled, false)
		end

		if M.StopAutoTP then
			pcall(M.StopAutoTP)
		end

		if M.StopAutoCarry then
			pcall(M.StopAutoCarry)
		end

		if M.VoltRunning then
			M.VoltRunning = nil
		end
	end
end

_MeridianMain()
